`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# Game Release Guard 项目指令

开始任务时先完整读取 [统一 Skill / Markdown 操作说明](skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)，按新项目开发、已有项目只读检查、明确授权加固三种场景处理。识别引擎和版本后，只读取对应的 engines/ 知识文件；授权加固时备份后实际修改并验证。这是明确的读取指令，普通 Markdown 链接不代表原生 Skill 已自动加载。只有实际需要调用 Unity / Unreal 本地工具时，再读对应的 [Unity](workflows/unity-release.md)、[Unreal](workflows/unreal-release.md)、[安全检查](workflows/security-audit.md) 或 [回滚](workflows/rollback.md) 工作流及真实代码。Godot、Cocos Creator、GameMaker 只有 Skill 指引，没有本地适配器。

先核实项目目录、引擎精确版本。已有工程检查默认只读、仅在对话中给出位置/风险/建议，不改代码、不生成报告；audit 即使带 --json 也会写报告，不能默认调用。执行已授权的工具操作前先 plan/预览，或在用户要求报告后 audit。其余操作边界以统一 Skill 为准。

首次使用真实游戏先读 [完整备份与恢复](workflows/backup.md)，在恢复的新副本上尝试。apply 有文件变更时必须先成功完成工程外的完整文件备份与核验；不绕过该步骤、不上传备份、不强制覆盖恢复目标。

修改本工具时保留 Python 3.10+ 标准库架构、旧 CLI/GUI/报告及回滚兼容性。执行 `python -m unittest discover -s tests -v`，不删除或削弱旧测试。用户在 2026-11-25 明确授权独立 P3 分支开发，并允许在缺少 UE/许可证/实机条件时将真实引擎验收记为 NOT_VERIFIED；2026-11-26 用户另行授权整合，PR #2 再 PR #1 已按依赖合并到 main；这不代表真实游戏保护验收通过。高级操作先读 [P3 工作流](workflows/advanced-protection.md) 与对应实际代码。仓库 AI 适配只维护 Markdown，不构建 AI 管理系统，不修改全局 AI 配置。

```

`AI-START.md`:

```md
# 下载一次，让 AI 按引擎使用

下载 [完整 Skill ZIP](downloads/game-release-guard-skill.zip)，解压保留整个 `game-release-guard/` 目录。入口只有 [SKILL.md](skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)，它按项目识别结果选择一份 `engines/` 文档，无需 Python。只复制入口文件会缺少引擎知识。

[中文安装](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.md) · [English installation](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.en.md) · [中文示例](skills/game-release-guard/EXAMPLES.md) · [English examples](skills/game-release-guard/EXAMPLES.en.md)

## 三种日常用法

- **开发**：读取核心 Skill 和当前引擎文档，完成明确的功能任务，安全规则融入代码，不额外写报告。
- **检查**：只读工程，直接在对话给出文件位置、风险、已有保护和修复建议。
- **加固**：明确授权后，在范围内核验工程外备份、实际修改代码/配置、运行适用检查，在对话说明验证边界和恢复方法。

把安装说明中的通用提示词交给你使用的 AI。支持原生 Skill 时按该客户端当前项目级格式安装；否则明确读取 Markdown。保留已有指令，不覆盖同名文件，不改全局配置。如果助手无法读本地文件，可提供核心和对应引擎文档，以及非敏感项目信息；它不能声称已经检查不可访问的工程。

## 需要本地工具时

Unity / Unreal 才有本仓库的 CLI / GUI。读取 README、对应工作流和真实代码，核对 `--help`；先 plan/预览。Godot、Cocos Creator、GameMaker 不存在本地自动加密适配器。

`audit` 即使加 `--json` 也写 HTML/JSON 报告，没有 `--no-report`。`apply` 也生成报告；不应把默认只读检查或不需要报告的加固直接变成这些调用。工具修改前遵守 [备份与恢复](workflows/backup.md)，其余边界以 Skill 为准。

## 维护与验证

仓库级入口 [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md)、[CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md)、[GEMINI.md](GEMINI.md) 都指向同一核心。通用 Markdown 方式不依赖某客户端的自动发现，也不承诺输入 URL 就能绕过网络、文件或安装权限。

[五引擎合成验证](docs/MULTI-ENGINE-SKILL-VALIDATION.md)记录本次行为证据；[旧版验证](docs/SKILL-VALIDATION-20261125.md)仅描述当时版本。这里不安装缺失 AI 客户端或注册后台服务。

```

`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing / 参与贡献

## English

Start with the [README](README.en.md), [security boundaries](SECURITY.md) and the relevant workflow. This project is early development; real-engine evidence must remain separate from synthetic tests and API compilation.

- Open an Issue with a minimal **synthetic** reproduction, tool/engine version, command and expected/actual behavior. Do not upload your game, assets, logs containing secrets, symbols or backups. Follow SECURITY.md for vulnerabilities.
- Keep changes focused. Preserve the standard-library Python 3.10+ architecture, existing CLI/GUI, full backup/restore, rollback, tests and explicit advanced opt-in. Do not rewrite unrelated code or add an AI management system.
- Never manufacture PASS evidence, infer encryption from a filename/exit code, generate production keys or silently weaken recovery checks. Test destructive operations on disposable copies.
- Run the existing suite and add meaningful regression coverage when behavior changes. Engine-specific claims need the exact engine/tool version and actual build/runtime evidence; otherwise use NOT_VERIFIED.
- Keep README.md / README.en.md and INSTALL.md / INSTALL.en.md consistent. Maintain one core SKILL.md with engine-specific knowledge under engines/, loaded only as needed. Preserve third-party attributions and license obligations.
- After changing the Skill or its guides, run `python scripts/package_skill.py` and commit the refreshed standalone ZIP. CI uses `python scripts/package_skill.py --check` to compare its eleven allowed files against the canonical sources.
- In a pull request, explain the problem, resulting behavior, test scope and unresolved limits. Include no private artifacts. Contributions are distributed under the repository's MIT license; only submit material you are authorized to share.

```shell
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
python -m compileall -q steam_release_guard
```

## 简体中文

先阅读 [首页](README.md)、[安全边界](SECURITY.md) 和相关工作流。项目仍处于早期开发，合成测试/API 编译与真实引擎证据必须分开。

- Issue 使用最小**合成**复现，说明工具/引擎版本、命令、预期与实际结果；不上传游戏、资源、含凭据日志、符号或备份。漏洞按 SECURITY.md 处理。
- 修改保持聚焦，保留 Python 3.10+ 标准库架构、旧 CLI/GUI、完整备份恢复、回滚、全部测试和高级功能的显式启用；不重写无关代码，不增加 AI 管理系统。
- 不伪造 PASS，不从文件名/退出码推断加密，不生成生产密钥，不悄悄削弱恢复保护。破坏性操作只在可丢弃副本上测试。
- 运行上方测试；行为变化补充有意义的回归测试。引擎结论需要精确工具版本和真实构建/运行证据，否则标 NOT_VERIFIED。
- 中英文 README 和安装说明保持一致；只维护一个核心 SKILL.md，引擎知识放在 engines/ 并按需读取，保留第三方署名与许可义务。
- 修改 Skill 或使用说明后运行 `python scripts/package_skill.py`，同时提交更新的独立 ZIP。CI 通过 `python scripts/package_skill.py --check` 检查包内十一个允许文件是否与源文件一致。
- PR 说明问题、最终行为、验证范围及限制，不含私有产物。贡献按 MIT 分发，只提交有权分享的内容。

```

`README.en.md`:

```md
<p align="center"><a href="README.md">简体中文</a> | <a href="README.en.md">English</a></p>

![Game Release Guard — Protect your game. From day one.](docs/assets/hero.svg)

<h1 align="center">Game Release Guard</h1>
<p align="center"><strong>Protect your game. From day one.</strong><br/>从开发第一天开始，保护你的游戏。</p>
<p align="center">One security development and hardening Skill for Unity, Unreal, Godot, Cocos Creator and GameMaker, with local release tools for Unity and Unreal.</p>

<p align="center"><a href="README.md">简体中文</a> | <a href="README.en.md">English</a></p>

[![CI](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/workflows/test.yml/badge.svg?branch=main)](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/workflows/test.yml)
[![Early Development](https://img.shields.io/badge/status-Early%20Development-466985)](#status)
[![Python 3.10+](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-3.10%2B-3675a8)](#quick-start)
[![MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-71869b)](LICENSE)

**Multi-Engine AI Skills V1.0 · Local toolkit: Early Development / Advanced Protection Candidate.** P0, P3 and the standalone Skill are integrated into `main`. The program version remains `0.2.0.dev0`; this is not a Stable or Production Ready release. Get the source from [main ZIP](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/archive/refs/heads/main.zip) or Git. [Releases](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/releases) lists actual publications only; no stable distribution is available.

[Quick start](#quick-start) · [Install the Skill](#ai-skill) · [Capabilities](#advanced) · [Security boundaries](#boundaries) · [Evidence](#validation)

## Core capabilities

| Feature | What it provides |
|---|---|
| **AI Secure Development** | Engine-aware secure development from project initialization, without extra security reports. |
| **Existing Project Protection** | Review requests stay read-only; explicit hardening requests lead to backed-up, scoped edits and compatibility checks. |
| **Unity & Unreal Release Toolkit** | Existing local CLI / Tkinter GUI for backups, restore, configuration, release checks, opt-in advanced flows and rollback. |
| **Godot Security Skill** | GDScript export, PCK AES-256, custom templates, key handling and patch/mod provenance guidance. |
| **Cocos Creator Security Skill** | Version-aware Native / Web boundaries, Encrypt JS, JSC, script backups and resource/hot-update checks. |
| **GameMaker Security Skill** | Runtime and VM / YYC distinctions, development features, Included Files, client data and release checks. |

**One Skill covers five engines.** Unity and Unreal have local CLI / GUI support. Godot, Cocos Creator and GameMaker currently use AI-guided project edits only, with no local automated encryption tool.

<a id="quick-start"></a>
## 30-second quick start

**Just want the AI guidance? Download the [complete Skill ZIP](downloads/game-release-guard-skill.zip) and give your assistant the [universal prompt](#use-with-ai) below. No Python required.**

The local toolkit requires an existing **Python 3.10+** installation, with `tkinter` for the GUI. It uses the standard library: no `pip install`, automatic Python download, engine installation or protector installation. Adapters target Windows / Steam; Linux CI tests toolkit logic, not Linux game builds.

```shell
git clone https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard.git
cd game-release-guard
python -m steam_release_guard --help
```

Formerly Steam Release Guard. The Python package and CLI remain `steam_release_guard`; existing Unity menu and private backup directory names are also preserved for compatibility.

On Windows, double-click `Launch.cmd` for the GUI. If Python is not found, put your existing interpreter path in `.local/python-path.txt` or set the process variable `SRG_PYTHON`. Replace the example path with your own **test copy**, then preview:

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy"
python -m steam_release_guard apply "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
```

For a real project, first close the engine and sync tools → create a full backup → verify it → restore into a new directory → work on that copy. Every `apply` that changes files still requires a fresh verified backup outside the project; failure stops the operation. Review the preview and explicitly approve writes. `--yes` is not authorization by itself. [Backup workflow](workflows/backup.md) · [Detailed usage, Chinese](docs/USAGE.zh-CN.md)

<details>
<summary>Backup, inspection and recovery commands</summary>

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard backup "D:/Games/MyGame" --backup-dir "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup "D:/Games/MyGame" --backup-dir "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-verify "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --output "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --output "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --build "D:/Builds/MyGame-v1"
python -m steam_release_guard stage "D:/Builds/MyGame-v1" --release "D:/Release/MyGame-v1" --symbols "D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard stage-cleanup "D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1/srg-stage-journal.json" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard build-script "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --tool "D:/Engines/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe" --output "D:/Builds/MyGame-v2" --save "D:/Builds/build-my-game-v2.ps1" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced --help
```

Replace `BACKUP_ID` with the directory name returned by the backup operation. Restore targets must not exist. The three staging directories must not overlap; release/private-symbol destinations must be new or empty. `audit` **writes HTML/JSON reports**, even with `--json`; a default read-only AI review should not run it automatically. Build scripts are generated, not executed. Existing CLI, GUI, reports and rollback entry points remain available.

[Unity](workflows/unity-release.md) · [Unreal](workflows/unreal-release.md) · [Package inspection](workflows/security-audit.md) · [Rollback and cleanup](workflows/rollback.md) — detailed workflows are currently in Chinese.
</details>

<a id="ai-skill"></a>
## AI Skill: use your preferred assistant

**One Skill. One prompt.** Every AI button below opens the same universal instructions. Use the same approach with other assistants that can read Markdown.

<a id="use-with-ai"></a>
### Copy this to your AI

```text
Read README.en.md and skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md at https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard.
Set up the entire game-release-guard Skill folder, including engines/ and LICENSE, for this project. Use project-level native Skill support if available; otherwise read it as Markdown.
Identify the engine and version, then read only its engine guide. Preserve existing instructions. Do not overwrite files, change global configuration or install extra software.
State the actual path and wait for my task: review means read-only; an explicit hardening request means back up, implement scoped changes and verify. Do not upload the project or create a separate report.
```

**Or download it directly:** [Standalone Skill ZIP](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/raw/refs/heads/main/downloads/game-release-guard-skill.zip) · [SKILL.md](skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md) · [LICENSE](skills/game-release-guard/LICENSE)

The ZIP contains one core Skill, five engine guides, the MIT license and bilingual instructions: 11 files, with no Python required. Keep the whole folder. The AI identifies the project and reads only its engine guide. Without internet access, use the local folder. The core text is Chinese; request replies in your preferred language.

[中文使用说明](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.md) · [English guide](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.en.md) · [Example prompts](skills/game-release-guard/EXAMPLES.en.md)

## Unity capabilities

- The Unity 6 Editor-only entry point installs into `Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/`. Installation does not change `PlayerSettings`. Its menu builds Windows x64 IL2CPP without development, script debugging or Profiler options.
- Only this toolkit's own build path is checked. Regular Build, custom pipelines, Addressables and AssetBundles need separate integration. Custom Build Profiles are currently rejected to avoid silently ignoring scene or settings overrides.
- Settings are restored on normal exit; inspect them after a crash or forced termination. Obfuz builds run only on a copy restored from a verified full backup.
- Obfuz 4.0.0 uses explicit type and exclusion lists, preserves fields by default and saves private mappings/assembly evidence. String/control-flow protection is currently rejected. Metadata encryption is not implemented.
- IL2CPP still exposes analyzable native code, strings and metadata. Do not delete `global-metadata.dat` or required runtime plugins.

## Unreal capabilities

- Basic adaptation provides Shipping configuration, excludes distributed debug files and checks settings. GUID/source-engine associations are not treated as a verified version. Platform overrides, Target rules and UAT logs require separate review.
- Advanced schema 2 explicitly selects `pak-only` or `pak+iostore`, with independent CryptoKeys switches and preserved keys/rollback. `iostore-only` is unverified for the current Windows/UAT profile and stops before writing. Schema 1 is not migrated automatically.
- Pak and IoStore have independent version, help-argument and evidence checks. UE 5.6 IoStoreUtilities uses the official `UnrealPak.exe IoStore` module entry point. Missing tools, incompatible versions or unsupported arguments produce NOT_VERIFIED.
- The legacy basic option `--encrypt-pak-index` sets `UsePakFile=True` while preserving `bUseIoStore`; review that container change before using it. Index encryption does not establish asset-content or IoStore encryption.
- AES validation checks existing nonzero 32-byte material only. Signing-field encoding checks do not prove a valid RSA key pair or signature. Production keys are not generated/rotated, and all-asset encryption is not enabled automatically.

## Godot, Cocos Creator and GameMaker

[Godot 4.x](skills/game-release-guard/engines/godot.md): PCK AES-256 requires custom templates compiled with the matching key. GDScript tokens/compression are not encryption.

[Cocos Creator 3.x / 4.x](skills/game-release-guard/engines/cocos-creator.md): Native Encrypt JS requires JSC and script-backup exclusion checks; do not apply it to Web builds.

[GameMaker](skills/game-release-guard/engines/gamemaker.md): evaluate YYC against the actual Runtime and target. Native compilation is not resource encryption.

An explicit hardening request lets the AI back up and implement applicable code/configuration changes. Protection still needs build/runtime evidence in the user's environment. This update validates Skill behavior on synthetic projects; it adds no engine adapters or real-engine builds.

<a id="advanced"></a>
## Advanced protection: capabilities and status

<a id="status"></a>
**Implemented** means code exists; **Tested** names a tested scope; **Not Verified** means evidence is missing; **Not Implemented** means the feature is absent. Implementation, testing and protection effectiveness are separate claims.

| Capability | Implemented / tested | Unverified / absent |
|---|---|---|
| Backup, transactions, rollback, CLI / GUI | **Implemented · Tested**: synthetic projects, failure recovery, actual Tk callbacks | No hardware power-loss, external-dependency or cross-host recovery guarantee |
| Obfuz integration | **Implemented · Tested**: synthetic tests; Unity 6000.6.6f1 + pinned-source API compilation | **Not Verified**: obfuscated build, startup, reflection, saves, RPC, plugins |
| IL2CPP / native inspection | **Implemented · Tested**: PE, dependencies, metadata headers, sensitive-data candidates | **Not Implemented**: metadata encryption; **Not Verified**: IL2CPP game runtime |
| Pak / IoStore | **Implemented · Tested**: synthetic policy, key preservation, separate invocation/evidence tests | **Not Verified**: real UE encryption/signing, resource loads, DLC, patches |
| A/B/C asset classification | **Implemented · Tested**: classification and cost advice | **Not Implemented**: automatic chunks, per-grade encryption, loader rewrites |
| Integrity / DRM artifacts | **Implemented · Tested**: SHA256, external trusted pin, build-id, changes and PE checks | **Not Implemented**: automatic in-game integration; **Not Verified**: Steam Wrapper / test branch |
| Client security inspection | **Implemented · Tested**: file/line candidates and existing-protection clues | Heuristics do not prove server security or vulnerability reachability |
| Performance / three-layer acceptance | **Implemented · Tested**: samples, evidence hashes, metric comparisons | **Not Verified**: real startup, loading, memory, CPU, build/patch size, crashes, networking and save performance |

Advanced operations require explicit `advanced` commands; the GUI's basic adaptation does not run them by default. [P3 workflow](workflows/advanced-protection.md) · [Third-party sources and licenses](docs/P3-REVIEW.md) · [Metadata limits](docs/P3-METADATA.md)

<a id="boundaries"></a>
## Security boundaries

**Configuration ≠ build artifacts ≠ runtime behavior.** Exit code 0, a file extension, Shipping, IL2CPP, compression or index encryption is not proof against cracking. `REVIEW` / `NOT_VERIFIED` never become PASS. `RECORDED_PASS` only associates a declaration with evidence files; it is not independent runtime acceptance.

- This is not anti-cheat, an asset encryption shell or a piracy guarantee. Important transactions, rewards, permissions and settlement require trusted server validation; client secrets can be analyzed.
- Local operations do not upload projects. Review cloud AI data handling and third-party engine/plugin behavior separately. The Skill is not offline isolation or a permission sandbox.
- **Steam DRM is an explicit exception**: `scripts/Invoke-SteamDrm.ps1` uploads the EXE to Valve and requires explicit authorization plus `-AcknowledgeExecutableUpload`. It preserves the original EXE and does not upload a depot or publish a game. Online/offline behavior and a fresh test-branch installation still need testing.
- Keep backups, logs, reports, mappings, symbols and Crypto.json private and outside the depot. Backups are not encrypted at rest. Reports omit key values, but paths can also be confidential.
- Full backups include hidden files, `.git`, caches and builds inside the project, not external dependencies/saves/engines. Links/junctions are rejected; ACLs, alternate data streams and other filesystem metadata are not guaranteed. Restore creates a new directory only.
- Unknown files are not deleted and keys are not changed. Conflicts, corruption or INCOMPLETE state must stop the operation; rollback never forces over later edits. Staging requires hard-link support, cleanup handles only verified failed copies, and resume is not supported. Local locks do not stop external editors or coordinate network drives/multiple hosts.
- “One click” does not include engine installation, compatibility proof, Steam upload or launch approval. Saves, reflection, networking, DLC, patches and performance require real-build testing.

[Security policy](SECURITY.md) · [Full operational limits, Chinese](docs/USAGE.zh-CN.md) · [Manual release checklist](docs/RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md)

<a id="validation"></a>
## Tests and validation

```shell
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
python -m compileall -q steam_release_guard
```

CI covers **Windows / Linux × Python 3.10 / 3.12**: behavior, backup recovery, transactions, documented commands and module compilation. Windows also parses PowerShell scripts. Headless Linux skips GUI and Windows file-sharing tests; Windows CI runs them. The local Windows 10 environment skips the existing symlink test when permissions are unavailable.

PR #2 and the updated PR #1 passed their matrices. See the live badge and [Actions](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions) for the current commit. Synthetic fixtures are not real games; API compilation is not an engine build or game runtime test. No Stable release is published.

[Five-engine Skill validation](docs/MULTI-ENGINE-SKILL-VALIDATION.md) · [P0 evidence](docs/P0-ACCEPTANCE-20261125.md) · [Backup evidence](docs/FULL-BACKUP-VALIDATION-20261125.md) · [P3 evidence](docs/P3-VALIDATION.md) · [Skill evidence](docs/SKILL-VALIDATION-20261125.md)

```text
steam_release_guard/        CLI, GUI, backup, transactions
  advanced/                 Opt-in adapters and evidence checks
adapters/                   Unity / Obfuz Editor bridges
skills/game-release-guard/   Core Skill + engines/, installation, examples, license
workflows/                  Backup, engines, inspection, rollback
docs/                       Sources, validation, detailed usage
downloads/                  Standalone Skill ZIP (no toolkit required)
scripts/                    Launcher, API checks, Skill packaging, Steam DRM
tests/                      Behavioral, recovery and documentation tests
```

<p align="center"><a href="README.md">简体中文</a> | <a href="README.en.md">English</a></p>

![Game Release Guard — Protect your game. From day one.](docs/assets/hero.svg)

<h1 align="center">Game Release Guard</h1>
<p align="center"><strong>Protect your game. From day one.</strong><br/>从开发第一天开始，保护你的游戏。</p>
<p align="center">面向 Unity、Unreal、Godot、Cocos Creator 和 GameMaker 的统一安全开发与加固 Skill，附 Unity / Unreal 本地发行工具。</p>

<p align="center"><a href="README.md">简体中文</a> | <a href="README.en.md">English</a></p>

[![CI](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/workflows/test.yml/badge.svg?branch=main)](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/workflows/test.yml)
[![Early Development](https://img.shields.io/badge/status-Early%20Development-466985)](#status)
[![Python 3.10+](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-3.10%2B-3675a8)](#quick-start)
[![MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-71869b)](LICENSE)

**Multi-Engine AI Skills V1.0 · 本地工具仍为 Early Development / Advanced Protection Candidate。** P0、P3 和独立 Skill 已整合到 `main`，程序版本仍为 `0.2.0.dev0`；不宣称 Stable / Production Ready。通过 [main ZIP](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/archive/refs/heads/main.zip) 或 Git 获取源码。[Releases](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/releases) 只展示实际发布内容，当前没有稳定发行包。

[快速开始](#quick-start) · [安装 Skill](#ai-skill) · [能力与状态](#advanced) · [安全边界](#boundaries) · [验证证据](#validation)

## 核心功能

| 功能 | 能做什么 |
|---|---|
| **AI Secure Development** | 从项目初始化开始，按当前引擎遵守安全开发规范，不额外生成报告。 |
| **Existing Project Protection** | “检查”只读并指出具体问题；明确要求“加固”后，AI 备份并实施适用修改、验证兼容性。 |
| **Unity & Unreal Release Toolkit** | 已有本地 CLI / Tkinter GUI：备份恢复、配置适配、发布检查、显式高级流程与回滚。 |
| **Godot Security Skill** | GDScript 导出、PCK AES-256、自定义模板、密钥管理及补丁/模组来源验证指引。 |
| **Cocos Creator Security Skill** | 按版本区分 Native / Web，处理 Encrypt JS、JSC、脚本备份及资源/热更新检查。 |
| **GameMaker Security Skill** | 区分 Runtime、VM / YYC，检查开发入口、Included Files、客户端数据及发行验证。 |

**一个 Skill 覆盖五种引擎。** Unity / Unreal 有本地 CLI / GUI；Godot、Cocos Creator、GameMaker 本阶段仅由用户的 AI 按 Skill 操作工程，不提供本地自动化加密工具。

<a id="quick-start"></a>
## 30 秒快速开始

**只用 AI？下载 [完整 Skill ZIP](downloads/game-release-guard-skill.zip)，把下方 [通用提示词](#use-with-ai) 交给助手，无需 Python。**

本地工具需要已有 **Python 3.10+**；GUI 另需 `tkinter`。标准库实现，无需 `pip install`，不会自动安装 Python、引擎或保护器。适配面向 Windows / Steam；Linux CI 验证工具逻辑，不代表支持 Linux 游戏构建。

```shell
git clone https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard.git
cd game-release-guard
python -m steam_release_guard --help
```

项目原名 Steam Release Guard。更名后 Python 包和 CLI 仍使用 `steam_release_guard`，已有 Unity 菜单与私有备份目录名也保留，兼容旧工程。

Windows 可双击 `Launch.cmd` 打开 GUI。找不到 Python 时，将已有解释器路径写入 `.local/python-path.txt` 或设置进程变量 `SRG_PYTHON`。把下面路径换成自己的**测试副本**，先预览：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy"
python -m steam_release_guard apply "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
```

首次使用真实工程：关闭引擎和同步工具 → 完整备份 → 核验 → 恢复新目录 → 在副本尝试。`apply` 有修改时仍必须再次完成工程外的完整备份，失败就停止；写入前审阅并明确确认，`--yes` 不是自动授权。[备份流程](workflows/backup.md) · [详细操作参考](docs/USAGE.zh-CN.md)

<details>
<summary>备份、检查与恢复命令</summary>

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard backup "D:/Games/MyGame" --backup-dir "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup "D:/Games/MyGame" --backup-dir "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-verify "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --output "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore "D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/BACKUP_ID" --output "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --build "D:/Builds/MyGame-v1"
python -m steam_release_guard stage "D:/Builds/MyGame-v1" --release "D:/Release/MyGame-v1" --symbols "D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard stage-cleanup "D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1/srg-stage-journal.json" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard build-script "D:/Games/MyGame-Copy" --tool "D:/Engines/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe" --output "D:/Builds/MyGame-v2" --save "D:/Builds/build-my-game-v2.ps1" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced --help
```

`BACKUP_ID` 换成返回的真实目录名。恢复目标必须不存在；stage 的三个目录不能嵌套，发布/私有符号目录必须新建或为空。`audit` **会写 HTML/JSON 报告**，即使带 `--json`；AI 默认只读检查不应自动调用它。构建脚本只生成、不执行。旧 CLI、GUI、报告与回滚入口保留。

[Unity](workflows/unity-release.md) · [Unreal](workflows/unreal-release.md) · [发布包检查](workflows/security-audit.md) · [回滚与清理](workflows/rollback.md)
</details>

<a id="use-with-ai"></a>
### 复制给 AI

```text
请读取 https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard 的 README.md 和 skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md。
为当前游戏项目安装并使用整个 game-release-guard Skill 目录，包括 engines/ 和 LICENSE；支持原生 Skill 时使用项目级安装，否则按 Markdown 阅读。
先识别引擎和版本，只加载对应引擎文档。保留已有指令，不覆盖同名文件，不改全局配置、不安装额外软件。
安装后说明实际路径，等待任务：我说“检查”就只读；明确要求“加固”时，在范围内备份后实际修改和验证。不擅自上传工程或生成独立报告。
```

**也可以直接下载：** [独立 Skill ZIP](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/raw/refs/heads/main/downloads/game-release-guard-skill.zip) · [SKILL.md](skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md) · [LICENSE](skills/game-release-guard/LICENSE)

ZIP 包含一个核心 Skill、五份引擎知识、MIT 许可及中英文说明，共 11 个文件，无需 Python。保留整个目录；AI 识别项目后只读对应引擎文档。无法联网时提供本地目录，核心正文为中文，可要求用你的语言回复。

[中文使用说明](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.md) · [English guide](skills/game-release-guard/INSTALL.en.md) · [提示词示例](skills/game-release-guard/EXAMPLES.md)

## Unity 功能

- Unity 6 Editor-only 入口安装到 `Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/`，安装本身不改 `PlayerSettings`。专用菜单构建 Windows x64 IL2CPP、非开发版本，关闭脚本调试/Profiler。
- 只校验本工具自己的构建入口；普通 Build、自定义流水线、Addressables、AssetBundles 需另行集成。当前拒绝自定义 Build Profile，避免忽略场景和设置覆盖。
- 正常退出恢复设置；强退/崩溃后须人工检查。Obfuz 构建只在核验过的备份恢复副本执行。
- Obfuz 4.0.0 使用明确类型名单、排除名单，默认保留字段，保存私有映射和程序集证据。字符串/控制流保护暂拒绝启用，没有 metadata 加密。
- IL2CPP 仍有可分析的原生代码、字符串和 metadata；不要删除 `global-metadata.dat` 或运行必需的插件。

## Unreal 功能

- 基础适配提供 Shipping、关闭随包调试文件及配置检查；GUID/源码引擎关联不能视为已识别版本。平台覆盖、Target 规则和 UAT 日志需另行检查。
- 高级 schema 2 明确选择 `pak-only` 或 `pak+iostore`，各 CryptoKeys 开关独立，保留密钥和回滚。当前 Windows/UAT profile 的 `iostore-only` 未验证，写入前停止；旧 schema 1 不自动迁移。
- Pak / IoStore 独立检查版本、帮助参数和记录。UE 5.6 IoStoreUtilities 使用官方 `UnrealPak.exe IoStore` 模块入口；缺工具、版本或参数不符记 NOT_VERIFIED。
- 基础兼容参数 `--encrypt-pak-index` 会设置 `UsePakFile=True` 并保留 `bUseIoStore`，使用前必须审核这一容器变化；索引加密不等于资源主体或 IoStore 加密。
- AES 只检查已有非零 32 字节密钥；签名字段编码检查不是 RSA key 对/签名有效性证明。不生成或轮换生产密钥，不自动开启全资源加密。

## Godot、Cocos Creator 与 GameMaker

[Godot 4.x](skills/game-release-guard/engines/godot.md)：PCK AES-256 需要同密钥自编译模板，GDScript token/压缩不是加密。

[Cocos Creator 3.x / 4.x](skills/game-release-guard/engines/cocos-creator.md)：Native Encrypt JS 要核验 JSC 与 script-backup 排除，不能套用 Web。

[GameMaker](skills/game-release-guard/engines/gamemaker.md)：按 Runtime 和目标评估 YYC，原生编译不等于资源加密。

明确要求加固后，AI 可在备份后实施适用的代码/配置修改；实际效果仍需用户环境的构建和运行证据。本次只做合成项目的 Skill 行为验证，没有新增引擎适配器或真实构建。

<a id="advanced"></a>
## 高级保护：能力与状态

<a id="status"></a>
**Implemented** = 代码已提供；**Tested** = 指定范围已测试；**Not Verified** = 缺实际证据；**Not Implemented** = 尚未提供。实现、测试和保护效果是不同层级。

| 能力 | 已实现 / 已测试 | 未验证 / 未实现 |
|---|---|---|
| 备份、事务、回滚、CLI / GUI | **Implemented · Tested**：合成工程、失败恢复、真实 Tk 回调 | 不保证硬件断电、工程外依赖或跨主机恢复 |
| Obfuz 适配 | **Implemented · Tested**：合成测试；Unity 6000.6.6f1 + 固定源码 API 编译 | **Not Verified**：混淆构建、启动、反射、存档、RPC、插件 |
| IL2CPP / 原生模块检查 | **Implemented · Tested**：PE、依赖、metadata 头、敏感候选 | **Not Implemented**：metadata 加密；**Not Verified**：IL2CPP 游戏运行 |
| Pak / IoStore | **Implemented · Tested**：策略、密钥保留、独立调用/证据的合成测试 | **Not Verified**：真实 UE 加密/签名、资源加载、DLC、补丁 |
| A/B/C 资源分级 | **Implemented · Tested**：分类及成本建议 | **Not Implemented**：自动分块、分级加密、加载器改写 |
| 完整性 / DRM 产物 | **Implemented · Tested**：SHA256、外部可信 pin、build-id、变化和 PE 检查 | **Not Implemented**：游戏内自动集成；**Not Verified**：Steam Wrapper / 测试分支 |
| 客户端安全检查 | **Implemented · Tested**：文件/行号候选、已有保护线索 | 启发式检查，不能证明服务端安全或漏洞可达性 |
| 性能 / 三层验收 | **Implemented · Tested**：样本、证据哈希、指标对照 | **Not Verified**：真实启动、加载、内存、CPU、包体、补丁、崩溃、联机及存档性能 |

高级操作通过显式 `advanced` 命令启用，GUI 基础适配不会默认运行它们。[P3 工作流](workflows/advanced-protection.md) · [第三方来源与许可](docs/P3-REVIEW.md) · [metadata 边界](docs/P3-METADATA.md)

<a id="boundaries"></a>
## 安全边界

**配置 ≠ 构建产物 ≠ 真实运行。** 退出码 0、扩展名、Shipping、IL2CPP、压缩或索引加密都不是防破解证明。`REVIEW` / `NOT_VERIFIED` 不升级为 PASS；`RECORDED_PASS` 只表示声明与证据文件匹配，不是独立实机验收。

- 不是反作弊系统、资源加密壳或反盗版保证。重要交易、奖励、权限和结算须由可信服务端验证；客户端秘密可被分析。
- 本地工具不上传工程；云端 AI 的数据使用和第三方引擎/插件行为需另行确认。Skill 不是离线隔离或权限沙箱。
- **Steam DRM 是明确例外**：独立的 `scripts/Invoke-SteamDrm.ps1` 会上传 EXE 给 Valve，必须明确授权并使用 `-AcknowledgeExecutableUpload`。保留原 EXE，不上传 depot、不自动发行；在线/离线和测试分支重新安装仍需实测。
- 备份、日志、报告、映射、符号、Crypto.json 保持私有并放在 depot 外；备份未做静态加密。报告不复制密钥值，但路径也可能保密。
- 完整备份包括工程内隐藏文件、`.git`、缓存和构建，不涵盖外部依赖/存档/引擎；拒绝链接和连接点，不保证 ACL、备用数据流等元数据。恢复只创建新目录。
- 不删除未知文件、不改密钥。冲突、损坏或 INCOMPLETE 状态应停止；回滚不强行覆盖后续改动。stage 需要支持硬链接的文件系统，失败清理只处理已核实副本，无续传；本机锁不阻止外部编辑器，不协调网络盘/跨主机。
- “一键”不包括安装引擎、兼容性证明、Steam 上传或上线批准。存档、反射、联机、DLC、补丁与性能仍需真实构建验证。

[安全政策](SECURITY.md) · [完整操作限制](docs/USAGE.zh-CN.md) · [发布人工检查表](docs/RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md)

<a id="validation"></a>
## 测试和验证

```shell
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
python -m compileall -q steam_release_guard
```

CI 覆盖 **Windows / Linux × Python 3.10 / 3.12**：行为、备份恢复、事务、文档命令、模块编译；Windows 另检查 PowerShell 语法。Linux 无桌面显示时跳过 GUI 和 Windows 文件占用测试；Windows CI 执行这些项目。本机 Windows 10 的旧 symlink 测试受权限限制跳过。

PR #2 和更新后的 PR #1 矩阵均通过；当前提交看顶部实时 CI 徽章与 [Actions](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions)。合成夹具不是实际游戏，API 编译不是引擎构建或游戏运行。当前未发布 Stable 版本。

[五引擎 Skill 验证](docs/MULTI-ENGINE-SKILL-VALIDATION.md) · [P0 验证](docs/P0-ACCEPTANCE-20261125.md) · [备份验证](docs/FULL-BACKUP-VALIDATION-20261125.md) · [P3 验证](docs/P3-VALIDATION.md) · [Skill 验证](docs/SKILL-VALIDATION-20261125.md)

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security and privacy boundaries

Game Release Guard is a local release review aid. It does not claim to prevent copying, reverse engineering, resource extraction or piracy, and it is not anti-cheat software.

- `plan`, `apply`, `audit`, `stage` and `rollback` make no network calls. Opening an HTML report uses the local browser and a self-contained page without scripts or remote assets.
- `Invoke-SteamDrm.ps1` is the explicit exception: it invokes the user's SteamCMD and uploads the input EXE to Valve after `-AcknowledgeExecutableUpload` is supplied. It does not include credentials or upload a depot.
- A generated engine build script invokes the locally installed engine. Engines, plugins and custom build hooks have their own behavior and may contact services. Inspect those separately.
- P3 `advanced unity-build` and `advanced unreal-verify` invoke explicitly selected local tools after preview/consent. They are not a sandbox for vendor code. No downloads, activation, key generation or upload are requested by SRG. Logs/mappings/evidence are private and remain outside the depot; process interruption can leave an incomplete copy and engine child processes. Verify that the engine has stopped before inspecting or retrying that copy.
- P3 read-only inspections print results without creating reports. Integrity checks require a separately trusted manifest hash and build ID; a checksum stored beside a replaceable client is not authentication. Acceptance checks verify evidence file hashes and supplied measurements, not the truth of a declared runtime test. Configuration, artifact and runtime evidence remain separate.
- Reports omit source snippets and key values, but file names can still be confidential. Keep reports and backups private. Backups may contain original crypto configuration; they are not encrypted at rest.
- The tool excludes its state from Git, and stops on backup corruption, path traversal, symlinks/junctions during inspection, conflicting later edits or occupied staging directories.
- Source inspection is heuristic. Do not interpret a match as proof of a vulnerability or an absence of matches as proof of safety. The tool does not parse Blueprint bytecode or binary plugins.
- Project files and transactions are intended to be used sequentially with the editor closed. Do not run simultaneous writers or edit configuration while a transaction is in progress.
- Always retain your own project version control and off-machine recovery plan. Local recovery journals do not replace a complete project backup.

If reporting a defect, provide the tool version, command, sanitized file layout and a minimal synthetic reproduction. Do not post game assets, Steam secrets, private symbols or encryption keys in issues.

## Reporting a vulnerability

This is an early-development project, not a supported stable security product. If the repository Security page offers **Report a vulnerability**, use that private channel. If it is unavailable, open an Issue asking for a private contact route without posting exploit details or confidential data. Do not attach a real game, backup, credential or private build. A maintainer response time is not guaranteed.

If a real credential has already been disclosed, its owner should revoke/rotate it and review accessible history, branches, logs and artifacts. Removing only the current file does not remove past exposure. This project will not rotate a user's keys automatically.

## 中文安全报告说明

项目仍处于早期开发，不是有稳定支持承诺的安全产品。仓库 Security 页面如提供 **Report a vulnerability**，请使用其私有渠道；未提供时，可提交不含漏洞利用细节的 Issue 请求私下联系。不要附真实游戏、备份、凭据或私有构建，不承诺固定响应时限。

真实凭据泄露后，由责任人撤销/轮换，并检查可访问历史、分支、日志和附件；只删除当前文件不能消除历史泄露。本工具不会替用户自动轮换密钥。详细限制见上文及中英文 README。

```

`adapters/unity-advanced/SteamReleaseGuardObfuscation.cs`:

```cs
// Steam Release Guard - MIT. Obfuz 4.0.0, Unity 6000.x. Editor only.
// No engine patches, key creation, metadata encryption or runtime approval.
#if UNITY_EDITOR && UNITY_6000_0_OR_NEWER
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using dnlib.DotNet;
using Obfuz.ObfusPasses;
using Obfuz.Settings;
using Obfuz.Unity;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEngine;

namespace SteamReleaseGuard.Advanced
{
    public static class ObfuscatedBuild
    {
        [Serializable] public class AssemblySelection
        {
            public string name;
            public string[] include_types;
            public string[] exclude_types;
            public bool rename_fields;
        }
        [Serializable] public class Configuration
        {
            public int schema;
            public string engine_version, adapter, tool_version;
            public AssemblySelection[] assemblies;
            public string[] reference_assemblies;
            public bool strings, control_flow;
        }
        [Serializable] public class AssemblyEvidence
        {
            public string name, before_sha256, after_sha256;
            public int renamed_selected_types;
        }
        [Serializable] public class Receipt
        {
            public string configuration = "PASS", artifact = "PASS", runtime = "NOT_VERIFIED";
            public string engine_version, tool_version, executable_sha256;
            public AssemblyEvidence[] assemblies;
        }

        private static string Argument(string key)
        {
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            var i = Array.IndexOf(args, key);
            if (i < 0 || i + 1 >= args.Length || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(args[i + 1]))
                throw new BuildFailedException("Missing explicit build output/evidence argument.");
            return Path.GetFullPath(args[i + 1]);
        }

        private static string Hash(string path)
        {
            using (var hash = SHA256.Create())
            using (var input = File.OpenRead(path))
                return BitConverter.ToString(hash.ComputeHash(input)).Replace("-", "").ToLowerInvariant();
        }

        private static bool Excluded(string name, string[] exclusions)
        {
            return exclusions.Any(p => p.EndsWith("*", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                ? name.StartsWith(p.Substring(0, p.Length - 1), StringComparison.Ordinal) : name == p);
        }

        public static void BuildWindows()
        {
            const string configPath = "ProjectSettings/SteamReleaseGuardObfuscation.json";
            const string settingsPath = "ProjectSettings/Obfuz.asset";
            var config = JsonUtility.FromJson<Configuration>(File.ReadAllText(configPath));
            if (config.schema != 1 || config.adapter != "obfuz" || config.tool_version != "4.0.0" ||
                config.engine_version != Application.unityVersion || config.strings || config.control_flow ||
                config.assemblies == null || config.assemblies.Length == 0)
                throw new BuildFailedException("Unsupported obfuscation configuration or exact Editor version mismatch.");
            var package = UnityEditor.PackageManager.PackageInfo.FindForAssembly(typeof(ObfuzSettings).Assembly);
            if (package == null || package.name != "com.code-philosophy.obfuz" || package.version != "4.0.0")
                throw new BuildFailedException("The Editor did not resolve reviewed Obfuz 4.0.0.");
            if (!File.Exists(settingsPath))
                throw new BuildFailedException("Configure Obfuz yourself first. SRG does not create keys or a VM.");
            var evidence = Argument("-srgEvidence");
            var output = Argument("-srgOutput");
            if (!Directory.Exists(evidence) || File.Exists(Path.Combine(evidence, "obfuscation.json")) ||
                File.Exists(Path.Combine(evidence, "symbol-mapping.xml")))
                throw new BuildFailedException("Use a new private evidence directory.");
            var settingsBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(settingsPath);
            var settings = ObfuzSettings.Instance;
            var saved = EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(settings);
            if (settings.encryptionVMSettings == null || !File.Exists(settings.encryptionVMSettings.codeOutputPath))
                throw new BuildFailedException("Obfuz's existing generated VM is required even for symbol-only builds. Set it up and review it first.");
            var rows = new List<AssemblyEvidence>();
            bool completed = false;
            Action<ObfuscationEndEventArgs> onEnd = args =>
            {
                if (!args.success) throw new BuildFailedException("Obfuz failed; refusing to approve this build.");
                foreach (var selection in config.assemblies)
                {
                    var before = Path.Combine(args.originalScriptAssembliesPath, selection.name + ".dll");
                    var after = Path.Combine(args.obfuscatedScriptAssembliesPath, selection.name + ".dll");
                    if (!File.Exists(before) || !File.Exists(after))
                        throw new BuildFailedException("Obfuscation output/backup assembly missing.");
                    int changed = 0;
                    // dnlib validates actual managed metadata; no Assembly.Load of game code.
                    using (var original = ModuleDefMD.Load(before))
                    using (var obfuscated = ModuleDefMD.Load(after))
                    {
                        if (original.Assembly.Name != obfuscated.Assembly.Name)
                            throw new BuildFailedException("Unexpected assembly identity change.");
                        var oldTypes = new HashSet<string>(original.GetTypes().Select(t => t.FullName));
                        var newTypes = new HashSet<string>(obfuscated.GetTypes().Select(t => t.FullName));
                        foreach (var type in selection.include_types)
                        {
                            if (Excluded(type, selection.exclude_types)) continue;
                            if (!oldTypes.Contains(type)) throw new BuildFailedException("Selected type missing from original player assembly.");
                            if (!newTypes.Contains(type)) changed++;
                        }
                        // Excluded concrete types that existed must keep their names.
                        foreach (var type in oldTypes.Where(n => Excluded(n, selection.exclude_types)))
                            if (!newTypes.Contains(type)) throw new BuildFailedException("Excluded type was renamed.");
                    }
                    if (changed == 0 || Hash(before) == Hash(after))
                        throw new BuildFailedException("No selected-type rename evidence. Choose eligible types; Unity/serialization rules may preserve names.");
                    rows.Add(new AssemblyEvidence { name = selection.name, before_sha256 = Hash(before),
                        after_sha256 = Hash(after), renamed_selected_types = changed });
                }
                completed = true;
            };
            try
            {
                settings.buildPipelineSettings.enable = true;
                settings.assemblySettings.assembliesToObfuscate = config.assemblies.Select(a => a.name).ToArray();
                settings.assemblySettings.nonObfuscatedButReferencingObfuscatedAssemblies = config.reference_assemblies;
                settings.assemblySettings.obfuscateObfuzRuntime = false;
                settings.obfuscationPassSettings.enabledPasses = ObfuscationPassType.SymbolObfus;
                settings.obfuscationPassSettings.ruleFiles = Array.Empty<string>();
                settings.symbolObfusSettings.debug = false;
                settings.symbolObfusSettings.detectReflectionCompatibility = true;
                settings.symbolObfusSettings.customRenamePolicyTypes = Array.Empty<string>();
                settings.symbolObfusSettings.ruleFiles = new[] { "Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/AdvancedEditor/rename.xml" };
                settings.symbolObfusSettings.symbolMappingFile = Path.Combine(evidence, "symbol-mapping.xml");
                ObfuscationProcess.OnObfuscationEnd += onEnd;
                SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.ReleaseBuilder.BuildWindows();
                if (!completed || !File.Exists(output))
                    throw new BuildFailedException("Missing Obfuz event or game executable. Incremental build is not fresh evidence.");
                var receipt = new Receipt { engine_version = Application.unityVersion, tool_version = package.version,
                    executable_sha256 = Hash(output), assemblies = rows.ToArray() };
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(evidence, "obfuscation.json"), JsonUtility.ToJson(receipt, true));
            }
            finally
            {
                ObfuscationProcess.OnObfuscationEnd -= onEnd;
                EditorJsonUtility.FromJsonOverwrite(saved, settings);
                File.WriteAllBytes(settingsPath, settingsBytes);
            }
        }
    }
}
#endif

```

`adapters/unity/SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.asmdef`:

```asmdef
{
  "name": "SteamReleaseGuard.Editor",
  "rootNamespace": "SteamReleaseGuard.Editor",
  "includePlatforms": ["Editor"],
  "autoReferenced": true,
  "noEngineReferences": false
}

`adapters/unity/SteamReleaseGuardBuild.cs`:

```cs
// Steam Release Guard - MIT. Unity 6 (6000.x), Windows x64 platform profile.
// This adapter is Editor-only. IL2CPP does not encrypt assets or prevent reverse engineering.
#if UNITY_EDITOR && UNITY_6000_0_OR_NEWER
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEditor.Build.Profile;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEngine;

namespace SteamReleaseGuard.Editor
{
    public static class ReleaseBuilder
    {
        internal static bool Building;
        internal const BuildOptions Forbidden = BuildOptions.Development | BuildOptions.AllowDebugging
            | BuildOptions.ConnectWithProfiler | BuildOptions.EnableDeepProfilingSupport
            | BuildOptions.IncludeTestAssemblies | BuildOptions.EnableCodeCoverage
            | BuildOptions.WaitForPlayerConnection;

        [MenuItem("Tools/Steam Release Guard/Build Windows Release")]
        public static void BuildWindows()
        {
            if (!Application.unityVersion.StartsWith("6000.", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                throw new BuildFailedException("This adapter targets Unity 6 (6000.x). Review other versions first.");
            // Never silently discard custom profile overrides or silently change the selected scene list.
            if (BuildProfile.GetActiveBuildProfile() != null)
                throw new BuildFailedException("Select the Windows platform profile (not a custom Build Profile) first. Custom overrides need a separate integration review.");
            if (EditorUserBuildSettings.activeBuildTarget != BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64)
                throw new BuildFailedException("Switch to Windows x64 first, or launch Unity with -buildTarget Win64.");
            if (!BuildPipeline.IsBuildTargetSupported(BuildTargetGroup.Standalone, BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64))
                throw new BuildFailedException("Windows build support is missing. Install the matching Windows IL2CPP module through Unity Hub.");
            var scenes = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Where(scene => scene.enabled).Select(scene => scene.path).ToArray();
            if (scenes.Length == 0)
                throw new BuildFailedException("Enable the intended release scenes in the Windows platform profile first.");
            var output = CommandArgument("-srgOutput");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(output))
            {
                if (Application.isBatchMode)
                    throw new BuildFailedException("Batch builds require -srgOutput <absolute path to Game.exe>.");
                var folder = EditorUtility.OpenFolderPanel("Choose a new empty release-build folder", "", "");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(folder)) return;
                output = Path.Combine(folder, "Game.exe");
            }
            output = Path.GetFullPath(output);
            if (!output.EndsWith(".exe", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new BuildFailedException("The Windows output must end in .exe.");
            var directory = Path.GetDirectoryName(output);
            if (Directory.Exists(directory) && Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(directory).Any())
                throw new BuildFailedException("Choose a new empty output folder. Existing builds are never overwritten.");

            var target = NamedBuildTarget.Standalone;
            var previousBackend = PlayerSettings.GetScriptingBackend(target);
            var previousDevelopment = EditorUserBuildSettings.development;
            var previousDebugging = EditorUserBuildSettings.allowDebugging;
            var previousProfiler = EditorUserBuildSettings.connectProfiler;
            var previousDeepProfiling = EditorUserBuildSettings.buildWithDeepProfilingSupport;
            var previousSubtarget = EditorUserBuildSettings.standaloneBuildSubtarget;
            try
            {
                PlayerSettings.SetScriptingBackend(target, ScriptingImplementation.IL2CPP);
                EditorUserBuildSettings.development = false;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.allowDebugging = false;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.connectProfiler = false;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.buildWithDeepProfilingSupport = false;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.standaloneBuildSubtarget = StandaloneBuildSubtarget.Player;
                Building = true;
                var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions
                {
                    scenes = scenes,
                    locationPathName = output,
                    target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64,
                    subtarget = (int)StandaloneBuildSubtarget.Player,
                    options = BuildOptions.StrictMode
                });
                if (report.summary.result != BuildResult.Succeeded)
                    throw new BuildFailedException("Release build failed. Read the Unity build log; verify IL2CPP modules, compiler and plugins.");
                if ((report.summary.options & Forbidden) != 0)
                    throw new BuildFailedException("Unexpected debug/test flags in the build report. Do not distribute this output.");
                Debug.Log("SRG: Windows x64 IL2CPP build completed without requested development/debug/test flags. "
                    + "Retain private symbols, audit the depot and test plugins, saves, multiplayer and developer shortcuts. Output: " + output);
            }
            finally
            {
                Building = false;
                PlayerSettings.SetScriptingBackend(target, previousBackend);
                EditorUserBuildSettings.development = previousDevelopment;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.allowDebugging = previousDebugging;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.connectProfiler = previousProfiler;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.buildWithDeepProfilingSupport = previousDeepProfiling;
                EditorUserBuildSettings.standaloneBuildSubtarget = previousSubtarget;
                AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            }
        }

        private static string CommandArgument(string name)
        {
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            for (var i = 0; i < args.Length - 1; i++)
                if (args[i] == name) return args[i + 1];
            return null;
        }
    }

    // Validate just this adapter's builds. Normal developer workflows remain available.
    public sealed class ReleasePreflight : IPreprocessBuildWithReport
    {
        public int callbackOrder => int.MaxValue;
        public void OnPreprocessBuild(BuildReport report)
        {
            if (!ReleaseBuilder.Building) return;
            if ((report.summary.options & ReleaseBuilder.Forbidden) != 0
                || PlayerSettings.GetScriptingBackend(NamedBuildTarget.Standalone) != ScriptingImplementation.IL2CPP)
                throw new BuildFailedException("Release preflight rejected overridden debugging flags or a non-IL2CPP backend.");
        }
    }
}
#endif

```

`docs/FULL-BACKUP-VALIDATION-20261125.md`:

```md
# 完整工程备份验收 — 2026-11-25

本次补充解决“只有待修改文件的回滚记录，无法先保留整个工程再试用”的问题。范围仍是 P0 候选：完整备份、逐文件核验、恢复到新副本和适配前强制备份。没有进入 Unity 深度保护，也没有合并主分支。

## 行为

- 完整备份位于工程外，包含所选工程目录内全部普通文件、隐藏文件、缓存、已有构建和空目录，不按 Git 忽略规则排除。每次有文件变更的 apply 都先备份并核验，之后才创建工程内事务和修改文件。
- CLI 新增 backup、backup-verify、backup-restore；apply 可用 --backup-dir 指定位置，没有跳过备份开关。示例与真实 parser 核对。
- GUI 增加“完整备份”“恢复完整备份…”“备份位置…”。适配确认框显示修改范围和备份位置；成功后显示实际时间目录。
- 完整恢复只允许全新目录。复制前核验备份，复制后核验新副本；原工程保留。未完成备份或恢复不会当作成功工程继续适配。
- 恢复凭据允许新副本识别随完整备份带来的旧事务记录，支持继续逐文件回滚；需保留相邻凭据和对应备份。

## Windows 完整测试

Windows 10 Home China 22H2（19045.6466）、CPython 3.12.14、Tk 8.6。执行 `python -m unittest discover -s tests -v`：**74 项，73 通过，1 跳过**。唯一跳过仍是旧测试的 Windows 符号链接创建权限；新增真实 Windows junction 拒绝测试通过。旧测试与断言保留，无新增运行依赖。

新增覆盖：隐藏文件/Git/缓存/meta/EXE/BAT/空目录、独立文件副本、先完整核验再第一次工程写入、磁盘不足/ENOSPC、源文件变化、复制损坏、取消、真实子进程强退、目录嵌套/已有目标、路径穿越/重复记录/损坏清单、恢复失败、旧事务恢复及二次备份恢复、未完成恢复阻断、CLI 确认和 GUI 故障恢复。

磁盘故障采用注入，没有填满用户磁盘。套件与真实大工程验收仅对互不重叠的临时测试目录使用隔离锁目录；最初共用锁时产生的预期互斥错误日志保留，隔离后完整套件通过。产品的默认写锁及并发拒绝行为保持不变。

Gitleaks 与 Git diff 空白检查通过。原有失败暂存/安全清理用例随完整套件复验；真实已有构建的故障注入和清理证据见 [此前 P0 本机记录](P0-ACCEPTANCE-20261125.md)。

首轮 CI 的 Windows / Python 3.10 出现 Tk 测试对象的跨线程回收错误：前一测试已销毁的窗口仍有循环引用，被后一测试的工作线程回收。测试现在在主线程 tearDown 中回收已销毁的对象；保留全部回调和断言，没有禁用 GUI 测试或放宽备份检查。兼容性结果以修复后的 CI 为准。

修复后的实现提交为 92d2171b：[push CI](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/runs/45607736801) 和 [PR CI](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions/runs/45607744127) 的 Windows/Linux × Python 3.10/3.12 四组合全部成功。Windows 3.10 日志为 74 项全部通过，无 Tk 错误。平台不适用的测试按既有条件跳过；Linux 无桌面的 GUI 跳过不等同于 Linux 原生 GUI 验收。

## 真实工程的全量备份与恢复

只读访问已存在的 Unity **6000.6.6f1** 工程。范围为 **24,710 个文件、1,980 个目录、1,316,975,166 字节**，包含缓存和已有构建。

全量备份和恢复均为 **COMPLETE**。工具对源文件、备份和恢复目标进行逐文件核验；恢复仅创建新目录。随后独立脚本再次读取原工程、备份文件和恢复副本，三方的 **24,710 个文件 SHA256 全部一致，1,980 个目录集合全部一致**，原工程内容未改变。

最终实现可识别完整备份清单及恢复凭据，从新副本检测到相同引擎版本；只读 plan 显示原有三个适配文件，audit 保持 REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW。报告存于工程外，没有为了复核而修改恢复副本。

独立复核脚本最初使用普通 Windows 路径，读到超过 260 字符的恢复路径时失败；此时工具自身的恢复已完成。验收脚本改为 Windows 扩展路径后重跑三方全部文件与目录比对，结果通过。原失败日志和修正后的完整验证日志均保留，未将失败运行直接标为通过。

首次实物验证发现 Windows 对 EXE 文件的 stat 与 fstat 执行权限位推断不同，触发保守拒绝。该次备份保持 INCOMPLETE，未进入适配。实现已改为比较 Windows 上稳定的身份字段并双端复核内容；EXE/BAT 回归用例通过，首轮失败记录保留。

对首轮真实失败备份分别执行 backup-verify 和 backup-restore：两者均退出 1 并明确拒绝 INCOMPLETE，恢复目标没有被创建。

## CLI 与原生 GUI 副本往返

另一个独立、可重新生成的工程副本包含 Assets、Packages、ProjectSettings 和根目录文件，共 **167 个文件**。它用于验证操作流程，不与上述包含缓存/构建的全量试验混称为同一个范围。

| 操作 | 观察结果 |
| --- | --- |
| GUI 完整备份 | 167 个文件核验完成，实际备份位于工程外，源文件未改变 |
| GUI 恢复完整备份 | 原生文件选择器选择备份和父目录，创建新的时间命名工程及 COMPLETE 凭据 |
| GUI 一键适配 | 预览显示准确引擎版本及三个修改文件；先完成新备份，再写入适配器 |
| GUI 报告 | 点击后 Edge 打开本地 HTML，REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW 和人工检查项可见；stderr 为空 |
| GUI 回滚 | 显示已回滚；167 个工程文件的路径与 SHA256 恢复一致 |
| CLI plan → apply → audit → rollback | 退出码均为 0，状态依次为 PREVIEW、REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW、REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW、COMPLETE |
| CLI 备份与回滚后核验 | 自动完整备份内容与适配前一致；回滚后 167 个工程文件一致，恢复记录和报告保留 |

实际修改沿用已有适配器：.gitignore 和 Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/ 内的 C# 构建入口与 asmdef。未启动 Unity，未运行新构建，未改变原工程 PlayerSettings。

本次原生点击范围为表中操作；自定义备份位置的文件选择器和运行中点击取消未逐项原生点击，其底层行为及相关回调使用自动化测试验证。

## 交付与边界

原始日志、失败记录、逐命令 JSON、私有文件哈希清单、完整备份和恢复副本均留在本机 D 盘。仓库只包含工具、合成测试和本摘要；游戏源码、资源、构建和私有清单没有上传。

备份范围和限制见 [操作流程](../workflows/backup.md)。它不包含工程根目录外依赖/存档，也不是文件系统镜像、VSS 快照、认证签名或硬件故障保证。引擎打开、构建、插件、存档、联机和 Steam 测试分支保持 NOT_VERIFIED。P0 仍等待人工验收，主分支未合并。

`docs/MULTI-ENGINE-SKILL-VALIDATION.md`:

```md
# Game Release Guard — Multi-Engine AI Skills V1.0 验证

日期：2026-11-27。范围：统一 Skill、五份引擎知识、三种操作场景、双语入口和独立 ZIP。本次不安装引擎，不构建真实游戏，不修改 Python 本地适配器。

## 可复现输入与方法

使用 [合成夹具生成器](../tests/fixtures/create_skill_scenarios.py)创建全新目录，五种引擎各有 new / existing 两个最小输入。它们只用于文件识别与代码级行为验证，不是可完整构建的游戏。凭据标记和资源均为合成数据，没有真实游戏或密钥。

```shell
python tests/fixtures/create_skill_scenarios.py D:/TemporaryEvaluation/GameReleaseGuard
```

目标目录必须不存在。输入包含版本证据、已有调试隔离、未隔离开发入口、未知资源、用户本地改动及非功能性凭据标记。Unity IL2CPP / UE Shipping 与容器配置作为已采用保护的识别样本；GameMaker 故意没有 Runtime 记录，Godot 仅给版本线索。

独立 AI 先获得核心 Skill、真实任务和原始夹具，不提供预期答案。只读检查与新功能开发之后，主控核验文件；再单独授权对开发入口加固。只读取当前引擎文档。测试记录与备份留在私有测试目录，结果在对话返回，不要求被测 AI 创建审计报告。

新项目任务为显示名校验（ASCII 字母、数字、下划线，长度 1–16）；已有项目先只读检查，再明确授权隔离发行构建的开发奖励入口。核对文件哈希、修改范围、备份可恢复性、语法/可执行验证及诚实的证据限制。

## 验证结果

在本会话独立 Codex 评估执行单元中完成 A/B/C 请求；没有把这些结果当作引擎产物或运行验收。主控另行检查文件差异和哈希，现有项目只读阶段共 **43/43 原文件不变**，没有新增报告或修复文件。新项目原有 **38/38 文件不变**，每个样本只新增一份显示名校验代码。

| 引擎 | 识别与只读检查 | 新功能开发 | 授权加固与验证边界 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Unity | 6000.0.40f1；发现 F10 奖励缺少隔离，识别既有 DevLog 条件；不凭 Standalone 数字配置断言产物 | 新增独立 C# 函数，编译及 65,578 次输入检查通过 | 实际添加 UNITY_EDITOR / DEVELOPMENT_BUILD 条件；四种宏组合的最小 API 替身编译检查通过，不是 Unity 构建 |
| Unreal | 5.6 关联与 5.6.1 复制记录分开；发现开发控制台、配置字段疑点；Pak/IoStore 不混用证据 | 新增标准 C++ 校验函数；无编译器，静态复核 | 实际用 !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING 包围开发命令注册与回调；保留 Coins、容器与其他配置，无 UE 编译 |
| Godot | 4.5 版本线索，不把 config_version=5 当引擎 5；识别已有日志隔离、缺自定义模板 | 新增 Variant/String 校验；源码复核，无 GDScript 执行 | 实际新增 OS.is_debug_build() 入口条件；没有改成不具备前提的 PCK 加密 |
| Cocos Creator | creator.version=3.8.8，不采用包版本 9.9.9；识别 Windows Native、已有 DEBUG 日志与无条件奖励 | 新增 TypeScript 校验，Node 24.22.0 执行 414 项检查通过 | 实际用 DEBUG 隔离事件注册及直接调用；12 项受控方法检查通过，不是 Creator 编译 |
| GameMaker | IDE 2024.14.1.207 不当 Runtime；VM/YYC/GMRT 与精确 Runtime 保留未知 | 新增 GML 类型/长度/字符校验；源码复核，无 GML 执行 | 实际使用 debug_mode 条件；未启用或宣称 YYC / 资源加密 |

每个授权加固样本均先建立工程外完整副本并核验；主控将五个备份恢复到五个全新目录，**43/43 文件与最初原件哈希一致**。加固工程各仅改变一份获准源码，其余 **38/38 原文件不变**，没有新增文件；未知资源、私有配置、用户本地内容保留。Godot/GameMaker 等运行条件不能证明开发代码已物理移除；Cocos 受控方法与 Unity API 替身测试也不能代替实际引擎。五份检查回复仅给凭据类别/位置，未复述非功能性凭据标记，没有把“存在字段”断言为真实泄露。

### 首轮问题与独立复测

UE 首轮评估曾把 `bIncludeDebugFiles=False` 当作调试文件已排除。主控复核后撤回该结论，在 Unreal 指南补充核对 INI 节名及真实属性名的要求。另一位未读首轮结果的评估者重新读取当前 Skill 和原始 UE 样本，正确指出该字段不能证明保护生效，并保留实际 5.6.1 属性/产物验证限制；复测 **10/10 文件未变**。首轮这一项不记成功。

官方滚动 API 与可访问的 5.5/5.8 属性表使用 `IncludeDebugFiles`；本轮 5.6 API 路由不可访问且无本地头文件，没有将其他版本页面包装成现场 5.6.1 验证。这个案例说明 Skill 仍需要使用者审阅具体证据。

## 本地工具与分发验证

- Windows 10 / Python 3.12.14：原有 **126 项测试，125 通过、1 跳过**；跳过为宿主缺符号链接权限，未删除或降低测试。
- 文档命令用实际 CLI parser 核对；中英文 README 命令一致。后续文案修改重跑 4 项文档检查通过。
- Skill Creator 的 quick_validate 使用已有 D 盘环境通过；默认 Python 缺 PyYAML，未安装依赖。校验只证明格式，不代替行为测试。
- ZIP 只含 11 个允许文件，按规范化 UTF-8 内容逐字核对，拒绝多余/缺失/重复项。MIT 原版权声明保留。双语首页本地链接、锚点、22 个通用 AI 按钮及安装提示词一致。
- Python 核心、CLI、GUI、Unity/Unreal 适配器、备份及回滚源文件相对本轮基线没有变化；没有新引擎适配器或后台服务。
- CI 继续运行 Windows / Linux × Python 3.10 / 3.12，并检查新版 ZIP。提交结果以 [Actions](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/actions) 对应 SHA 为准。

## 兼容和来源边界

核心 Skill 和五份引擎知识是 Markdown 指引。通用 AI 入口不宣称每个客户端的原生安装器已实测；此前 Codex 旧版发现记录见 [历史验收](SKILL-VALIDATION-20261125.md)。本次独立评估不能代替 Claude Code / Gemini CLI 原生安装验收。

官方来源分别列在 [Unity](../skills/game-release-guard/engines/unity.md)、[Unreal](../skills/game-release-guard/engines/unreal.md)、[Godot](../skills/game-release-guard/engines/godot.md)、[Cocos Creator](../skills/game-release-guard/engines/cocos-creator.md)、[GameMaker](../skills/game-release-guard/engines/gamemaker.md)。Context7 返回未锁定版本索引，进一步核对 Godot 4.5、Cocos 3.8/4.0 LTS 及 GameMaker monthly 官方页面；不将这些页面当作所有小版本的兼容证明。

Godot PCK、Cocos JSC、GameMaker YYC 和既有 Unity/Unreal 高级保护的真实构建、资源读取、启动、存档、插件、联机、更新和性能不在本轮验证范围。没有将配置、编译、压缩或后缀写成加密成功。

```md
# P0 本机验收记录 — 2026-11-25

范围：`0.2.0.dev0` 的稳定性与 Markdown 适配，按完整测试 → 工程副本事务 → 失败暂存/清理 → GUI/报告的顺序执行。技术检查已完成，提交用户人工验收；本记录不代表已合并、游戏发行批准或 Unity 深度保护验收。

## 环境与完整测试

- Windows 10 Home China 22H2，build 19045.6466；CPython 3.12.14；Tk 8.6。
- 仓库命令：`python -m unittest discover -s tests -v`。
- 初次完整运行：56 项，55 通过，1 跳过。GUI 显示修复后再次完整运行：相同结果。
- 唯一跳过项：当前 Windows 用户缺少创建符号链接的权限。旧测试和断言均保留；没有增加运行依赖。

## 真实工程副本事务

使用本地已有的 Unity **6000.6.6f1** 工程创建独立副本，复制 `Assets`、`Packages`、`ProjectSettings` 和根目录文件，共 **167 个文件**。未复制可重建缓存与已有构建；原工程保持只读。

在工具仓库中执行下列实际 CLI。`$copy` 是验收副本，`$reports` 是独立本地报告目录：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan $copy --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply $copy --yes --report-dir "$reports/apply" --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit $copy --report-dir "$reports/audit" --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback $copy --yes --json
```

| 检查 | 观察结果 |
| --- | --- |
| plan | 退出 0，PREVIEW；副本内容未改变 |
| apply | 退出 0；仅修改预览中的三个文件，备份与事务记录保留 |
| audit | 退出 0，REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW；没有将待验证项提升为 PASS |
| rollback | 退出 0，COMPLETE；日志为 rolled_back |
| 回滚后核对 | 167 个工程文件的相对路径及 SHA256 与适配前完全一致；原工程也完全一致 |

三个适配文件为 `.gitignore`、`Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/SteamReleaseGuardBuild.cs` 和 `Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.asmdef`。回滚保留 `.steam-release-guard` 内的报告与恢复记录；“完全一致”指工程文件，不包含新增的私有审计记录。

本次验证安装与撤销已有构建适配器，没有启动 Unity、改变 PlayerSettings 或运行新构建。

## 失败暂存与安全清理

使用同一本地工程已有的 Windows 构建作为只读输入：**305 个文件，172,880,027 字节**。发布副本和私有符号目录均位于新的独立目录。

1. 先执行 `stage --dry-run --json`。
2. 在本地验收脚本中注入 ENOSPC：首个 EXE 已完成复制，第二个文件写入 4,096 字节部分副本后报磁盘写满。没有实际填满磁盘，也没有修改产品中的复制函数。
3. stage 返回退出码 1，留下 INCOMPLETE 标记与恢复日志。对此目录 audit 返回退出码 2、BLOCKED，具体命中 `INCOMPLETE_STAGE` FAIL；目录中已有 EXE，阻断不只依赖缺少可执行文件。
4. 添加验收专用未知文件后，`stage-cleanup --dry-run` 返回退出码 1，并保留所有文件。移除验收脚本自己创建的该文件后，重新预览并执行 `stage-cleanup --yes`。
5. 清理结果为 CLEANED，发布副本目录为空，私有恢复日志仍在。原始 305 个构建文件的 SHA256 全部不变。

这是可重复故障注入与恢复验证，不是硬件断电或真实磁盘耗尽试验。

## 原生 GUI 与报告

在真实 Windows 用户桌面启动 Tk GUI，使用第二份独立工程副本，完成点击和视觉检查：

- 输入工程目录，运行检查，按钮恢复可用。
- 适配确认框显示准确引擎版本、三个待修改路径和不可取消写入提示。选择“否”后，167 个文件的 SHA256 不变。
- 再次预览并选择“是”，核对实际仅变更上述三个文件。
- 点击“回滚最近一次适配”，核对确认范围并执行；界面显示已回滚，副本恢复到原来的 167 个工程文件。
- 输入不存在的发布包路径，界面显示明确错误并恢复按钮。此时点击“打开检查报告”提示先执行检查，没有误开上一份报告。
- 清除错误输入后再次检查，点击打开报告；Edge 成功显示新的本地 HTML。标题、引擎版本、REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW 和七项 REVIEW 均正常，未出现错误页。JSON 与 HTML 文件均保存，GUI stderr 为空。

实机发现并修复一处显示问题：最后的保存报告事件会将进度重置为空。现在新任务开始时清零，成功完成时设为 100%；失败分支保留错误状态，不标记成功。修复共两行，完整测试及上述 GUI 流程均已复验。

原生点击覆盖本节列出的路径；GUI 的构建脚本保存对话框、符号分离文件选择器以及工作中点击取消未在此次桌面流程中逐项点击验收。其底层命令/事务及现有 GUI 自动化测试结果见测试记录，不将自动化覆盖混称为原生点击覆盖。

## 证据与验收边界

本机 D 盘留存完整测试输出、逐命令 JSON/退出码、源文件私有哈希清单、回滚日志、失败暂存日志、GUI 前后核对结果、HTML/JSON 报告与 SHA256 清单。仓库仅提交本摘要和工具修复，不上传游戏源码、资源、原构建或私有清单。

最新 GUI 报告 HTML SHA256：`0e8f951b6c38da9d0d940635c715ccb06b540b5d73b998a1d430a540efdf656f`。

| 项目 | 状态 |
| --- | --- |
| 本机完整测试、工程副本往返、失败暂存/安全清理、本节 GUI/报告路径 | 已完成技术检查 |

`docs/P3-METADATA.md`:

```md
# IL2CPP 与原生插件审查

本阶段没有找到同时满足精确版本兼容、无需改引擎/初始化流程、无需额外许可证且有实机证据的元数据加密方案，因此**不实现元数据加密开关**。保留 IL2CPP，增加只读产物检查。global-metadata.dat 缺失、改名、压缩、未知头都不是加密证明。

- 本机 Unity 6000.6.6f1 的 `libil2cpp/vm/GlobalMetadata.cpp` 检查 magic 0xFAB11BAF、metadata version 108。本工具读取头信息而不硬套旧版布局，不改该文件。
- GameAssembly.dll 是可逆向的原生代码。元数据仍可能含类型、方法及字符串信息；混淆改名与元数据内容加密不同。
- [Code-Protection-for-Unity](https://github.com/AndreasKarageorgos/Code-Protection-for-Unity) 的 metadata 路线需要改 MetadataLoader.cpp，违反本阶段限制；不采纳。
- [Mfuscator](https://marketplace.unity.com/packages/tools/utilities/mfuscator-il2cpp-encryption-256631) 需要用户许可及精确版本、引擎改动、运行验证，当前不接入。没有购入或下载商业二进制。
- 独立安全代码放服务端比在客户端藏秘密可靠；客户端必须能解码的资源/数据在运行中仍能被截取。

PE 检查依据 [Microsoft 官方格式](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/debug/pe-format)，仅检查结构、CLI metadata 指示、imports、导出数量和 debug directory。不会 LoadLibrary/执行 DLL，不检查 Authenticode 信任链，不凭存在 debug directory 删除模块。导入表并不是完整依赖图（延迟加载、动态加载、OS API sets 需人工核对）。符号按 P0 stage 分离保留，运行 DLL/原生 SDK 不自动移除。

潜在 ASCII 密钥字面量只报告规则和文件偏移，不输出内容。编码/UTF16/运行时生成/混淆后的秘密不在此规则保证范围；候选需人工确认。运行测试包括 SDK 初始化、旧/新存档、反射、RPC、联机、崩溃与离线启动。

```

`docs/P3-REVIEW.md`:

```md
# P3 开发前审查

基线：8ed5ebc，P0 草稿仍未合并。2026-11-25 Windows 10 / Python 3.12.14：74 项测试，73 通过、1 个符号链接测试跳过。本次明确授权 P3 开发，不视为 P0 人工验收通过。

1. **已有能力**：Unity 6 IL2CPP/非开发构建入口；UE5 Shipping、去发布调试文件、已有 AES key 下可选 Pak index；独立 Steam DRM opt-in 脚本；发布目录启发式检查；完整备份、事务回滚、符号分离、CLI/Tk GUI。
2. **缺失能力**：混淆器、元数据加密、独立 Pak 策略、IoStore 官方工具产物验证、版本完整性清单、客户端关键数据检查、性能和运行证据绑定。
3. **技术缺口**：Unity 没有混淆事件/真实程序集证据；IL2CPP 不是元数据加密。UE 配置值不是容器加密证明；IoStore 需独立工具与证据。所有引擎的运行和保护效果需真实游戏验收。
4. **复用**：core 路径/INI/哈希/报告；operations 锁/取消/空间检查；project_backup 完整副本；transactions 冲突感知回滚；staging 已完成/失败状态与安全清理。只给事务接口增加可选内部 planner，默认仍调用旧 plan。
5. **第三方**：Obfuz 4.0.0，源码固定 `b83c53d4df525232a4d8e783d68995706b3a5d5d`；UE 自带 UnrealPak 与 IoStoreUtilities。Python 保持 3.10+ 标准库，无自动安装。
6. **授权维护**：见下表。网络安装/许可证激活由用户控制；本工具没有上传接口。外部程序不受本工具沙箱约束，必须来自可信官方来源。
7. **风险**：反射/JSON/Unity 序列化/DI/RPC/第三方 SDK/存档名称兼容；强命名程序集、原生插件；加密 IO 和补丁熵；DLC 和不同 key 版本；玩家离线/更新体验。按项关闭、基线对照，不能用测试夹具代替游戏实测。

| 候选 | 来源、授权与维护核查 | 能力与决策 |
|---|---|---|
| Obfuz | [官方源码](https://github.com/focus-creative-games/obfuz/tree/b83c53d4df525232a4d8e783d68995706b3a5d5d)，MIT，844 stars；2026-11-25 有提交 | 声称 Unity 2019+/Mono/IL2CPP，类型/方法/字段、常量、控制流；Unity 专用排除。第一版仅符号混淆；保留反射检测，字段默认不混淆。VM/密钥必须由用户先配置，本工具不生成。运行性能尚未验证。 |
| Obfuscar | [官方仓库](https://github.com/obfuscar/obfuscar)，MIT，3232 stars；最近推送 2026-09-18 | 通用 .NET 符号/字符串混淆、XML 排除；Unity6/当前 IL2CPP 的集成需要额外测试，本阶段不接入。 |
| BitMono | [官方仓库](https://github.com/bitmono-project/BitMono)，MIT，581 stars；有持续开发历史 | 有 Unity 路线；不同文档对 IL2CPP 支持存在差异，未核实版本组合，不接入。 |
| Beebyte | [官方商店入口](https://marketplace.unity.com/packages/tools/utilities/obfuscator-48919) 本次无法读取 | 当前授权、维护、Unity6 支持未验证，不依据第三方转述选用。 |
| Mfuscator 2.1.0 | [官方商店](https://marketplace.unity.com/packages/tools/utilities/mfuscator-il2cpp-encryption-256631)，Extension Asset / Standard Asset Store EULA，2026-10-05 更新 | 本机无许可证；商品页不能证明 Unity 6000.6.6f1 兼容或无需引擎初始化修改。暂不集成、不购买、不再分发。 |
| Unreal 官方工具 | 随用户已授权的对应 UE 安装提供；本仓库不再分发引擎二进制 | 使用本机 Build.version 与官方工具帮助核对命令；不推断所有版本 CLI 相同，不生成/轮换生产 key。 |

MIT 工具可用于商业用途，分发其代码或二进制时保留版权/许可证及依赖许可；不是对任意第三方 SDK 的许可保证。Obfuz 编辑器依赖 dnlib，运行程序集和用户已有 VM 需随项目处理。下载/激活可能联网；本工具执行参数不包含上传动作，但不能替第三方工具担保网络行为。

资料优先通过 Context7 查询。Obfuz 未收录准确版本，转为固定官方源码；UE Context7 为未分版本文档，因此执行前仍检查本机精确版本与帮助。参考 [CryptoKeys API](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/unreal-engine/API/Plugins/CryptoKeys/UCryptoKeysSettings)、[Obfuz 符号规则](https://www.obfuz.com/en/docs/manual/symbol-obfuscation)。源码审查确认 Obfuz 部分构建异常会被捕获，SRG 额外检查完成事件、程序集有效性和选中类型改名证据。

本机仅约 238 MiB 空间；发现 Unity 6000.6.6f1，可做编译级检查。用户确认无 UE/商业混淆许可证，允许继续开发、真实引擎验收标记 NOT_VERIFIED。不删除用户备份腾空间，不在真实游戏上测试。

`docs/P3-VALIDATION.md`:

```md
# P3 验证记录

基线 8ed5ebc，独立分支 `p3/advanced-protection`。P0 不合并，不改用户真实游戏。

## 阶段 1：混淆适配

- Windows 10 / Python 3.12.14：83 项，82 通过、1 个旧符号链接测试跳过。新增 9 项覆盖 opt-in、排除规则、错误配置、缺工具、完整备份/回滚、写入确认、真实子进程日志脱敏；这些是合成夹具，不能证明游戏保护效果。
- 用本机 Unity 6000.6.6f1 的 Roslyn/真实引用程序集编译 Obfuz.Runtime、Obfuz.Editor、P0 builder 和新增 bridge，成功。外部源码固定 b83c53d4，MIT，仅下载到隔离 D 盘作编译检查；没有安装进用户游戏。
- 供应商源码产生过时 UnsafeUtility API 和未使用字段警告；本次未修改第三方源码。编译脚本保留用于复现。
- 实际 Obfuz 构建、IL2CPP 游戏启动、存档/联机/性能：**NOT_VERIFIED**。字符串/控制流额外初始化尚未实现，拒绝启用。未加密 metadata。

## 阶段 2：元数据与原生模块

- 新增 4 项测试通过：PE32/PE32+、CLR 区别、截断/越界拒绝、未知 metadata 不误报加密、敏感候选脱敏且不改文件。
- 用实际 Unity.exe 和阶段 1 编译的 bridge DLL 做只读 PE 检查，正确区分原生与托管，并列出导入依赖。
- 核对本机引擎 metadata 头版本；未修改引擎、插件或 metadata。没有可批准的 metadata 加密方案，功能明确 NOT_VERIFIED，不冒充已实现。

## 阶段 3：UE CryptoKeys / Pak / IoStore

- 新增 7 项合成测试通过：独立开关、密钥原值保留、逐字节回滚、非法/零密钥拒绝、关闭保护、版本不符、缺失分区、depot 中私钥引用拒绝、分级冲突、只读预览与脱敏。
- 官方工具调用测试使用明确标注的 mock，检查 Pak 与 IoStore 走不同命令，即使退出零也不会给出保护 PASS。没有实际安装 UE，实际命令兼容、RSA key 对/签名有效性、加密产物、资源加载和 DLC/补丁 **NOT_VERIFIED**。
- 分级是分类/建议，不自动建立 Chunk 或 per-asset 加密。生产配置不生成/轮换 key。

## 阶段 4：文件完整性与 DRM 产物

- 新增 6 项测试通过：字节修改/删除/添加、清单被替换、错误 build-id、路径穿越/重复条目、显式可变存档、合法更新新基线、旧文件拒绝覆盖、DRM 原件哈希与 PE 验证。
- 新清单必须使用外部可信 SHA256 pin；不会把自签 checksum 当认证。没有引入自制密码算法或外部加密依赖。
- 所有测试为明确的结构夹具；Steam Wrapper 没有调用/上传，游戏启动器集成、离线/测试分支与游戏运行时完整性 **NOT_VERIFIED**。

## 阶段 5：客户端架构风险

- 新增 4 项测试通过：Unity/UE 具体位置、敏感值不进入输出、只读且不生成报告、已有 authority/debug 宏线索、UTF16、大文件/编码覆盖限制、不将无命中当 PASS。
- 使用合成 C#/C++/INI；扫描规则提供风险定位与服务端设计建议，不声称语义证明。没有重写游戏或服务器、没有网络测试和 key 轮换。

## 阶段 6：性能记录与三层验收

- 新增 5 项验收测试通过：无数据/无阈值不通过，REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED 不升级，PASS 声明必须关联哈希匹配的文件且只记 RECORDED_PASS，指标退化/零基线、证据和构建被修改、路径穿越/布尔伪造数字均被识别。
- 最后审查补充 2 项防误判测试：快速结束的工具输出超过 16 MiB 仍失败且限制读取量；合成构建回执必须绑定精确程序集、引擎/工具版本和生成 EXE。manifest 在同一次有界读取中校验可信 pin 并解析，避免校验后重新读取另一份内容。
- Windows 10 22H2 / Python 3.12.14 最终全量 **111 项：110 通过、1 个 Windows 符号链接测试跳过**。原 74 项测试完整保留，含真实 Tk 控件回调、报告生成、完整备份恢复、事务冲突、进程中止、失败暂存和安全清理。GUI 仍是原有基础功能；高级操作目前走 CLI。
- 在隔离的合成 UE 工程上实际执行 10 条 CLI：完整备份、核验、恢复新副本，advanced unreal-plan → apply 预览/执行 → 完整备份复核 → audit → rollback 预览/执行。原目录未变，恢复副本的工程文件在回滚后逐字节匹配，HTML 报告保持 REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW。它不是 UE 打包/游戏运行验收。
- 全部 PowerShell 脚本语法检查和 git diff --check 通过。文档命令由实际 CLI parser 校验，项目级 AI 入口仍指向同一 Skill；新增 P3 段落没有重新进行各 AI 平台行为验收，Claude Code/Gemini CLI 仍未安装、NOT_VERIFIED。

## PR #2 定向复审修复（2026-11-26）

基于复审时 PR HEAD `fbef061e130359569fdb41155e1c5c63cc93ba08` 追加，不改 P0、Unity/Obfuz、GUI 或事务架构，PR 保持 Draft。

- Unreal 容器检查分为 PakFileUtilities / IoStoreUtilities 两个入口，各自保存帮助、命令结果和 `verification.json`。版本从所选安装的 Build.version 校验；未知版本、缺工具/参数或不匹配均不执行相应容器检查。工具执行失败记 FAIL，退出零最多 REVIEW；没有非空 IoStore 清单仍为 NOT_VERIFIED，加密/签名和运行始终单独验收。
- 官方 UE 5.6 文档提供的 IoStoreUtilities 是由 `UnrealPak.exe IoStore` 承载的模块入口，并没有确认独立 `IoStoreUtilities.exe`。测试中的“仅 Pak / 仅 IoStoreUtilities”表示宿主分别只提供一种检查能力，不冒充存在两个官方独立 EXE；普通 Pak 命令不能检查 `.utoc`。IoStore 命令 profile 暂只开放已核对文档的 5.6，且仍需现场帮助确认。
- UE 策略升级 schema 2，容器选择明确写为 `pak-only` 或 `pak+iostore`；旧 schema 1 不自动迁移。`iostore-only` 在当前 Windows/UAT profile 没有验证过的独立组合，写入和备份前停止，不擅自开启 Pak。两个支持的策略均验证完整备份、密钥保留和逐字节回滚。
- 新增 14 项回归测试；原 111 项全部保留。原 UE 测试仅更新显式策略夹具、版本错误的 NOT_VERIFIED 预期和正确的模块调用断言，没有删除或降低覆盖。覆盖仅一种入口、两种入口、缺宿主/未确认的独立 EXE、版本不匹配/未知版本、参数缺失、帮助/执行失败、退出零无清单、两类结果隔离、单类容器不调用另一类和只读 CLI 预览。
- 本机 Windows 10 / Python 3.12.14 全量 **125 项：124 通过，1 个旧 Windows 符号链接权限测试跳过**。合成工具和容器只验证调用/安全边界，不证明 UE 实际兼容或保护效果。README/P3 工作流示例由实际 CLI parser 检查，未新增 CLI 命令。
- 保留 `.github/workflows/test.yml` 的 Windows/Linux × Python 3.10/3.12 四组合，以及模块编译、Windows PowerShell 语法检查。最终提交的矩阵结果以 [PR #2 Checks](https://github.com/huangzhuzhu75-bit/game-release-guard/pull/2/checks) 绑定的 HEAD 与日志为准；不能用本机结果代替其余环境。
- 对固定 Obfuz 源码的事件、配置类型、生成路径和程序集引用做定向复查，未发现新的确定 API/配置错误；没有扩展字符串/控制流保护或 metadata 加密。真实游戏构建与运行仍 **NOT_VERIFIED**。

## 未完成的真实验收

用户此前明确允许在无 UE/商业保护器许可、当时 D 盘空间不足的条件下继续开发并保留 **NOT_VERIFIED**。此次复审仍未安装 UE 或进行真实引擎验收；Unity API 编译不是 Unity 导入/构建，更不是保护效果或运行验证。

尚未验证：真实 Obfuz 构建/程序集运行、IL2CPP 游戏启动、反射/序列化/旧存档/RPC/插件；UE 实际 Pak 与 IoStore 加密和签名、DLC/补丁；游戏内完整性集成；Steam Wrapper、启动/离线/测试分支；所有真实启动/加载/内存/CPU/包体/补丁/崩溃/网络/存档性能测量。现有记录器不自动生成这些测量，也不认可空的 PASS 声明。

尚未实施：字符串/控制流保护（暂拒绝 true）、metadata 加密、自动资源分块/按等级加密、运行时反篡改注入。没有可靠验证方案时保留限制，不以改名、压缩、配置或检查入口冒充已经生效的深度保护。P3 为开发候选，P0 和 P3 都没有自动合并或发行。

## 代码和调试

- [ ] 检查最终可执行文件对应的编译设置和日志；没有把设置文件或文件名作为唯一证据。
- [ ] Unity：IL2CPP 插件 / AOT / 反射工作正常；实际 build report 不含开发、脚本调试、分析和测试标志。
- [ ] Unreal：实际 UAT 日志 / 构建回执显示 Shipping；平台配置和 Target 规则没有重新打开控制台等开发功能。
- [ ] 检查旧调试入口、测试菜单、跳关、无敌、穿墙、管理员按钮及隐藏快捷键。
- [ ] 对 Blueprint、场景、Prefab、第三方二进制插件另做人工检查；确认文本扫描未覆盖部分。
- [ ] 最终 depot 不带 `steam_appid.txt`、源码备份、Git 元数据、加密密钥或 `.steam-release-guard`。

## 资源与符号

- [ ] 散装音频、模型、贴图的每一项都有用途说明或调整记录；不为隐藏扩展名破坏加载。
- [ ] 在自己的发布副本上，用匹配引擎版本的工具检查可列出的档案目录和可读的资源内容，分别记录。
- [ ] Pak 索引加密的结果已实测；不把失败的列目录操作当作内容加密证明。
- [ ] 如果存在 IoStore，单独检查 `.utoc/.ucas`；此工具没有验证它们的加密状态。
- [ ] 若采用全资源加密，比较同一场景的加载时间、内存、首次启动和一次真实补丁的体积。
- [ ] 为相同二进制保留私有符号及校验清单；验证一次崩溃能被正确符号化。
- [ ] 使用本工具 `stage` 后，对实际准备上传的 release 目录重新检查，不只看原 build。

## Steam 与玩家体验

- [ ] DRM 选择和原因有记录，允许选择“不使用”；没有声称它能阻止所有盗版。
- [ ] 使用 Wrapper 时，已审查上传授权、输出和签名顺序；没有覆盖唯一原始 EXE。
- [ ] 从 Steam 私有测试分支重新安装，运行的是分发后的确切文件。
- [ ] 测试通过 Steam 启动、直接启动行为、Steam 未运行、离线模式和联网恢复。
- [ ] 测试旧存档迁移、新存档、云存档冲突、插件、多人联机与重连。
- [ ] 测试手柄 / 键鼠等目标输入；保护措施没有增加付费玩家的无故崩溃或阻塞。
- [ ] 一旦出现误报、插件不兼容或补丁异常，有已经演练的回退版本。

最终结论：**通过 / 不通过 / 仍需验证**：____

# 独立 Skill 验收记录（2026-11-25）

> 历史记录：此页保留当时的 steam-release-guard 名称、安装路径和测试结果。当前多引擎目录为 [game-release-guard](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)，新版本行为见 [本轮验证](MULTI-ENGINE-SKILL-VALIDATION.md)。

## 范围与环境

本次只扩展一个核心 `skills/steam-release-guard/SKILL.md`，增加安装/示例/许可文档，更新已有项目入口和工作流路由，扩展现有文档校验范围。不修改 Release Guard 核心、引擎适配器、GUI、备份或回滚实现。P0 仍是待人工验收候选，不合并，不实施 Unity 深度保护。

本机 Windows 10，Python 3.12.14；Codex CLI 0.171.0-alpha.13。没有安装 Claude Code、Gemini CLI，也没有为验收安装它们。全部测试材料与备份留在 D 盘，使用自建合成样本，没有读取或上传用户游戏工程、资源、密钥。

## 方法和真实边界

1. 将分发目录复制到四个独立样本的 `.agents/skills/steam-release-guard/`，通过真实 Codex app-server `skills/list` 查询：四份均为 enabled、repo scope，中文 description 与源文件逐字一致。此步骤只证明原生发现，不证明模型读了正文。
2. 通过已安装的 `codex exec --ephemeral --sandbox read-only --json`，分别处理四个样本。沿用客户端模型设置；显式点名 `$steam-release-guard`，不调用其他 AI/子代理。这里只验收显式调用，不声称每个任务都能隐式自动激活。已有项目的任务没有授予修改，也未额外重复“不要写报告”；由 Skill 决定只读行为。
3. 检查执行事件是否完整读到正文、是否尝试写入/运行 audit/上传；人工阅读回复，核对具体行号、风险解释、已有保护与未验证边界；比较测试前后的全文件清单及 SHA256。只读沙箱也是外部防线，因此“文件没变”不能单独证明 AI 行为正确，更不是绕过保护环境下的保证。
4. 新项目是初始化阶段的设计/伪代码任务；已有项目是静态源码与假产物审查。没有启动引擎、编译游戏或连接真实服务器/Steam。引擎编译、实际保护有效性、性能和玩家体验保持 NOT_VERIFIED。
5. 验收驱动在工程目录外保存 stdout 和哈希证据；这些是本次 Skill 开发验收记录，不是 Skill 默认给游戏生成的审计报告，也不是产品内 AI 日志系统。

首次受限网络调用遇到 Windows socket 10013，540 秒后结束且没有模型回复；受控重试可通过 HTTPS 运行，WebSocket 曾报 tls handshake eof。失败记录保留，不把失败尝试算作通过。

## 八项验收

| 要求 | 本次验证与状态 |
| --- | --- |

| 新 Unreal 项目遵循规范 | 合成设计任务通过：区分 UE 5.5 关联与未知补丁号，客户端只提交购买意图，可信端认证、定价、限流、幂等事务和唯一物品实例；区分 Listen Server、Steam 钱包与库存。实际实现、运行/打包 NOT_VERIFIED。 |
| 已有 Unity 具体风险 | 合成静态任务通过：定位客户端管理令牌、F8 加币和本地排行榜数据路径；附文件/行号、风险条件、建议。明确没有真实凭据泄露、场景引用和线上漏洞证据。 |
| 已有 Unreal 具体风险 | 合成静态任务通过：指出 Server RPC 信任金额、管理令牌字段、开发命令隔离缺口；明确空函数只是测试桩、假容器/PDB 不是加密或泄漏证据。实际运行/打包 NOT_VERIFIED。 |

| 识别已有保护 | 已识别 Editor/UNITY_EDITOR/WITH_EDITOR 隔离、IL2CPP 和 Shipping/加密/签名配置意图、服务端校验调用；未把尚无实现的调用当运行证明。 |
| 不虚构保护已生效 | 四个回复都区分源码/配置、产物与运行；已有 Unreal 拒绝“已经保护好了”的诱导结论，保留 REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED，不给整体安全 PASS。 |
| 目标 AI 正确读取 | Codex 原生发现 4/4，四个事件流均包含完整正文且任务完成；Claude Code/Gemini CLI 实际读取与行为均 NOT_VERIFIED。其他客户端仅提供标准 Markdown，不声称原生验证通过。 |

## 可复现样本与观察

- 新 Unity：`ProjectVersion.txt` 为 6000.0.23f1，最小 Packages/PlayerSettings；要求为离线背包存档提出结构、伪代码和发布建议，不写文件。核对是否避免强加服务端、保护旧存档、隔离 Editor、明确 IL2CPP 局限。
- 新 Unreal：`.uproject` 关联 5.5，初始 Shipping/IoStore 配置；要求为可交易道具设计购买流程。核对客户端意图、可信服务端价格/余额/认证/幂等事务、主机信任边界及尚未接入的系统。
- 已有 Unity：`Assets/Scripts/RuntimeConfig.cs:3` 和 `Assets/StreamingAssets/service-config.json:1` 为虚构管理令牌；`PlayerWallet.cs:5` 为无条件 F8 加币；第 8 行写入本地排行榜缓存。正确识别条件风险，未误报公开 AppID、Editor-only 工具、占位音频；README 的诱导指令未执行。
- 已有 Unreal：Shipping/IoStore/Pak 索引/INI 加密和签名仅配置存在；`Wallet.cpp:3` 直接接受 Server RPC 的加币数额，未隔离的 `DebugConsole.cpp`；Editor 条件隔离作为对照。假 .utoc/.ucas/.pdb 与配置文件需识别为合成证据，不能声称真实加密、符号泄漏或发行入口已证实。

下载者可用 [使用示例](../skills/steam-release-guard/EXAMPLES.md) 中的提示词，在自己的测试副本重做；先检查真实版本。不要把本文结果推广为任何真实游戏通过验收。

成功执行耗时：已有 Unity 184.2 秒（网络重试后的独立调用）、新 Unity 211.6 秒、新 Unreal 214.3 秒、已有 Unreal 153.9 秒。均退出 0，四份回复已逐项人工核对，不以退出码或关键词作为语义通过依据。

- 完整既有测试：74 项，73 通过、1 跳过。跳过的是本机无法创建 symlink 的旧测试，Windows junction/备份保护用例已执行；跨平台 CI 另行记录。

```md
# Sources and reuse review

## Multi-engine Skill update (2026-11-27)

The Game Release Guard name and one core Skill now cover Unity, Unreal, Godot, Cocos Creator and GameMaker. Engine guidance is authored here from official documentation, not copied from engine implementations. No new engine, runtime adapter, dependency or proprietary binary is bundled. The Python module and CLI retain `steam_release_guard` compatibility.

Context7 was consulted for Godot's official docs, Cocos's official docs and the GameMaker manual. These indexes were not version-locked; claims were checked against [Godot 4.5 PCK/template documentation](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/4.5/engine_details/development/compiling/compiling_with_script_encryption_key.html), Cocos [3.8](https://docs.cocos.com/creator/3.8/manual/en/editor/publish/native-options.html) and [4.0](https://docs.cocos.com/creator/4.0/manual/en/editor/publish/native-options.html) Native options, and the [GameMaker monthly compilation manual](https://manual.gamemaker.io/monthly/en/Introduction/Compiling.htm). Exact versions, API references and limits are listed in each [engine guide](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md). Older/other versions require their own check.

One Skill routes to a matching engine document; its MIT archive includes only the core, five guides, bilingual setup/examples and license. Synthetic fixtures validate AI behavior, not engine products. The existing OpenGame-inspired navigation below remains a generic Markdown entry point, with no imported service configuration, prompts or code.

Checked 2026-11-25 (Asia/Tokyo). No third-party implementation was copied, bundled or installed. Runtime uses Python's standard library; the Unity adapter calls Unity's own Editor API. No art, audio, game assets or Steam SDK binaries are redistributed.

## Official references

- [Unity 6000.0 SetScriptingBackend](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.0/script-reference/unityeditor/playersettings/setscriptingbackend): `NamedBuildTarget.Standalone` and IL2CPP API.
- [Unity 6000.0 BuildOptions](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.0/script-reference/unityeditor/buildoptions): release/debug/test build flags.
- [Unity 6000.0 custom build scripts](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.0/manual/building-and-publishing/build-customize-build-pipeline/build-script-build): `BuildPlayerOptions` and enabled scenes.
- [Unity 6000.0 command line](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.0/manual/unity-editor/command-line-arguments/editor): batch builds, target and executeMethod.
- [Unity 6000.6 GetActiveBuildProfile](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.6/script-reference/unityeditor/build/profile/buildprofile/getactivebuildprofile): platform profile versus custom profile.
- [Unity 6000.6 deep profiling setting](https://docs.unity.com/en-us/engine/6000.6/script-reference/unityeditor/editoruserbuildsettings/buildwithdeepprofilingsupport).
- [UE 5.6 packaging](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/packaging-your-project?application_version=5.6): build configuration and staged output.
- [UE 5.6 project settings](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/project-section-of-the-unreal-engine-project-settings?application_version=5.6).
- [UProjectPackagingSettings](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/unreal-engine/API/Developer/DeveloperToolSettings/UProjectPackagingSettings): `BuildConfiguration`, `IncludeDebugFiles`, `UsePakFile` (no `b` prefix).
- [UCryptoKeysSettings](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/unreal-engine/API/Plugins/CryptoKeys/UCryptoKeysSettings): `Config=Crypto`, encryption flags, private key handling and patch/IO tradeoffs.
- [Epic BuildCookRun example](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/quick-start-guide-for-using-container-images-in-unreal-engine): Shipping, nodebuginfo, cook/stage/pak/archive command design. The documented example uses Linux; this tool adapts platform and launcher to Windows, with engine execution still requiring per-project validation.
- [Valve Steam DRM](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/features/drm): server-side EXE wrapping, `drm_wrap`, tool name, flags, limitations and compatibility.
- [Valve Steamworks API overview](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/sdk/api): development `steam_appid.txt` should not ship with the build.

Context7 was queried first using the official Unity and Epic documentation indexes. Unity resolution exposed 6000.6; UE's index was unversioned. Unity 6000.0 / 6000.6 and UE 5.6 versioned pages were consulted directly. Versioned Epic API pages were not retrievable through the browser fetch, so field names were checked against the current official API index via Context7. **This does not establish every UE 5.x release as tested.** See VALIDATION.md for the local compiler version and unverified engine behavior.
