`steam_release_guard/advanced/acceptance.py`:

```py
"""Compare measured data and bind reviewer evidence to two immutable builds.

Reports record evidence, never substitute a JSON checkbox for a real game test.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
import statistics

from .. import core as c
from ..operations import GuardError
from ..project_backup import safe_path, relative_name
from . import integrity
from .common import shape

METRICS = {"startup_ms": "ms", "scene_load_ms": "ms", "memory_mib": "MiB", "cpu_percent": "%",
           "file_bytes": "bytes", "patch_bytes": "bytes", "crash_count": "count", "network_rtt_ms": "ms",
           "save_read_ms": "ms", "save_write_ms": "ms"}
RUNTIME = ("startup", "resource_loading", "old_save", "new_save", "multiplayer", "plugins", "offline",
           "patch_update", "dlc", "steam_test_branch", "debug_entrypoints")
STATUSES = {"PASS", "FAIL", "REVIEW", "NOT_VERIFIED", "NOT_APPLICABLE"}


def template():
    pending = lambda: {"status": "NOT_VERIFIED", "reason": "", "evidence": []}
    return {"schema": 1, "environment": {"engine_version": "", "hardware": "", "os": "", "scenario": "", "measurement_tool": ""},
            "baseline": {"build_id": "baseline-001", "manifest_sha256": ""},
            "candidate": {"build_id": "protected-001", "manifest_sha256": ""},
            "metrics": {k: {"baseline_samples": [], "candidate_samples": [], "max_regression_pct": None,
                             "evidence": []} for k in METRICS},
            "runtime_checks": {k: pending() for k in RUNTIME},
            "protection_checks": [{"name": "replace-with-enabled-protection", "configuration": pending(),
                                   "artifact": pending(), "runtime": pending()}]}


def evidence_status(items, evidence_root):
    if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > 100:
        raise GuardError("Evidence must be a bounded list of local path/SHA256 references.")
    if not items:
        return "NOT_VERIFIED"
    status = "VERIFIED_FILES"
    for item in items:
        shape(item, {"path", "sha256"})
        name = relative_name(item["path"])
        if not isinstance(item["sha256"], str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", item["sha256"]):
            raise GuardError("Evidence requires a valid SHA256, never a secret value.")
        path = c.local_path(evidence_root, name)
        if not path.is_file():
            if status != "FAIL": status = "NOT_VERIFIED"
        elif c.file_digest(path) != item["sha256"]:
            status = "FAIL"
    return status


def check_record(value, root):
    shape(value, {"status", "reason", "evidence"})
    if value["status"] not in STATUSES or not isinstance(value["reason"], str):
        raise GuardError("Invalid acceptance status/reason.")
    proof = evidence_status(value["evidence"], root)
    if proof == "FAIL" or value["status"] == "FAIL":
        status = "FAIL"
    elif value["status"] == "PASS":
        status = "RECORDED_PASS" if proof == "VERIFIED_FILES" else "NOT_VERIFIED"
    elif value["status"] == "NOT_APPLICABLE":
        status = "REVIEW"  # Applicability requires human review, never silently disappears.
    else:
        status = value["status"]
    return {"status": status, "declared_status": value["status"], "evidence_files": proof,
            "scope": "Reviewer declaration; file hashes checked, content/real-world test not independently attested."}


def compare_metric(name, data, root, baseline_files, candidate_files):
    shape(data, {"baseline_samples", "candidate_samples", "max_regression_pct", "evidence"})
    samples = [data["baseline_samples"], data["candidate_samples"]]
    for group in samples:
        if not isinstance(group, list) or len(group) > 10000 or any(type(v) not in {int, float} or not math.isfinite(v) or v < 0 for v in group):
            raise GuardError("Metrics require finite, nonnegative numeric samples (not booleans).")
    limit = data["max_regression_pct"]
    if limit is not None and (type(limit) not in {int, float} or not math.isfinite(limit) or limit < 0):
        raise GuardError("Set an explicit nonnegative regression threshold or leave it unverified.")
    proof = evidence_status(data["evidence"], root)
    if proof == "FAIL":
        return {"status": "FAIL", "reason": "Measurement evidence hash mismatch"}
    if name == "file_bytes":
        samples = [[sum(f["bytes"] for f in baseline_files)], [sum(f["bytes"] for f in candidate_files)]]
        proof = "VERIFIED_FILES"
    minimum = 1 if name in {"file_bytes", "patch_bytes", "crash_count"} else 3
    if limit is None or any(len(group) < minimum for group in samples) or proof != "VERIFIED_FILES":
        return {"status": "NOT_VERIFIED", "reason": "Need threshold, enough comparable samples and hashed evidence", "unit": METRICS[name]}
    before, after = map(statistics.median, samples)
    regression = 100 * (after - before) / before if before else (0 if not after else None)
    passed = regression is not None and regression <= limit
    if name == "crash_count" and any(samples[1]):
        passed = False
    return {"status": "WITHIN_LIMIT" if passed else "FAIL", "unit": METRICS[name],
            "baseline_median": before, "candidate_median": after, "regression_pct": regression,
            "limit_pct": limit, "scope": "Arithmetic on supplied measurements; only file_bytes measured automatically."}


def check(record, baseline_build, candidate_build, baseline_manifest, candidate_manifest, evidence_root):
    shape(record, {"schema", "environment", "baseline", "candidate", "metrics", "runtime_checks", "protection_checks"})
    if record["schema"] != 1:
        raise GuardError("Unsupported acceptance schema.")
    shape(record["environment"], {"engine_version", "hardware", "os", "scenario", "measurement_tool"})
    if any(not isinstance(v, str) for v in record["environment"].values()):
        raise GuardError("Environment fields must be descriptions without secrets.")
    for side in ("baseline", "candidate"):
        shape(record[side], {"build_id", "manifest_sha256"})
    if record["baseline"]["build_id"] == record["candidate"]["build_id"]:
        raise GuardError("Baseline and protected candidate need distinct build IDs.")
    root = safe_path(evidence_root)
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Local private evidence root is missing.")
    builds, manifests = {}, {}
    for side, build, manifest in (("baseline", baseline_build, baseline_manifest), ("candidate", candidate_build, candidate_manifest)):
        identity = record[side]
        manifests[side] = integrity.load_pinned(manifest, identity["manifest_sha256"], identity["build_id"])
        builds[side] = integrity.verify(build, manifest, identity["build_id"], identity["manifest_sha256"])
    if any(b["status"] != "INTEGRITY_MATCH" for b in builds.values()):
        return {"release_status": "BLOCKED", "reason": "Build bytes no longer match pinned acceptance subjects.", "builds": builds}
    shape(record["metrics"], METRICS)
    metrics = {name: compare_metric(name, data, root, manifests["baseline"]["files"], manifests["candidate"]["files"])
               for name, data in record["metrics"].items()}
    shape(record["runtime_checks"], RUNTIME)
    runtime = {name: check_record(value, root) for name, value in record["runtime_checks"].items()}
    protections = record["protection_checks"]
    if not isinstance(protections, list) or not 1 <= len(protections) <= 100:
        raise GuardError("Record each enabled protection with separate configuration, artifact and runtime evidence.")
    checked, seen = [], set()
    for entry in protections:
        shape(entry, {"name", "configuration", "artifact", "runtime"})
        if not isinstance(entry["name"], str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,100}", entry["name"]) or entry["name"] in seen:
            raise GuardError("Each protection requires a unique bounded name.")
        seen.add(entry["name"])
        checked.append({"name": entry["name"], **{tier: check_record(entry[tier], root) for tier in ("configuration", "artifact", "runtime")}})
    failed = any(v["status"] == "FAIL" for v in [*metrics.values(), *runtime.values(),
                                                *(p[t] for p in checked for t in ("configuration", "artifact", "runtime"))])
    return {"release_status": "BLOCKED" if failed else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW", "builds": builds,
            "environment": "RECORDED" if all(record["environment"].values()) else "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "metrics": metrics, "runtime_checks": runtime, "protection_checks": checked,
            "scope": "No game executed. PASS declarations are only RECORDED_PASS with matching evidence files. REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED never become PASS. Human inspection of real engine/runtime/protection evidence remains required."}

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/binary.py`:

```py
"""Bounded PE/CLI structure inspection. Never load or execute inspected modules."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import struct

from .. import core as c
from ..operations import GuardError
from ..project_backup import safe_path, disjoint
from .common import finding, result


def pe_info(path):
    path = safe_path(path)
    size = path.stat().st_size
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        def at(offset, length):
            if offset < 0 or length < 0 or length > 65536 or offset + length > size:
                raise GuardError("Malformed/truncated PE bounds.")
            handle.seek(offset)
            value = handle.read(length)
            if len(value) != length:
                raise GuardError("Binary changed during inspection.")
            return value

        def u16(offset):
            return struct.unpack("<H", at(offset, 2))[0]

        def u32(offset):
            return struct.unpack("<I", at(offset, 4))[0]

        if at(0, 2) != b"MZ":
            raise GuardError("Not a PE executable.")
        pe = u32(0x3c)
        if at(pe, 4) != b"PE\0\0":
            raise GuardError("Invalid PE signature.")
        machine, sections = u16(pe + 4), u16(pe + 6)
        optional_size, optional = u16(pe + 20), pe + 24
        if not 1 <= sections <= 96 or optional_size < 96:
            raise GuardError("Invalid PE section/optional header count.")
        magic = u16(optional)
        if magic not in (0x10b, 0x20b):
            raise GuardError("Unsupported PE optional header.")
        data_offset = 96 if magic == 0x10b else 112
        if optional_size < data_offset:
            raise GuardError("Truncated optional header.")
        count = u32(optional + data_offset - 4)
        if count > (optional_size - data_offset) // 8:
            raise GuardError("Invalid PE data directory count.")
        directories = [struct.unpack("<II", at(optional + data_offset + n * 8, 8)) for n in range(min(count, 16))]
        table = []
        for n in range(sections):
            pos = optional + optional_size + n * 40
            header = at(pos, 40)
            virtual_size, rva, raw_size, raw_pos = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", header, 8)
            if raw_size and raw_pos + raw_size > size:
                raise GuardError("PE section extends beyond file.")
            table.append((rva, raw_pos, raw_size))

        def locate(rva, length):
            for start, raw, length_raw in table:
                if start <= rva and rva + length <= start + length_raw:
                    return raw + rva - start
            raise GuardError("PE directory has no backed section.")

        def directory(index):
            return directories[index] if index < len(directories) else (0, 0)

        def string(rva):
            value = bytearray()
            for n in range(256):
                byte = at(locate(rva + n, 1), 1)
                if byte == b"\0":
                    try:
                        return value.decode("ascii")
                    except UnicodeError:
                        raise GuardError("Non-ASCII PE import name.") from None
                value += byte
            raise GuardError("Unterminated PE import name.")

        cli, cli_size = directory(14)
        managed = bool(cli)
        if managed:
            if cli_size < 0x48:
                raise GuardError("Truncated CLR header.")
            cli_pos = locate(cli, 0x48)
            metadata, metadata_size = struct.unpack("<II", at(cli_pos + 8, 8))
            if metadata_size < 16 or at(locate(metadata, 16), 4) != b"BSJB":
                raise GuardError("Invalid CLR metadata signature.")
        imports, imports_size = directory(1)
        dependencies = []
        if imports:
            for n in range(min(imports_size // 20, 4096)):
                descriptor = at(locate(imports + n * 20, 20), 20)
                if descriptor == bytes(20):
                    break
                name = string(struct.unpack_from("<I", descriptor, 12)[0])
                if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.+-]{1,255}", name):
                    raise GuardError("Invalid PE import basename.")
                dependencies.append(name)
            else:
                raise GuardError("Unterminated/oversized PE import table.")
        exports, export_size = directory(0)
        export_names = u32(locate(exports, 40) + 24) if exports and export_size >= 40 else 0
        debug_rva, debug_size = directory(6)
        return {"machine": hex(machine), "format": "PE32+" if magic == 0x20b else "PE32",
                "managed": managed, "dependencies": sorted(set(dependencies)), "export_name_count": export_names,
                "debug_directory_present": bool(debug_rva and debug_size), "bytes": size}


def literal_candidates(path):
    """Search ASCII key assignments and private key headers without emitting values."""
    patterns = [("PRIVATE_KEY_HEADER", re.compile(rb"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----")),
                ("SECRET_ASSIGNMENT", re.compile(rb"(?i)(?:api[_-]?key|client[_-]?secret|password|access[_-]?token)\s*[:=]\s*[\"']?[A-Za-z0-9_+/=-]{8,}"))]
    matches, offset, carry = [], 0, b""
    with safe_path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        while True:
            block = handle.read(65536)
            if not block:
                break
            text = carry + block
            for rule, pattern in patterns:
                for match in pattern.finditer(text):
                    matches.append({"rule": rule, "byte_offset": offset - len(carry) + match.start()})
            # Explicitly document ASCII coverage; UTF16/encoded/runtime secrets need manual review.
            offset += len(block)
            carry = text[-512:]
            if len(matches) >= 100:
                break
    return list({(m["rule"], m["byte_offset"]): m for m in matches}.values())[:100]


def unity_audit(project, build):
    if project.engine != "unity":
        raise GuardError("Unity metadata inspection requires a Unity project.")
    build = safe_path(build)
    disjoint(project.root, build)
    if not build.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Packaged build directory is missing.")
    paths = list(c.walk_files(build))
    findings = [finding("METADATA_PROTECTOR", "configuration", "NOT_VERIFIED",
                        "No metadata encryption provider approved for this exact Unity version. Engine/loader patches are not performed.")]
    for path in paths:
        name = path.relative_to(build).as_posix()
        if path.suffix.lower() in {".exe", ".dll"}:
            try:
                info = pe_info(path)
            except GuardError:
                findings.append(finding("PE_STRUCTURE", "artifact", "FAIL", "Invalid/truncated executable or DLL; do not delete dependencies to hide it.", [name]))
                continue
            findings.append(finding("MANAGED_MODULE" if info["managed"] else "NATIVE_MODULE", "artifact", "REVIEW",
                                    "Managed code exposes IL; native code remains reversible. Import names are dependencies, not a deletion list.",
                                    [{"path": name, "sha256": c.file_digest(path), **info}]))
            candidates = literal_candidates(path)
            if candidates:
                findings.append(finding("BINARY_SECRET_CANDIDATE", "artifact", "REVIEW", "Potential sensitive literals; confirm locally, never copy values to reports.",
                                        [{"path": name, **m} for m in candidates]))
        if path.name.lower() == "global-metadata.dat":
            with path.open("rb") as handle:
                header = handle.read(8)
            plain = len(header) == 8 and header[:4] == b"\xaf\x1b\xb1\xfa"
            findings.append(finding("IL2CPP_METADATA", "artifact", "REVIEW" if plain else "NOT_VERIFIED",
                                    "Standard metadata header visible; metadata is not encrypted." if plain else
                                    "Unknown/missing metadata header is not proof of encryption; it may be corrupted or a different layout.",
                                    [{"path": name, "sha256": c.file_digest(path), "header_version": struct.unpack("<I", header[4:])[0] if plain else None}]))
        if c.private_symbol(path.relative_to(build)):
            findings.append(finding("PRIVATE_SYMBOL", "artifact", "FAIL", "Separate debug symbols into private storage using stage; retain crash diagnostics.", [name]))
    if not any(p.name.lower() == "global-metadata.dat" for p in paths):
        findings.append(finding("IL2CPP_METADATA", "artifact", "NOT_VERIFIED", "Metadata file not found. Absence does not establish protection."))
    findings.append(finding("NATIVE_RUNTIME", "runtime", "NOT_VERIFIED", "Verify plugin loads, saves, reflection, RPC and exact distributed game. No modules modified. UTF16/encoded/runtime secrets need separate review."))
    return result(project.engine, project.version, findings)

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/cli.py`:

```py
"""Additive CLI namespace; no existing command is replaced."""
from pathlib import Path

from .. import core as c
from . import obfuscation
from .common import read_json


def add_parser(sub):
    root = sub.add_parser("advanced", help="Opt-in P3 protection and evidence tools")
    commands = root.add_subparsers(dest="advanced_command", required=True)
    detect = commands.add_parser("obfuscator-detect", help="Inspect an optional local Obfuz package without executing it")
    detect.add_argument("project", type=Path)
    detect.add_argument("--tool-path", type=Path)
    native = commands.add_parser("unity-inspect", help="Read-only PE, metadata and native-plugin evidence")
    native.add_argument("project", type=Path)
    native.add_argument("--build", type=Path, required=True)
    security = commands.add_parser("security-inspect", help="Read-only source/config risk candidates; no report file")
    security.add_argument("project", type=Path)
    for name in ("unreal-plan", "unreal-apply", "asset-plan"):
        cmd = commands.add_parser(name)
        cmd.add_argument("project", type=Path)
        cmd.add_argument("--config", type=Path, required=True)
        if name == "unreal-apply":
            cmd.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path)
            cmd.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true")
            cmd.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    verify = commands.add_parser("unreal-verify", help="Run matching UE official tools on Pak and IoStore separately")
    verify.add_argument("project", type=Path)
    verify.add_argument("--engine-root", type=Path, required=True)
    verify.add_argument("--build", type=Path, required=True)
    verify.add_argument("--evidence-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    verify.add_argument("--crypto-json", type=Path)
    verify.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true")
    verify.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    create = commands.add_parser("integrity-create", help="Create a release SHA256 manifest outside the build")
    create.add_argument("build", type=Path)
    create.add_argument("--build-id", required=True)
    create.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    create.add_argument("--mutable", action="append", default=[])
    create.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true")
    create.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    integrity = commands.add_parser("integrity-verify", help="Check files against an externally pinned manifest")
    integrity.add_argument("build", type=Path)
    integrity.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, required=True)
    integrity.add_argument("--build-id", required=True)
    integrity.add_argument("--expected-manifest-sha256", required=True)
    drm = commands.add_parser("drm-inspect", help="Check retained original and wrapped PE; never upload")
    drm.add_argument("--original", type=Path, required=True)
    drm.add_argument("--wrapped", type=Path, required=True)
    drm.add_argument("--expected-original-sha256", required=True)
    commands.add_parser("acceptance-template", help="Print an unverified performance/runtime record template; no file writes")
    acceptance = commands.add_parser("acceptance-check", help="Compare metrics and bind evidence to exact baseline/candidate builds")
    acceptance.add_argument("record", type=Path)
    for arg in ("baseline-build", "candidate-build", "baseline-manifest", "candidate-manifest", "evidence-root"):
        acceptance.add_argument("--" + arg, type=Path, required=True)
    for name in ("unity-plan", "unity-apply", "unity-build"):
        cmd = commands.add_parser(name)
        cmd.add_argument("project", type=Path)
        cmd.add_argument("--config", required=True, type=Path)
        if name == "unity-apply":
            cmd.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path)
        if name == "unity-build":
            cmd.add_argument("--editor", required=True, type=Path)
            cmd.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
            cmd.add_argument("--evidence-dir", required=True, type=Path)
            cmd.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=3600)
        if name != "unity-plan":
            cmd.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true")
            cmd.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    for cmd in commands.choices.values():
        cmd.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", default=__import__("argparse").SUPPRESS)
    return commands


def run(args, confirm, emit, operation):
    command = args.advanced_command
    if command.startswith("acceptance-"):
        from . import acceptance
        if command == "acceptance-template":
            return emit("TEMPLATE", acceptance.template())
        record = acceptance.check(read_json(args.record), args.baseline_build, args.candidate_build,
                                  args.baseline_manifest, args.candidate_manifest, args.evidence_root)
        return emit("INSPECTED", record, code=2 if record["release_status"] == "BLOCKED" else 0)
    if command.startswith("integrity-") or command == "drm-inspect":
        return run_integrity(args, confirm, emit, operation)
    project = c.detect_project(args.project)
    if command == "security-inspect":
        from .security import audit
        return emit("INSPECTED", audit(project, operation=operation))
    if command.startswith("unreal-") or command == "asset-plan":
        return run_unreal(args, project, confirm, emit, operation)
    if command == "unity-inspect":
        from .binary import unity_audit
        inspected = unity_audit(project, args.build)
        return emit("INSPECTED", inspected, code=2 if inspected["release_status"] == "BLOCKED" else 0)
    adapter = obfuscation.ObfuzAdapter()
    if command == "obfuscator-detect":
        return emit("INSPECTED", adapter.detect(project, args.tool_path))
    config = read_json(args.config)
    if command == "unity-build":
        preview = obfuscation.build_preview(project, config, args.editor, args.output, args.evidence_dir)
        decision = confirm(args, preview)
        if decision is not None:
            return decision
        record = obfuscation.build(project, config, args.editor, args.output, args.evidence_dir,
                                   operation=operation, timeout=args.timeout)
        return emit(record["status"], record, code=2 if record["status"] == "FAIL" else 0)
    planned = adapter.plan(project, config)
    preview = {"engine": project.engine, "engine_version": project.version,
               "changes": [{"path": x.path, "purpose": x.purpose, "before_sha256": x.before_sha256} for x in planned],
               "configuration": "REVIEW", "artifact": "NOT_VERIFIED", "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED"}
    if command == "unity-plan":
        return emit("PREVIEW", preview)
    decision = confirm(args, preview)
    if decision is not None:
        return decision
    found = adapter.detect(project)
    if not any(x["api_supported"] for x in found["installed_candidates"]):
        raise c.GuardError("Install reviewed Obfuz 4.0.0 in the project before applying its bridge; no files changed.")
    manifest = obfuscation.apply(project, config, expected_plan=planned, backup_dir=args.backup_dir, operation=operation)
    return emit("APPLIED", {**preview, "journal": str(manifest) if manifest else None})


def run_unreal(args, project, confirm, emit, operation):
    from . import unreal
    command = args.advanced_command
    if command == "unreal-verify":
        preview = unreal.verify_preview(project, args.engine_root, args.build, args.evidence_dir, args.crypto_json)
        decision = confirm(args, preview)
        if decision is not None:
            return decision
        record = unreal.verify(project, args.engine_root, args.build, args.evidence_dir, args.crypto_json, operation=operation)
        return emit(record["status"], record, code=2 if record["status"] == "FAIL" else 0)
    config = read_json(args.config)
    if command == "asset-plan":
        return emit("PREVIEW", unreal.asset_plan(project, config))
    planned = unreal.UnrealAdapter().plan(project, config)
    preview = {"engine": project.engine, "engine_version": project.version,
               "changes": [{"path": x.path, "purpose": x.purpose, "before_sha256": x.before_sha256} for x in planned],
               "requested_switches": config["pak"], "container_policy": config["container_policy"],
               "pak_requested": unreal.CONTAINERS[config["container_policy"]][0],
               "iostore_requested": unreal.CONTAINERS[config["container_policy"]][1],
               "signing_key_validity": "NOT_VERIFIED" if config["pak"]["signing"] else "NOT_REQUESTED",
               "artifact": "NOT_VERIFIED", "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED",
               "tradeoff": "Disabling a switch reduces its corresponding protection. All-asset encryption increases IO/patch cost; grade rules do not automatically create chunks."}
    if command == "unreal-plan":
        return emit("PREVIEW", preview)
    decision = confirm(args, preview)
    if decision is not None:
        return decision
    journal = unreal.apply(project, config, expected_plan=planned, backup_dir=args.backup_dir, operation=operation)
    return emit("APPLIED", {**preview, "journal": str(journal) if journal else None})


def run_integrity(args, confirm, emit, operation):
    from . import integrity
    if args.advanced_command == "drm-inspect":
        return emit("INSPECTED", integrity.drm_inspect(args.original, args.wrapped, args.expected_original_sha256))
    if args.advanced_command == "integrity-verify":
        record = integrity.verify(args.build, args.manifest, args.build_id, args.expected_manifest_sha256, operation=operation)
        return emit(record["status"], record, code=2 if record["status"] == "INTEGRITY_MISMATCH" else 0)
    preview = integrity.create_preview(args.build, args.build_id, args.output, args.mutable, operation=operation)
    decision = confirm(args, preview)
    if decision is not None:
        return decision
    return emit("CREATED", integrity.create(args.build, args.build_id, args.output, args.mutable,
                                           operation=operation, expected_preview=preview))
```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/common.py`:

```py
"""Shared local adapter contracts, strict input and private tool execution."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import time
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from pathlib import Path

from .. import core as c
from ..operations import GuardError, Operation, check_space
from ..project_backup import safe_path, disjoint, relative_name


class ProtectionAdapter(ABC):
    """Tools are optional. Detection must never install or execute them."""
    @abstractmethod
    def detect(self, project, tool_path=None):
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def plan(self, project, config):
        raise NotImplementedError


def parse_json(data):
    def pairs(items):
        result = {}
        for key, value in items:
            if key in result:
                raise ValueError("duplicate key")
            result[key] = value
        return result
    try:
        return json.loads(data.decode("utf-8-sig"), object_pairs_hook=pairs,
                          parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError("nonfinite")))
    except (OSError, ValueError, UnicodeError):
        raise GuardError("Cannot read a bounded, valid JSON document (duplicate keys are forbidden).") from None


def read_bounded(path, max_bytes):
    try:
        with safe_path(path).open("rb") as handle:
            data = handle.read(max_bytes + 1)
        if len(data) > max_bytes:
            raise GuardError("Input exceeds the supported size limit.")
        return data
    except OSError:
        raise GuardError("Cannot read the selected input file.") from None


def read_json(path, *, max_bytes=4 * 1024 * 1024):
    return parse_json(read_bounded(path, max_bytes))


def shape(value, required, optional=()):
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) - set(required) - set(optional) or set(required) - set(value):
        raise GuardError("Configuration has missing or unknown fields; use the documented schema.")


def names(values, *, patterns=False, empty=False):
    if not isinstance(values, list) or (not values and not empty) or len(values) > 1000:
        raise GuardError("Expected a bounded list of explicit names.")
    regex = r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.`/+-]*(?:\*)?" if patterns else r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.`/+-]*"
    if any(not isinstance(v, str) or len(v) > 256 or not re.fullmatch(regex, v) for v in values):
        raise GuardError("Use explicit type/assembly names; exclusions may end in a wildcard.")
    if len(set(v.casefold() for v in values)) != len(values):
        raise GuardError("Duplicate names are not allowed.")
    return values


def changes(project, values):
    # Preserve P0's version and incomplete-full-restore preflight.
    from ..project_backup import restored_roots
    c.require_supported(project)
    restored_roots(project.root)
    result, seen = [], set()
    for name, data, purpose in values:
        relative_name(name)
        target = c.local_path(project.root, name)
        if name.casefold() in seen or target.is_relative_to(project.root / c.STATE):
            raise GuardError("Duplicate/private transaction target.")
        seen.add(name.casefold())
        before = target.read_bytes() if target.exists() else None
        if before != data:
            result.append(c.Change(name, data, purpose, c.digest(before) if before is not None else None))
    return result


def new_output(value, *inputs, create=False, bytes_needed=0):
    folder = safe_path(value)
    for source in inputs:
        disjoint(safe_path(source), folder)
    if folder.exists():
        raise GuardError("Choose a NEW private output directory; existing paths are never reused.")
    check_space([(folder, bytes_needed)])
    if create:
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        (folder / ".gitignore").write_bytes(b"*\n")
    return folder


def private_run(argv, folder, *, timeout=600, secrets=(), operation=None, cwd=None):
    """No shell. Explicit local executable; bounded/redacted log, hidden Windows window.

    This is not a sandbox for third party code. Only run a trusted, reviewed tool.
    Do not pass secrets in argv. Their local reference files are read by the tool.
    """
    executable = safe_path(argv[0])
    if not executable.is_file() or (os.name == "nt" and executable.suffix.lower() != ".exe"):
        raise GuardError("An installed executable is required; shell scripts are not accepted.")
    if not 1 <= timeout <= 86400:
        raise GuardError("Tool timeout must be between 1 and 86400 seconds.")
    op = operation or Operation()
    log = folder / "tool.log"
    spool = folder / "tool-output.private"
    started = time.monotonic()
    log_limit = 16 * 1024 * 1024
    oversized = False
    # File-backed output avoids an unbounded in-memory PIPE or pipe deadlock.
    process = None
    try:
        with spool.open("xb") as output:
            process = subprocess.Popen(list(map(str, argv)), cwd=cwd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                       stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, shell=False,
                                       creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
            while process.poll() is None:
                op.check_cancel()
                if time.monotonic() - started > timeout or spool.stat().st_size > log_limit:
                    raise GuardError("Tool exceeded time/log limit; partial output retained, no protection approved.")
                time.sleep(.1)
        code = process.returncode
    finally:
        if process is not None and process.poll() is None:
            process.kill()
            process.wait(timeout=10)
        if spool.exists():
            # Also bound a fast process which exits between polls. Never read the
            # whole spool before slicing; that defeats the memory limit.
            with spool.open("rb") as handle:
                data = handle.read(log_limit + 1)
            oversized = len(data) > log_limit
            raw = data[:log_limit].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            for value in sorted((v for v in secrets if v), key=len, reverse=True):
                raw = raw.replace(value, "[REDACTED]")
            raw = re.sub(r"(?im)((?:key|secret|password|token|exponent|modulus)\s*[:=]\s*)[^\r\n]+", r"\1[REDACTED]", raw)
            with log.open("xb") as handle:
                handle.write(raw.encode("utf-8"))
            spool.unlink()  # Only our own bounded temporary log; no game file deletion.
    if oversized:
        raise GuardError("Tool exceeded log limit; truncated private log retained, no protection approved.")
    return {"exit_code": code, "log": str(log), "log_sha256": c.file_digest(log),
            "tool_sha256": c.file_digest(executable), "elapsed_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - started, 3)}


def result(engine, version, findings):
    return {"schema": 1, "engine": engine, "engine_version": version,
            "release_status": "BLOCKED" if any(f["status"] == "FAIL" for f in findings) else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW",
            "findings": findings}


def finding(identifier, layer, status, detail, evidence=()):
    if layer not in {"configuration", "artifact", "runtime"} or status not in {"PASS", "FAIL", "REVIEW", "NOT_VERIFIED"}:
        raise ValueError("Invalid evidence classification")
    return {"id": identifier, "layer": layer, "status": status, "detail": detail, "evidence": list(evidence)}

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/integrity.py`:

```py
"""SHA256 release snapshots with an externally pinned manifest and build ID.

This detects changes against a trusted baseline; it is not anti-cheat, DRM or
an authenticated signature. Never ban players or delete files from this result.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import fnmatch
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

from .. import core as c
from ..operations import GuardError, Operation, check_space, writer
from ..project_backup import safe_path, relative_name
from .common import read_bounded, parse_json, shape


def build_id(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}", value):
        raise GuardError("Use a nonempty, bounded build ID (letters, digits, dot, dash, underscore).")
    return value


def patterns(values):
    if not isinstance(values, list) or len(values) > 100:
        raise GuardError("Invalid mutable-path list.")
    for value in values:
        relative_name(value)
        if value in {"*", "**"} or not any(char.isalnum() for char in value):
            raise GuardError("A whole-build mutable pattern would disable integrity checking.")
    return values


def inventory(build, mutable, *, operation=None):
    build = safe_path(build)
    if not build.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Build directory is missing.")
    op = operation or Operation()
    files, ignored, seen = [], [], set()
    for path in sorted(c.walk_files(build)):
        op.check_cancel()
        name = relative_name(path.relative_to(build).as_posix())
        if name.casefold() in seen:
            raise GuardError("Case-colliding release paths are not portable.")
        seen.add(name.casefold())
        if any(fnmatch.fnmatchcase(name.casefold(), p.casefold()) for p in mutable):
            ignored.append(name)
            continue
        before = path.stat()
        checksum = c.file_digest(path)
        after = path.stat()
        if (before.st_size, before.st_mtime_ns, before.st_ino) != (after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ino):
            raise GuardError("Build changed during hashing; stop the game/updater and retry.")
        files.append({"path": name, "bytes": before.st_size, "sha256": checksum})
    if not files:
        raise GuardError("No immutable build files selected.")
    return files, ignored


def create_preview(build, identifier, output, mutable=(), *, operation=None):
    identifier = build_id(identifier)
    mutable = patterns(list(mutable))
    build, output = safe_path(build), safe_path(output)
    if output.is_relative_to(build) or output == build or output.exists():
        raise GuardError("Manifest output must be a new file OUTSIDE the build.")
    files, ignored = inventory(build, mutable, operation=operation)
    return {"build_id": identifier, "output": str(output), "files": files,
            "mutable": mutable, "ignored": ignored, "authentication": "External manifest hash required; SHA256 is not a signature."}


def create(build, identifier, output, mutable=(), *, operation=None, expected_preview=None):
    with writer():
        preview = create_preview(build, identifier, output, mutable, operation=operation)
        if expected_preview is not None and preview != expected_preview:
            raise GuardError("Build changed after manifest preview.")
        second, _ = inventory(build, preview["mutable"], operation=operation)
        if second != preview["files"]:
            raise GuardError("Build changed during manifest creation.")
        manifest = {"schema": 1, "algorithm": "SHA256", "build_id": identifier,
                    "created_utc": c.utc_now(), "files": preview["files"], "mutable": preview["mutable"]}
        data = (json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
        if len(data) > 64 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise GuardError("Manifest exceeds the supported 64 MiB limit; use smaller release/depot scopes.")
        target = safe_path(output)
        check_space([(target.parent, len(data))])
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with target.open("xb") as handle:
            handle.write(data)
            handle.flush()
            __import__("os").fsync(handle.fileno())
        return {"status": "CREATED", "build_id": identifier, "manifest": str(target),
                "manifest_sha256": c.file_digest(target), "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
                "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED", "instruction": "Pin this manifest hash outside the client (trusted CI/release record/server). Do not trust a hash stored beside replaceable files."}


def load_pinned(path, expected_hash, identifier):
    build_id(identifier)
    path = safe_path(path)
    if not isinstance(expected_hash, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{64}", expected_hash):
        raise GuardError("An externally trusted manifest SHA256 is required.")
    data = read_bounded(path, 64 * 1024 * 1024)
    if c.digest(data) != expected_hash.lower():
        raise GuardError("Manifest hash does not match the trusted pin; no baseline trusted.")
    # Parse exactly the bytes whose pin was checked, not a second filesystem read.
    manifest = parse_json(data)
    shape(manifest, {"schema", "algorithm", "build_id", "created_utc", "files", "mutable"})
    if manifest["schema"] != 1 or manifest["algorithm"] != "SHA256" or manifest["build_id"] != identifier:
        raise GuardError("Manifest schema/algorithm/build ID mismatch; updates require a new trusted baseline.")
    patterns(manifest["mutable"])
    if not isinstance(manifest["files"], list) or not manifest["files"]:
        raise GuardError("Invalid/empty release manifest.")
    seen = set()
    for item in manifest["files"]:
        shape(item, {"path", "bytes", "sha256"})
        name = relative_name(item["path"])
        if (name.casefold() in seen or type(item["bytes"]) is not int or item["bytes"] < 0
                or not isinstance(item["sha256"], str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", item["sha256"])
                or any(fnmatch.fnmatchcase(name.casefold(), p.casefold()) for p in manifest["mutable"])):
            raise GuardError("Invalid, duplicate or mutable entry in immutable manifest.")
        seen.add(name.casefold())
    return manifest


def verify(build, manifest_path, identifier, expected_hash, *, operation=None):
    manifest = load_pinned(manifest_path, expected_hash, identifier)
    actual, ignored = inventory(build, manifest["mutable"], operation=operation)
    expected = {f["path"]: f for f in manifest["files"]}
    current = {f["path"]: f for f in actual}
    missing = sorted(set(expected) - set(current))
    unexpected = sorted(set(current) - set(expected))
    changed = sorted(p for p in expected.keys() & current.keys() if expected[p] != current[p])
    matched = not (missing or unexpected or changed)
    return {"status": "INTEGRITY_MATCH" if matched else "INTEGRITY_MISMATCH", "build_id": identifier,
            "manifest_sha256": expected_hash.lower(), "missing": missing, "unexpected": unexpected,
            "changed": changed, "mutable_files_not_checked": ignored,
            "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED", "protection": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "scope": "File bytes/inventory only. Client checks can be patched; trust the manifest pin independently.",
            "action": "No action required for this file check." if matched else
                      "Pause this check during authorized updates. Offer Steam file verification/repair; preserve saves/mods; never delete files, auto-ban or accuse the player."}


def drm_inspect(original, wrapped, expected_original_hash):
    from .binary import pe_info
    original, wrapped = safe_path(original), safe_path(wrapped)
    if original == wrapped or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{64}", expected_original_hash):
        raise GuardError("Keep distinct original/wrapped executables and the original pre-wrapper SHA256.")
    if c.file_digest(original) != expected_original_hash.lower():
        raise GuardError("Original executable changed since the pre-wrapper checkpoint.")
    before, after = pe_info(original), pe_info(wrapped)
    changed = c.file_digest(original) != c.file_digest(wrapped)
    return {"status": "REVIEW" if changed else "NOT_VERIFIED", "original_preserved": True,
            "wrapped_pe_structure": "VALID", "bytes_changed": changed, "machine_matches": before["machine"] == after["machine"],
            "wrapped_sha256": c.file_digest(wrapped), "wrapper_success": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "steam_launch": "NOT_VERIFIED", "offline_launch": "NOT_VERIFIED", "test_branch_reinstall": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "scope": "PE structure/hash comparison does not prove Steam wrapping. Inspect authorized wrapper log and the actual Steam test branch. No executable run or upload occurred."}

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/obfuscation.py`:

```py
"""Explicit, version-gated Obfuz integration before Unity's IL2CPP step."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

from .. import core as c, project_backup, transactions
from ..operations import GuardError, writer
from .common import ProtectionAdapter, changes, names, new_output, private_run, read_json, shape

PACKAGE = "com.code-philosophy.obfuz"
VERSION = "4.0.0"
SOURCE_COMMIT = "b83c53d4df525232a4d8e783d68995706b3a5d5d"
ROOT = "Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/AdvancedEditor"
CONFIG = "ProjectSettings/SteamReleaseGuardObfuscation.json"


def validate(project, value):
    shape(value, {"schema", "engine_version", "adapter", "tool_version", "assemblies", "reference_assemblies", "strings", "control_flow"})
    if project.engine != "unity" or value["schema"] != 1 or value["engine_version"] != project.version:
        raise GuardError("Unity project and exact config engine version must match.")
    c.require_supported(project)
    if value["adapter"] != "obfuz" or value["tool_version"] != VERSION:
        raise GuardError("Only the reviewed Obfuz 4.0.0 API is supported; other versions need review.")
    if type(value["strings"]) is not bool or type(value["control_flow"]) is not bool:
        raise GuardError("Protection switches must be booleans.")
    # These passes depend on a generated VM/runtime initialization. No unverified
    # initializer or secret material is generated by this adapter.
    if value["strings"] or value["control_flow"]:
        raise GuardError("This adapter enables symbol renaming only. String/control-flow passes require a separately validated runtime integration; NOT_VERIFIED.")
    assemblies = value["assemblies"]
    if not isinstance(assemblies, list) or not assemblies or len(assemblies) > 100:
        raise GuardError("Select explicit game assemblies and safe types; no default whole-project obfuscation.")
    for assembly in assemblies:
        shape(assembly, {"name", "include_types", "exclude_types", "rename_fields"})
        names([assembly["name"]])
        if "/" in assembly["name"] or assembly["name"].startswith(("Unity", "System", "Obfuz", "Microsoft")):
            raise GuardError("Select your own assembly, not an engine/framework/tool assembly.")
        names(assembly["include_types"])
        names(assembly["exclude_types"], patterns=True, empty=True)
        if type(assembly["rename_fields"]) is not bool:
            raise GuardError("rename_fields must be an explicit boolean (recommended false).")
    names([a["name"] for a in assemblies])
    names(value["reference_assemblies"], empty=True)
    for name in [*(a["name"] for a in assemblies), *value["reference_assemblies"]]:
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*", name) or name.endswith(".dll") or ".." in name:
            raise GuardError("Assembly names must be simple names without a path or .dll extension.")
    if set(value["reference_assemblies"]) & {a["name"] for a in assemblies}:
        raise GuardError("Obfuscated and referencing-only assemblies must be disjoint.")
    return value


def rules(config):
    root = ET.Element("obfuz")
    for item in config["assemblies"]:
        assembly = ET.SubElement(root, "assembly", name=item["name"])
        ET.SubElement(assembly, "type", name="*", obName="0", applyToMembers="*", applyToNestedTypes="1")
        for name in item["include_types"]:
            selected = ET.SubElement(assembly, "type", name=name, obName="1", applyToMembers="method", applyToNestedTypes="0")
            ET.SubElement(selected, "field", name="*", obName="1" if item["rename_fields"] else "0")
        # Obfuz's symbol policy applies matching rules in document order: last wins.
        for name in item["exclude_types"]:
            ET.SubElement(assembly, "type", name=name, obName="0", applyToMembers="*", applyToNestedTypes="1")
    ET.indent(root)
    return ET.tostring(root, encoding="utf-8", xml_declaration=True) + b"\n"


class ObfuzAdapter(ProtectionAdapter):
    def detect(self, project, tool_path=None):
        candidates = [Path(tool_path)] if tool_path else [project.root / "Packages" / PACKAGE]
        cache = project.root / "Library/PackageCache"
        if tool_path is None and cache.is_dir():
            candidates += sorted(cache.glob(PACKAGE + "@*"))
        found = []
        for path in candidates:
            if not (path / "package.json").is_file():
                continue
            data = read_json(path / "package.json")
            if data.get("name") == PACKAGE:
                found.append({"path": str(path), "version": data.get("version"),
                              "api_supported": data.get("version") == VERSION})
        return {"adapter": "obfuz", "installed_candidates": found,
                "status": "DETECTED" if found else "MISSING",
                "reason": "Version/path detection only; Unity must resolve this package at build time." if found else
                          "Obfuz package not found. Install a reviewed version yourself or provide --tool-path; nothing was downloaded.",
                "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED", "reviewed_source": SOURCE_COMMIT}

    def plan(self, project, config):
        validate(project, config)
        # Install baseline Editor builder through the same single transaction.
        values = [(x.path, x.data, x.purpose) for x in c.plan(project)]
        source = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "adapters/unity-advanced"
        for name in ("SteamReleaseGuardObfuscation.cs", "SteamReleaseGuard.Obfuscation.Editor.asmdef"):
            values.append((ROOT + "/" + name, (source / name).read_bytes(), "Opt-in Obfuz build bridge (Editor only)"))
        values += [(CONFIG, (json.dumps(config, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n").encode(), "Explicit obfuscation allowlist; no keys"),
                   (ROOT + "/rename.xml", rules(config), "Keep all names except reviewed selected types; exclusions win")]
        return changes(project, values)


def apply(project, config, **kwargs):
    if not any(x["api_supported"] for x in ObfuzAdapter().detect(project)["installed_candidates"]):
        raise GuardError("Reviewed Obfuz package missing; bridge was not installed.")
    return transactions.apply(project, planner=lambda: ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, config), **kwargs)


def build_preview(project, config, editor, output, evidence_dir):
    validate(project, config)
    if not project_backup.restored_roots(project.root):
        raise GuardError("Advanced engine execution requires a verified backup-restore project copy.")
    if ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, config):
        raise GuardError("Review and apply unity-plan first; bridge/config does not match.")
    editor = project_backup.safe_path(editor)
    if not editor.is_file() or editor.name.lower() != "unity.exe":
        raise GuardError("Choose the exact installed Unity Editor executable.")
    output = new_output(output, project.root)
    evidence = new_output(evidence_dir, project.root, output)
    return {"editor": str(editor), "output": str(output), "evidence": str(evidence),
            "engine_version": project.version, "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "scope": "Run the local Editor on a restored copy; preserve original project and keys. Private logs/mapping stay outside depot."}


def build(project, config, editor, output, evidence_dir, *, operation=None, timeout=3600):
    with writer():
        preview = build_preview(project, config, editor, output, evidence_dir)
        evidence = new_output(evidence_dir, project.root, output, create=True)
        argv = [str(editor), "-batchmode", "-quit", "-projectPath", str(project.root), "-buildTarget", "Win64",
                "-executeMethod", "SteamReleaseGuard.Advanced.ObfuscatedBuild.BuildWindows", "-srgOutput", str(Path(output) / "Game.exe"),
                "-srgEvidence", str(evidence), "-logFile", "-"]
        record = {**preview, "status": "INCOMPLETE"}
        c.write_json(evidence / "execution.json", record)
        execution = private_run(argv, evidence, operation=operation, timeout=timeout)
        receipt_path = evidence / "obfuscation.json"
        receipt = read_json(receipt_path) if receipt_path.exists() else None
        if receipt is not None:
            shape(receipt, {"configuration", "artifact", "runtime", "engine_version", "tool_version", "executable_sha256", "assemblies"})
            if (receipt["configuration"] != "PASS" or receipt["artifact"] != "PASS" or receipt["runtime"] != "NOT_VERIFIED"
                    or receipt["engine_version"] != project.version or receipt["tool_version"] != VERSION
                    or not isinstance(receipt["assemblies"], list)):
                raise GuardError("Invalid/unsupported obfuscation evidence; no protection approved.")
            expected_names = {a["name"] for a in config["assemblies"]}
            actual_names = []
            for row in receipt["assemblies"]:
                shape(row, {"name", "before_sha256", "after_sha256", "renamed_selected_types"})
                if (row["name"] not in expected_names or type(row["renamed_selected_types"]) is not int or row["renamed_selected_types"] < 1
                        or any(not isinstance(row[k], str) or not re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{64}", row[k]) for k in ("before_sha256", "after_sha256"))
                        or row["before_sha256"] == row["after_sha256"]):
                    raise GuardError("Missing selected assembly rename evidence.")
                actual_names.append(row["name"])
            executable = Path(output) / "Game.exe"
            if set(actual_names) != expected_names or len(actual_names) != len(expected_names) or not executable.is_file() or c.file_digest(executable) != receipt["executable_sha256"]:
                raise GuardError("Obfuscation receipt is not bound to the generated game output.")
            if ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, config):
                raise GuardError("Adapter/config changed during build; review this incomplete evidence.")
        record.update(execution=execution, artifact=receipt, status="REVIEW" if execution["exit_code"] == 0 and receipt else "FAIL")
        c.write_json(evidence / "execution.json", record)
        return record

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/security.py`:

```py
"""Read-only, location-only client security triage, not semantic vulnerability proof."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from .. import core as c
from ..operations import Operation
from .common import finding, result

SKIP = {".git", c.STATE, "Library", "Temp", "Logs", "obj", "Obj", "Intermediate", "Saved",
        "Binaries", "node_modules", "Builds", "build", "dist", "__pycache__"}
TEXT = {".cs", ".cpp", ".cc", ".c", ".h", ".hpp", ".ini", ".json", ".yaml", ".yml", ".config", ".env", ".lua", ".js", ".ts"}
ECONOMY = r"(?:coins?|currency|wallet|balance|inventory|rewards?|trade|winner|score|rank|loot|damage)"
RULES = [
    ("HARDCODED_SECRET", re.compile(r'''(?i)\b(?:api[_-]?key|client[_-]?secret|password|access[_-]?token|private[_-]?key)\b["']?\s*[:=]\s*["'][^"'\r\n]{8,}["']'''),
     "Potential literal credential in client code/config.",
     "Confirm privately. Move privileged secrets to a server/secret store; revoke exposed credentials through the owner's approved process, never rotate automatically."),
    ("PRIVATE_KEY_MATERIAL", re.compile(r"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----"),
     "Private-key material marker in project text.",
     "Check whether it is a test fixture or production key. Keep production private keys out of the client and depot; do not copy values into chat/reports."),
    ("CLIENT_ECONOMY_WRITE", re.compile(r"(?i)\b" + ECONOMY + r"\w*\b[^\n]{0,80}(?:\+=|-=|(?<![=!<>])=(?!=)|\.Add\(|\.Remove\()"),
     "Potential client-side mutation of a valuable result.",
     "Trace the authoritative server path. Server should validate ownership, balance, prices, limits, replay/idempotency and atomic settlement; client sends intent, not final rewards."),
    ("LOCAL_CRITICAL_SAVE", re.compile(r'''(?i)PlayerPrefs\.Set(?:Int|Float|String)\s*\(\s*["'][^"']*''' + ECONOMY),
     "Important-looking game data stored in editable client preferences.",
     "Offline saves may be intentionally editable. Multiplayer economy/permissions must be checked against authoritative server state; encrypting PlayerPrefs does not establish authority."),
    ("CLIENT_PRIVILEGE", re.compile(r'''(?i)\b(?:isAdmin|adminRole|userRole|permissionLevel)\b\s*(?:=|\+=)|(?:role|permission)\s*=\s*["']admin["']'''),
     "Client-side permission/role assignment candidate.",
     "Server must derive permissions from the authenticated session and validate each privileged request; UI flags are not authorization."),
    ("NETWORK_MUTATION", re.compile(r"\b(?:ServerRpc|RequireOwnership|WithValidation)\b|UFUNCTION\s*\([^\n]*\bServer\b|\b(?:Request|Submit|Send)(?:Trade|Reward|Result|Purchase)\b"),
     "Network entry point or authority annotation needs semantic review.",
     "Inspect the matching server handler: identity, ownership, ranges, rate/replay checks and transaction consistency. An RPC/validation attribute alone is not proof; it may already be implemented correctly."),
    ("UNTRUSTED_DESERIALIZATION", re.compile(r"\bBinaryFormatter\b|TypeNameHandling\s*=\s*TypeNameHandling\.(?:All|Auto|Objects)"),
     "Deserialization mode can be risky with untrusted save/network data.",
     "Trace data provenance; prefer explicit bounded schemas and permitted types. Do not deserialize arbitrary client-supplied types on a server."),
    ("TLS_VALIDATION_BYPASS", re.compile(r"ServerCertificateValidationCallback[^\n]*(?:=>\s*true|return\s+true)|ValidateCertificate[^\n]*=>\s*true"),
     "Possible certificate-validation bypass.",
     "Restore normal certificate checks for release; inspect multi-line implementations and platform-specific transport settings separately."),
    ("RELEASE_DEBUG_ENTRY", re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:DebugMenu|GodMode|SkipLevel|CheatConsole|EnableCheats)\b|UFUNCTION\s*\([^\n]*\bExec\b"),
     "Potential development/cheat entry point.",
     "Verify compile-time guards and the actual Shipping/non-development build; Editor-only source is not proof of a shipped debug feature."),
]
SERVER_HINT = re.compile(r"\b(?:IsServer|HasAuthority|WithValidation|RequireOwnership|_Validate)\b|\[Server\]")
DEV_HINT = re.compile(r"UNITY_EDITOR|DEVELOPMENT_BUILD|UE_BUILD_SHIPPING|WITH_EDITOR")
RNG = re.compile(r"\b(?:Random\.(?:Range|Next|value)|FMath::Rand\w*)\b")


def audit(project, *, operation=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    findings, scanned, skipped = [], 0, []
    for path in c.walk_files(project.root, SKIP):
        op.check_cancel()
        if path.suffix.lower() not in TEXT and path.name != ".env":
            continue
        relative = path.relative_to(project.root).as_posix()
        if path.stat().st_size > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
            skipped.append({"path": relative, "reason": "text file exceeds 2 MiB review limit"})
            continue
        try:
            raw = path.read_bytes()
            text = raw.decode("utf-16" if raw.startswith((b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff")) else "utf-8-sig")
        except UnicodeError:
            skipped.append({"path": relative, "reason": "unsupported text encoding"})
            continue
        scanned += 1
        lines = text.splitlines()
        observations = {"authority_tokens_present_in_file": bool(SERVER_HINT.search(text)),
                        "development_guard_tokens_present_in_file": bool(DEV_HINT.search(text))}
        for line_number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
            for identifier, pattern, problem, remedy in RULES:
                if pattern.search(line):
                    findings.append(finding(identifier, "configuration", "REVIEW", problem + " " + remedy,
                                            [{"path": relative, "line": line_number, **observations}]))
            if RNG.search(line) and re.search(ECONOMY, "\n".join(lines[max(0, line_number - 4):line_number + 3]), re.I):
                findings.append(finding("LOCAL_REWARD_RANDOMNESS", "configuration", "REVIEW",
                                        "Local RNG near valuable outcomes. Trace whether it is cosmetic/offline or authoritative; multiplayer reward outcomes must be generated/validated by the server.",
                                        [{"path": relative, "line": line_number, **observations}]))
            if len(findings) >= 2000:
                skipped.append({"path": relative, "reason": "finding limit reached; narrow the review scope"})
                break
        if len(findings) >= 2000:
            break
    findings.append(finding("SERVER_AUTHORITY", "runtime", "NOT_VERIFIED",
                            "Text matches do not establish that server checks are missing or correct. Review handlers and run unauthorized/replay/ownership/invalid-value tests against an authorized test server. No server or game logic changed."))
    findings.append(finding("SCAN_COVERAGE", "configuration", "REVIEW" if skipped else "NOT_VERIFIED",
                            "Heuristic text triage only; comments/examples may match. Negative results and authority tokens are not a security PASS. Blueprints, binaries, dynamic code, encoded secrets and external services require separate review.", skipped))
    output = result(project.engine, project.version, findings)
    output.update(scanned_text_files=scanned, excluded_directory_names=sorted(SKIP), output="Conversation/stdout only; no report file written.")
    return output

```

`steam_release_guard/advanced/unreal.py`:

```py
"""UE CryptoKeys policy and separate official Pak / IoStore inspection."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import binascii
import fnmatch
import re
from pathlib import Path

from .. import core as c, transactions
from ..operations import Cancelled, GuardError, writer
from ..project_backup import safe_path, disjoint, relative_name
from .common import ProtectionAdapter, changes, finding, new_output, private_run, read_json, shape

FLAGS = {"index": "bEncryptPakIndex", "ini": "bEncryptPakIniFiles", "uasset": "bEncryptUAssetFiles",
         "all_assets": "bEncryptAllAssetFiles", "signing": "bEnablePakSigning"}
CONTAINERS = {"pak-only": (True, False), "iostore-only": (False, True), "pak+iostore": (True, True)}


def version_matches(project, exact):
    return isinstance(exact, str) and bool(re.fullmatch(r"5\.[0-6](?:\.\d+)?", exact)) and (exact == project.version or exact.startswith(project.version + "."))


def signing_shape(values):
    """Encoding/nonzero checks only. UE must validate actual RSA material/signatures."""
    for name in ("signingmodulus", "signingprivateexponent", "signingpublicexponent"):
        raw = values.get(name, "").strip().strip('"')
        try:
            data = bytes.fromhex(raw[2:]) if raw.startswith("0x") else base64.b64decode(raw, validate=True)
        except (ValueError, binascii.Error):
            raise GuardError("Existing signing material has an unsupported encoding; validate it in the exact UE Editor.") from None
        if not data or len(data) > 1024 or not any(data):
            raise GuardError("Existing signing material is missing, zero or malformed; no keys are generated.")


def validate(project, config):
    if isinstance(config, dict) and config.get("schema") == 1:
        raise GuardError("UE policy schema 1 does not explicitly select Pak. Review and migrate to schema 2 with container_policy: pak-only, iostore-only or pak+iostore; no automatic conversion or changes.")
    shape(config, {"schema", "engine_version", "pak", "container_policy", "assets"})
    if project.engine != "unreal" or type(config["schema"]) is not int or config["schema"] != 2 or not version_matches(project, config["engine_version"]):
        raise GuardError("Reviewed UE 5.0–5.6 settings profile must match the project version; other versions need review.")
    shape(config["pak"], FLAGS)
    if not isinstance(config["container_policy"], str) or config["container_policy"] not in CONTAINERS:
        raise GuardError("Explicitly select container_policy: pak-only, iostore-only or pak+iostore.")
    if config["container_policy"] == "iostore-only":
        raise GuardError("IoStore-only is NOT_VERIFIED for this Windows/UAT profile. Official Zen Loader staging also uses companion Pak files; no validated standalone combination is available. No files changed and Pak will not be enabled automatically.")
    if any(type(v) is not bool for v in config["pak"].values()):
        raise GuardError("Each CryptoKeys option must be an explicit boolean.")
    crypto_bytes = c.read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultCrypto.ini")
    crypto = c.ini_values(crypto_bytes, c.CRYPTO)
    if any(config["pak"][k] for k in ("index", "ini", "uasset", "all_assets")) and not c.valid_crypto_key(crypto_bytes):
        raise GuardError("Encryption requires an existing valid nonzero 32-byte AES key; no key changes are made.")
    if config["pak"]["signing"]:
        signing_shape(crypto)
    rules = config["assets"]
    if not isinstance(rules, list) or len(rules) > 1000:
        raise GuardError("Invalid resource classification list.")
    for rule in rules:
        shape(rule, {"path", "grade", "loading"})
        relative_name(rule["path"])
        if not rule["path"].startswith("Content/") or rule["grade"] not in {"A", "B", "C"} or rule["loading"] not in {"startup", "streamed", "dlc", "public"}:
            raise GuardError("Asset rules require Content/... paths, A/B/C grade and a documented loading mode.")
    return config


class UnrealAdapter(ProtectionAdapter):
    def detect(self, project, tool_path=None):
        # IoStoreUtilities is an official Developer module hosted by UnrealPak,
        # not a documented standalone IoStoreUtilities.exe. Detect entry points
        # independently; module help must confirm capabilities before execution.
        tools = {"pak": {"module": "PakFileUtilities", "prefix": []},
                 "iostore": {"module": "IoStoreUtilities", "prefix": ["IoStore"]}}
        record = {"status": "NOT_VERIFIED", "tools": tools, "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED"}
        def unavailable(reason):
            for entry in tools.values():
                entry.update(status="NOT_VERIFIED", reason=reason)
            record["reason"] = reason
            return record
        if tool_path is None:
            return unavailable("Provide the exact UE installation root; nothing is installed automatically.")
        root = safe_path(tool_path)
        version_file = root / "Engine/Build/Build.version"
        if not version_file.is_file():
            return unavailable("Engine/Build/Build.version is missing; neither tool version can be established.")
        try:
            data = read_json(version_file)
            numbers = [data[k] for k in ("MajorVersion", "MinorVersion", "PatchVersion")]
            if any(type(n) is not int or n < 0 for n in numbers):
                return unavailable("Invalid UE Build.version components.")
        except (GuardError, KeyError, TypeError):
            return unavailable("Cannot establish the tool version from Build.version.")
        version = ".".join(map(str, numbers))
        record.update(version=version, version_file_sha256=c.file_digest(version_file))
        if project.engine != "unreal" or not version_matches(project, version):
            return unavailable("UE installation version is unsupported or does not match the project; no tools executed.")
        tool = root / "Engine/Binaries/Win64/UnrealPak.exe"
        if not tool.is_file():
            return unavailable("Matching official UnrealPak host is missing. A DLL or undocumented IoStoreUtilities.exe is not a verified CLI substitute.")
        for entry in tools.values():
            entry.update(status="DETECTED", executable=str(safe_path(tool)), tool_sha256=c.file_digest(tool),
                         version=version, capability="NOT_VERIFIED")
        # Only the 5.6 keyword-list/CSV interface has been reviewed here.
        if numbers[:2] != [5, 6]:
            tools["iostore"].update(status="NOT_VERIFIED", reason="IoStoreUtilities command profile is reviewed for UE 5.6 only; this version needs its own documentation/help review.")
        record["status"] = "DETECTED"
        return record

    def plan(self, project, config):
        validate(project, config)
        # Reuse only baseline .gitignore. Compose each INI once to avoid duplicate targets.
        values = [(x.path, x.data, x.purpose) for x in c.plan(project) if x.path == ".gitignore"]
        pak, iostore = CONTAINERS[config["container_policy"]]
        game = c.patch_ini(c.read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultGame.ini"), c.PACKAGING,
                           {"BuildConfiguration": "PPBC_Shipping", "IncludeDebugFiles": "False", "UsePakFile": str(pak),
                            "bUseIoStore": str(iostore)})
        crypto = c.patch_ini(c.read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultCrypto.ini"), c.CRYPTO,
                             {FLAGS[k]: str(v) for k, v in config["pak"].items()})
        values += [("Config/DefaultGame.ini", game, "Shipping packaging; explicit " + config["container_policy"] + " choice"),
                   ("Config/DefaultCrypto.ini", crypto, "Independent CryptoKeys switches; preserve existing key bytes/values")]
        return changes(project, values)


def apply(project, config, **kwargs):
    return transactions.apply(project, planner=lambda: UnrealAdapter().plan(project, config), **kwargs)


def asset_plan(project, config):
    validate(project, config)
    rows = []
    content = c.local_path(project.root, "Content")
    for path in c.walk_files(content) if content.is_dir() else []:
        name = path.relative_to(project.root).as_posix()
        matched = [r for r in config["assets"] if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(name, r["path"])]
        if len(matched) != 1:
            rows.append({"path": name, "status": "REVIEW", "reason": "No rule" if not matched else "Ambiguous rules"})
            continue
        rule = matched[0]
        advice = {"A": "Use an explicitly reviewed chunk/container and existing key; verify actual assets and DLC dependencies.",
                  "B": "Measure loading/patch cost before selecting content encryption.",
                  "C": "Keep required public/direct-load content usable; encryption may offer little benefit."}[rule["grade"]]
        rows.append({"path": name, "grade": rule["grade"], "loading": rule["loading"], "status": "REVIEW", "advice": advice})
    return {"resources": rows, "status": "REVIEW", "scope": "Classification only. No chunks, secondary keys, AssetManager rules or content files are changed.",
            "all_asset_encryption": config["pak"]["all_assets"],
            "tradeoff": "All-asset encryption is a global switch, not per-grade protection. Benchmark startup, memory, loading, patch size and DLC."}


def verify_preview(project, engine_root, build, evidence_dir, crypto_json=None):
    install = UnrealAdapter().detect(project, engine_root)
    build = safe_path(build)
    disjoint(project.root, build)
    if not build.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Packaged build directory is missing.")
    files = list(c.walk_files(build))
    containers = [p for p in files if p.suffix.lower() in {".pak", ".utoc"}]
    if not containers:
        raise GuardError("No Pak or IoStore TOC found; configuration alone is not evidence.")
    for path in containers:
        if path.suffix.lower() == ".utoc" and not path.with_suffix(".ucas").is_file():
            raise GuardError("IoStore TOC has no corresponding .ucas; preserve the incomplete build.")
    if crypto_json:
        crypto_json = safe_path(crypto_json)
        if crypto_json.is_relative_to(build):
            raise GuardError("Crypto.json must remain outside the distributed build.")
        read_json(crypto_json)
    evidence = new_output(evidence_dir, project.root, build, Path(engine_root))
    selected = [p for p in files if p.suffix.lower() in {".pak", ".utoc", ".ucas", ".sig"}]
    return {"install": install, "build": str(build), "evidence": str(evidence),
            "files": [{"path": p.relative_to(build).as_posix(), "sha256": c.file_digest(p), "bytes": p.stat().st_size} for p in selected],
            "crypto_reference": str(crypto_json) if crypto_json else None,
            "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED", "protection": "NOT_VERIFIED",
            "scope": "Official read/test commands on actual containers; logs are private. No extraction, packaging or key changes."}


def _supports(help_text, flag, *, value=False):
    suffix = r"\s*=" if value else r"(?![A-Za-z0-9_-])"
    return re.search(r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_-])" + re.escape(flag) + suffix, help_text, re.IGNORECASE) is not None


def _verify_group(kind, entry, items, build, folder, crypto_json, secrets, operation):
    group = {"kind": kind, "module": entry["module"], "status": "NOT_VERIFIED", "checks": [],
             "entrypoint": entry, "protection": "NOT_VERIFIED", "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED"}
    if not items:
        group.update(status="NOT_REQUESTED", reason="No containers of this kind in the selected build.")
        return group
    if entry["status"] != "DETECTED":
        group["reason"] = entry["reason"]
        return group
    tool, prefix = entry["executable"], entry["prefix"]
    help_dir = folder / "help"
    help_dir.mkdir()
    try:
        group["help"] = private_run([tool, *prefix, "-Help"], help_dir, secrets=secrets, operation=operation)
    except Cancelled:
        raise
    except (GuardError, OSError):
        group.update(status="FAIL", reason="Tool help could not execute; preserve its private log.")
        return group
    if group["help"]["exit_code"] != 0:
        group["reason"] = "This entry point did not accept its help request; command compatibility is NOT_VERIFIED."
        return group
    help_text = (help_dir / "tool.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if kind == "iostore" and (not re.search(r"\bIoStore(?:Utilities)?\b", help_text, re.IGNORECASE)
                              or not _supports(help_text, "-List", value=True)
                              or not _supports(help_text, "-Csv", value=True)):
        group["reason"] = "IoStoreUtilities must independently advertise IoStore, -List=<container> and -Csv=<output>; Pak help cannot establish this interface."
        return group
    commands = ("-Test", "-List") if kind == "pak" else ("-List",)
    for item in items:
        path = c.local_path(build, item["path"])
        for command in commands:
            check = {"container": item["path"], "module": entry["module"], "command": command,
                     "status": "NOT_VERIFIED", "tool_sha256": entry["tool_sha256"]}
            group["checks"].append(check)
            if (kind == "pak" and not _supports(help_text, command)) or (crypto_json and not _supports(help_text, "-CryptoKeys", value=True)):
                check["reason"] = "Required argument is absent from this entry point's help; no container command executed."
                continue
            private = folder / str(len(group["checks"]))
            private.mkdir()
            listing = private / "contents.csv"
            argv = ([tool, str(path), command] if kind == "pak" else
                    [tool, *prefix, "-List=" + str(path), "-Csv=" + str(listing)])
            if crypto_json:
                argv.append("-CryptoKeys=" + str(safe_path(crypto_json)))
            if c.file_digest(path) != item["sha256"]:
                raise GuardError("Container changed before verification; evidence invalid.")
            try:
                execution = private_run(argv, private, secrets=secrets, operation=operation)
                check.update(execution, status="REVIEW" if execution["exit_code"] == 0 else "FAIL")
                if kind == "iostore" and execution["exit_code"] == 0:
                    listing = safe_path(listing)
                    if not listing.is_file() or not listing.stat().st_size:
                        check.update(status="NOT_VERIFIED", reason="IoStoreUtilities returned zero without the requested listing; no artifact result established.")
                    else:
                        check.update(listing=str(listing), listing_sha256=c.file_digest(listing))
            except Cancelled:
                raise
            except (GuardError, OSError):
                check.update(status="FAIL", reason="Container tool failed; preserve the private log and original container.")
            if c.file_digest(path) != item["sha256"]:
                raise GuardError("Container changed during verification; evidence invalid.")
    statuses = {row["status"] for row in group["checks"]}
    group["status"] = "FAIL" if "FAIL" in statuses else "NOT_VERIFIED" if "NOT_VERIFIED" in statuses else "REVIEW"
    group["scope"] = "This container type only. Exit zero/listing is not encryption or signature proof."
    return group


def verify(project, engine_root, build, evidence_dir, crypto_json=None, *, operation=None):
    with writer():
        preview = verify_preview(project, engine_root, build, evidence_dir, crypto_json)
        evidence = new_output(evidence_dir, project.root, build, engine_root, create=True)
        secrets = []
        if crypto_json:
            def collect(value):
                if isinstance(value, dict):
                    for v in value.values(): collect(v)
                elif isinstance(value, list):
                    for v in value: collect(v)
                elif isinstance(value, str) and len(value) >= 8:
                    secrets.append(value)
            collect(read_json(crypto_json))
        findings, checks, groups = [], [], {}
        # Store initial status before invoking even the help command.
        record = {**preview, "status": "INCOMPLETE", "checks": checks, "groups": groups}
        c.write_json(evidence / "verification.json", record)
        for kind, suffix in (("pak", ".pak"), ("iostore", ".utoc")):
            folder = evidence / kind
            folder.mkdir()
            c.write_json(folder / "verification.json", {"kind": kind, "status": "INCOMPLETE"})
            items = [x for x in preview["files"] if Path(x["path"]).suffix.lower() == suffix]
            group = _verify_group(kind, preview["install"]["tools"][kind], items,
                                  Path(preview["build"]), folder, crypto_json, secrets, operation)
            groups[kind] = group
            checks.extend(group["checks"])
            c.write_json(folder / "verification.json", group)
            if items:
                findings.append(finding(kind.upper() + "_OFFICIAL_CHECK", "artifact", group["status"],
                                        group.get("reason", group.get("scope", "Review this container type's private tool records.")),
                                        [x["path"] for x in items]))
        for item in preview["files"]:
            if c.file_digest(c.local_path(Path(preview["build"]), item["path"])) != item["sha256"]:
                raise GuardError("Container/partition/signature changed during verification.")
        if UnrealAdapter().detect(project, engine_root) != preview["install"]:
```
