```py
            raise GuardError("UE tool installation/version changed during verification; evidence invalid.")
        findings += [finding("PAK_ENCRYPTION_SIGNING", "artifact", "NOT_VERIFIED", "Review index/content flags and verify signing with trusted key in this UE version; a .sig file or settings alone is not proof."),
                     finding("IOSTORE_ENCRYPTION_SIGNING", "artifact", "NOT_VERIFIED", "Separate TOC/data encryption, signing and chunk integrity proof required; Pak index result is not transferable."),
                     finding("CONTAINER_RUNTIME", "runtime", "NOT_VERIFIED", "Test actual resource loads, DLC, patches, saves, offline startup and performance.")]
        statuses = {group["status"] for group in groups.values()}
        record.update(status="FAIL" if "FAIL" in statuses else "NOT_VERIFIED" if "NOT_VERIFIED" in statuses else "REVIEW", findings=findings)
        c.write_json(evidence / "verification.json", record)
        return record

```

`steam_release_guard/cli.py`:

```py
"""Local CLI: preview, explicit write consent and stable JSON envelopes."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__
from .core import (STATE, GuardError, apply, audit, atomic_write, build_script, detect_project,
                   plan, rollback, save_report, stage_release, validate_report_directory)
from .operations import Cancelled, Operation, safe_error, writer
from .staging import cleanup_preview, cleanup_stage, preview as stage_preview
from .transactions import rollback_preview
from . import project_backup


class Parser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    def error(self, message):
        raise GuardError("Invalid command or arguments. Run --help for the supported syntax.")


def parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    root = Parser(description="Local Steam release hardening for Unity 6 / UE5 Windows projects.")
    root.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=__version__)
    root.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit one structured JSON result; never prompts")
    sub = root.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("gui", help="Open the local desktop interface")
    for name in ("audit", "plan", "apply", "rollback", "build-script"):
        command = sub.add_parser(name)
        command.add_argument("project", type=Path, help="Project root or .uproject")
        if name in {"plan", "apply"}:
            command.add_argument("--encrypt-pak-index", action="store_true", help="UE only; requires an existing valid key")
        if name in {"audit", "apply"}:
            command.add_argument("--build", type=Path, help="Exact packaged depot folder, not the source project")
            command.add_argument("--report-dir", type=Path)
        if name == "apply":
            command.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, help="Full backup parent outside project; default sibling <project>.srg-backups")
        if name == "audit":
            command.add_argument("--fail-on-warnings", action="store_true")
        if name == "build-script":
            command.add_argument("--tool", type=Path, required=True, help="Exact Unity.exe or RunUAT.bat")
            command.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True, help="New build output directory")
            command.add_argument("--save", type=Path, help="Save a script; never execute it")
    stage = sub.add_parser("stage", help="Copy a build into disjoint release/private-symbol folders")
    stage.add_argument("build", type=Path)
    stage.add_argument("--release", required=True, type=Path)
    stage.add_argument("--symbols", required=True, type=Path)
    stage.add_argument("--project", type=Path, help="Optional project identity for the project lock")
    cleanup = sub.add_parser("stage-cleanup", help="Verify and remove only unchanged failed stage copies")
    cleanup.add_argument("journal", type=Path, help="Private srg-stage-journal.json")
    backup = sub.add_parser("backup", help="Copy and verify ALL project files outside the project")
    backup.add_argument("project", type=Path)
    backup.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, help="Parent for a new dated backup; default sibling <project>.srg-backups")
    verify = sub.add_parser("backup-verify", help="Verify full backup contents without changing the project")
    verify.add_argument("backup", type=Path, help="Dated backup folder containing srg-project-backup.json")
    restore = sub.add_parser("backup-restore", help="Restore full backup into a NEW directory; never overwrite")
    restore.add_argument("backup", type=Path)
    restore.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True, help="A new project directory that does not exist")
    from .advanced.cli import add_parser
    add_parser(sub)
    for name, command in sub.choices.items():
        command.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", default=argparse.SUPPRESS)
        command.add_argument("--progress", action="store_true", help="Write phase progress to stderr")
        if name in {"apply", "rollback", "build-script", "stage", "stage-cleanup", "backup", "backup-restore"}:
            command.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Validate and preview only; no writes")
            command.add_argument("--yes", action="store_true", help="Explicit consent to the reviewed write operation")
    return root


def plan_result(project, changes):
    return {"engine": project.engine, "version": project.version,
            "changes": [{"path": c.path, "purpose": c.purpose, "before_sha256": c.before_sha256} for c in changes]}


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    raw = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    json_mode = "--json" in raw
    command = None

    def emit(status, result=None, error=None, code=0):
        if json_mode:
            print(json.dumps({"schema": 1, "tool_version": __version__, "command": command,
                              "status": status, "result": result, "error": error}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        elif error:
            print("ERROR: " + error, file=sys.stderr)
        elif isinstance(result, str):
            print(result)
        else:
            print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return code

    def confirm(args, preview):
        if args.dry_run:
            return emit("PREVIEW", preview)
        if args.yes:
            return None
        if not json_mode and sys.stdin.isatty():
            print(json.dumps(preview, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            if input("Apply this reviewed operation? [y/N] ").strip().lower() == "y":
                return None
        return emit("CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED", preview, code=3)

    try:
        args = parser().parse_args(raw)
        command = args.command
        if command == "advanced":
            from .advanced.cli import run
            return run(args, confirm, emit, Operation())
        if command == "gui":
            if json_mode:
                raise GuardError("gui does not support JSON mode; use a CLI command.")
            from .gui import launch
            launch()
            return 0

        def progress(event):
            if args.progress:
                print(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False), file=sys.stderr)
        op = Operation(progress)
        if command == "backup-verify":
            return emit("VERIFIED", project_backup.verify(args.backup, operation=op))
        if command == "backup-restore":
            preview = project_backup.restore_preview(args.backup, args.output)
            decision = confirm(args, preview)
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            return emit("COMPLETE", project_backup.restore(args.backup, args.output, operation=op, expected_preview=preview))
        if command == "stage":
            project = detect_project(args.project) if args.project else None
            preview = stage_preview(args.build, args.release, args.symbols, operation=op)
            decision = confirm(args, preview)
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            result = stage_release(args.build, args.release, args.symbols, operation=op,
                                   project=project, expected_preview=preview)
            return emit("COMPLETE", result)
        if command == "stage-cleanup":
            journal, targets = cleanup_preview(args.journal)
            preview = {"status": journal["status"], "remove": [str(p) for p in targets],
                       "preserve": [journal["build"], str(args.journal)]}
            decision = confirm(args, preview)
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            return emit("COMPLETE", cleanup_stage(args.journal, operation=op, expected_integrity=journal["integrity_sha256"]))
        project = detect_project(args.project)
        if command == "backup":
            preview = project_backup.preview(project, args.backup_dir, operation=op)
            decision = confirm(args, preview)
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            return emit("COMPLETE", project_backup.create(project, args.backup_dir, operation=op, expected_preview=preview))
        if command == "plan":
            return emit("PREVIEW", plan_result(project, plan(project, args.encrypt_pak_index)))
        if command == "rollback":
            preview = rollback_preview(project)
            decision = confirm(args, preview)
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            return emit("COMPLETE", {"restored": str(rollback(project, operation=op, expected_preview=preview))})
        if command == "build-script":
            script = build_script(project, args.tool, args.output)
            if not args.save:
                return emit("PREVIEW", {"script": script} if json_mode else script)
            destination = args.save.expanduser().resolve()
            if destination.exists():
                raise GuardError("Build script already exists; choose a new --save path.")
            decision = confirm(args, {"save": str(destination), "script": script, "execute": False})
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
            with writer(project):
                # Exclusive creation avoids overwriting a file that appeared after preview.
                destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with destination.open("xb") as handle:
                    handle.write(script.encode("utf-8-sig"))
            return emit("COMPLETE", {"saved": str(destination), "executed": False})
        report_dir = args.report_dir or project.root / STATE / "reports" / "latest"
        validate_report_directory(report_dir)
        if args.build:
            audit(project, args.build, operation=op)  # Validate before any mutation, including consent prompts.
        backup = None
        changes = None
        full_preview = None
        if command == "apply":
            changes = plan(project, args.encrypt_pak_index)
            full_preview = project_backup.preview(project, args.backup_dir, operation=op) if changes else None
            decision = confirm(args, {**plan_result(project, changes), "full_backup": full_preview})
            if decision is not None:
                return decision
        # Keep apply and its resulting report in one serialized operation.
        with writer():
            if command == "apply":
                backup = apply(project, args.encrypt_pak_index, operation=op, expected_plan=changes,
                               backup_dir=args.backup_dir, expected_backup_preview=full_preview)
            op.step("auditing configuration and artifact", cancellable=False if backup else True)
            report = audit(project, args.build, operation=op if command == "audit" else None)
            op.step("writing local report", cancellable=False)
            path = save_report(report, report_dir)
        blocked = report["automated_gate"] == "failed"
        if getattr(args, "fail_on_warnings", False):
            blocked = blocked or any(f["status"] == "warn" for f in report["findings"])
        return emit("BLOCKED" if blocked else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW",
                    {"backup": str(backup) if backup else None,
                     "full_backup": json.loads(backup.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("full_backup") if backup else None,
                     "report_path": str(path), "report": report}, code=2 if blocked else 0)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, Cancelled) as error:
        return emit("CANCELLED", error=safe_error(error) if isinstance(error, Cancelled) else "Cancelled before completion; inspect recovery records.", code=130)
    except Exception as error:
        return emit("ERROR", error=safe_error(error), code=1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

`steam_release_guard/core.py`:

```py
"""Dependency-free project adapters, transactional changes and release inspection."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import html
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import stat
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__
from .operations import GuardError, Operation, serialized

STATE = ".steam-release-guard"
PACKAGING = "/Script/UnrealEd.ProjectPackagingSettings"
CRYPTO = "/Script/CryptoKeys.CryptoKeysSettings"
UNITY_ADAPTER = "Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/SteamReleaseGuardBuild.cs"
REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SYMBOL_EXTENSIONS = {".pdb", ".mdb", ".debug", ".sym"}
RAW_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".wav", ".ogg", ".mp3", ".flac", ".aiff", ".fbx", ".obj", ".blend", ".gltf",
    ".glb", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".tga", ".psd", ".exr", ".tif", ".dds",
}
SOURCE_EXTENSIONS = {".cs", ".cpp", ".c", ".h", ".hpp", ".py", ".lua"}
SOURCE_RULES = {
    "debug-menu": re.compile(r"\b(debugmenu|devmenu|debugconsole|devconsole)\b", re.I),
    "cheat-command": re.compile(r"\b(godmode|noclip|enablecheats|skip_level|levelskip|skipLevel)\b", re.I),
    "shipping-override": re.compile(r"\b(ALLOW_CONSOLE_IN_SHIPPING|bUseLoggingInShipping|bUseChecksInShipping)\b"),
    "unreal-exec": re.compile(r"UFUNCTION\s*\(\s*Exec\b|\bConsoleKeys\s*=", re.I),
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Project:
    root: Path
    engine: str
    version: str
    descriptor: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Change:
    path: str
    data: bytes
    purpose: str
    before_sha256: str | None = None


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def digest(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def file_digest(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(block)
    return h.hexdigest()


def linked(path: Path) -> bool:
    # Python 3.10 has no is_junction(); include Windows reparse points there too.
    try:
        info = path.lstat()
        return stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode) or bool(getattr(info, "st_file_attributes", 0) & 0x400)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return False


def local_path(root: Path, relative: str) -> Path:
    rel = Path(relative)
    if rel.is_absolute() or not rel.parts or any(p in {"..", "."} for p in rel.parts):
        raise GuardError(f"Unsafe relative path: {relative}")
    current = root
    for part in rel.parts:
        current = current / part
        if linked(current):
            raise GuardError(f"Refusing a symlink/junction: {relative}")
    if not current.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
        raise GuardError(f"Path escapes project: {relative}")
    return current


def absolute_path(value: str | Path) -> Path:
    path = Path(value).expanduser().resolve()
    if os.name == "nt" and not str(path).startswith("\\\\?\\"):
        text = str(path)
        path = Path("\\\\?\\UNC\\" + text[2:] if text.startswith("\\\\") else "\\\\?\\" + text)
    return path


def detect_project(value: str | Path) -> Project:
    candidate = absolute_path(value)
    chosen = candidate if candidate.suffix.lower() == ".uproject" and candidate.is_file() else None
    root = chosen.parent if chosen else candidate
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Select an existing project root or .uproject file.")
    unity = root / "ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt"
    unreal = [chosen] if chosen else list(root.glob("*.uproject"))
    if unity.is_file() and unreal:
        raise GuardError("Both engines found. Select a single engine project root.")
    if unity.is_file() and (root / "Assets").is_dir():
        match = re.search(r"m_EditorVersion:\s*(\S+)", unity.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        return Project(root, "unity", match.group(1) if match else "unknown", unity)
    if len(unreal) != 1:
        raise GuardError("Expected one Unity project or one .uproject; choose the .uproject explicitly if needed.")
    data = json.loads(unreal[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    return Project(root, "unreal", str(data.get("EngineAssociation", "unknown")), unreal[0])


def require_supported(project: Project) -> None:
    if project.engine == "unity" and not project.version.startswith("6000."):
        raise GuardError("This adapter targets Unity 6 (6000.x). Other versions are audit-only.")
    if project.engine == "unreal" and not re.match(r"^5\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?$", project.version):
        raise GuardError("This adapter targets UE 5.x. A custom/GUID engine association needs manual version review.")


def decode_config(data: bytes) -> tuple[str, str]:
    if data.startswith((b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff")):
        return data.decode("utf-16"), "utf-16"
    encoding = "utf-8-sig" if data.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf") else "utf-8"
    return data.decode(encoding), encoding


def ini_values(data: bytes, section: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    text, _ = decode_config(data)
    current = ""
    values = {}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("[") and stripped.endswith("]"):
            current = stripped[1:-1].casefold()
        elif current == section.casefold() and "=" in stripped and not stripped.startswith((";", "#")):
            key, value = stripped.split("=", 1)
            values[key.strip().casefold()] = value.strip()
    return values


def patch_ini(data: bytes, section: str, changes: dict[str, str]) -> bytes:
    """Preserve unrelated settings/comments/newlines, including repeated UE array entries."""
    text, encoding = decode_config(data)
    newline = "\r\n" if "\r\n" in text else "\n"
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    current = ""
    found_section = False
    seen = set()
    end_of_section = len(lines)
    replacements = {key.casefold(): (key, value) for key, value in changes.items()}
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("[") and stripped.endswith("]"):
            if current == section.casefold():
                end_of_section = i
            current = stripped[1:-1].casefold()
            if current == section.casefold():
                found_section = True
                end_of_section = len(lines)
        elif current == section.casefold() and "=" in stripped and not stripped.startswith((";", "#")):
            key = stripped.split("=", 1)[0].strip().casefold()
            if key in replacements:
                original, value = replacements[key]
                lines[i] = f"{original}={value}{newline}"
                seen.add(key)
    missing = [f"{key}={value}{newline}" for lower, (key, value) in replacements.items() if lower not in seen]
    if not found_section:
        if lines and not lines[-1].endswith(("\n", "\r")):
            lines[-1] += newline
        lines.extend([newline, f"[{section}]{newline}"] + missing)
    elif missing:
        if end_of_section and not lines[end_of_section - 1].endswith(("\n", "\r")):
            lines[end_of_section - 1] += newline
        lines[end_of_section:end_of_section] = missing
    return "".join(lines).encode(encoding)


def read_optional(root: Path, relative: str) -> bytes:
    path = local_path(root, relative)
    return path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else b""


def valid_crypto_key(data: bytes) -> bool:
    value = ini_values(data, CRYPTO).get("encryptionkey", "").strip('"')
    try:
        key = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)
        return len(key) == 32 and any(key)
    except ValueError:
        return False


def plan(project: Project, encrypt_index: bool = False) -> list[Change]:
    from .project_backup import restored_roots
    restored_roots(project.root)  # A partial full restore must not be adapted.
    require_supported(project)
    originals = {}

    def original(relative):
        if relative not in originals:
            path = local_path(project.root, relative)
            originals[relative] = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None
        return originals[relative] or b""

    changes = []
    ignore_data = original(".gitignore")
    ignore_text, ignore_encoding = decode_config(ignore_data)
    if f"/{STATE}/" not in ignore_text.splitlines() and f"{STATE}/" not in ignore_text.splitlines():
        newline = "\r\n" if "\r\n" in ignore_text else "\n"
        updated = ignore_text + (newline if ignore_text and not ignore_text.endswith("\n") else "")
        updated += f"# Local release reports, backups and logs; may contain private project data{newline}/{STATE}/{newline}"
        changes.append(Change(".gitignore", updated.encode(ignore_encoding), "Exclude private local state from Git"))
    if project.engine == "unity":
        if encrypt_index:
            raise GuardError("Pak-index encryption is an Unreal option, not a Unity asset encryption feature.")
        changes.append(Change(UNITY_ADAPTER, (REPO / "adapters/unity/SteamReleaseGuardBuild.cs").read_bytes(),
                              "Install the Unity 6 Windows IL2CPP release builder"))
        changes.append(Change("Assets/SteamReleaseGuard/Editor/SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.asmdef",
                              (REPO / "adapters/unity/SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.asmdef").read_bytes(),
                              "Keep the build adapter Editor-only"))
    else:
        game = "Config/DefaultGame.ini"
        changes.append(Change(game, patch_ini(original(game), PACKAGING, {
            "BuildConfiguration": "PPBC_Shipping", "IncludeDebugFiles": "False",
        }), "Set Shipping and exclude debug files from the staged build"))
        if encrypt_index:
            crypto_path = "Config/DefaultCrypto.ini"
            crypto = original(crypto_path)
            if not valid_crypto_key(crypto):
                raise GuardError("Generate a nonzero 256-bit key in Unreal Project Settings > Encryption first. No changes applied.")
            changes.append(Change(game, patch_ini(changes[-1].data, PACKAGING, {"UsePakFile": "True"}),
                                  "Use Pak packaging; preserve the existing IoStore setting"))
            changes.append(Change(crypto_path, patch_ini(crypto, CRYPTO, {"bEncryptPakIndex": "True"}),
                                  "Encrypt the Pak index using the existing key; not asset contents"))
    unique = {change.path: change for change in changes}
    result = []
    for change in unique.values():
        before = original(change.path)
        if before != change.data:
            saved = originals[change.path]
            result.append(Change(change.path, change.data, change.purpose, digest(saved) if saved is not None else None))
    return result


def atomic_write(path: Path, data: bytes) -> None:
    if any(linked(p) for p in (path, *path.parents)):
        raise GuardError("Write path must not contain symlinks or junctions.")
    path = absolute_path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".srg-" + uuid.uuid4().hex + ".tmp")
    try:
        with temporary.open("xb") as handle:
            handle.write(data)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if temporary.exists():
            temporary.unlink()


def write_json(path: Path, value: dict) -> None:
    atomic_write(path, (json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))


def apply(project: Project, encrypt_index: bool = False, **kwargs) -> Path | None:
    from .transactions import apply as execute
    return execute(project, encrypt_index, **kwargs)


def rollback(project: Project, **kwargs) -> Path:
    from .transactions import rollback as execute
    return execute(project, **kwargs)


def walk_files(root: Path, excluded: set[str] | None = None, operation: Operation | None = None):
    """Never follow symlinks/junctions or silently ignore unreadable directories."""
    excluded = {item.casefold() for item in (excluded or set())}

    def on_error(error):
        raise GuardError(f"Cannot fully inspect directory: {error.filename}")

    for base, directories, filenames in os.walk(root, followlinks=False, onerror=on_error):
        if operation:
            operation.check_cancel()
        directories.sort()
        filenames.sort()
        for directory in list(directories):
            if directory.casefold() in excluded:
                directories.remove(directory)
            elif linked(Path(base) / directory):
                raise GuardError("Inspection is incomplete: a directory is a symlink/junction.")
        for filename in filenames:
            if operation:
                operation.check_cancel()
            path = Path(base) / filename
            if linked(path):
                raise GuardError("Inspection is incomplete: a file is a symlink.")
            yield path


def private_symbol(relative: Path) -> bool:
    return relative.suffix.lower() in SYMBOL_EXTENSIONS or any(
        part.lower().endswith(".dsym") or "_backupthisfolder_butdontshipitwithyourgame" in part.lower()
        or "_burstdebuginformation_donotship" in part.lower() for part in relative.parts)


def source_candidates(project: Project, operation: Operation | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    roots = [project.root / "Assets"] if project.engine == "unity" else [project.root / p for p in ("Source", "Plugins", "Config")]
    results = []
    for root in roots:
        if not root.is_dir():
            continue
        for path in walk_files(root, {".git", STATE, "Library", "Intermediate", "Binaries", "Editor", "node_modules"}, operation):
            if path.suffix.lower() not in SOURCE_EXTENSIONS | {".ini"}:
                continue
            if path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
                results.append({"file": path.relative_to(project.root).as_posix(), "line": 0, "rule": "too-large-to-scan"})
                continue
            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig", errors="replace")
            for number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
                if operation and number % 100 == 0:
                    operation.check_cancel()
                for name, pattern in SOURCE_RULES.items():
                    if pattern.search(line):
                        # Do not put code, credentials or config values in reports.
                        results.append({"file": path.relative_to(project.root).as_posix(), "line": number, "rule": name})
    return results


@serialized
def audit(project: Project, build: Path | None = None, *, operation: Operation | None = None) -> dict:
    from .transactions import pending
    from .staging import MARKER
    findings = []
    op = operation or Operation()
    op.step("checking configuration", 0, 3)

    def add(rule, status, category, title, detail, evidence=None):
        findings.append({"id": rule, "status": status, "category": category, "title": title,
                         "detail": detail, "evidence": evidence or []})

    if pending(project):
        add("INCOMPLETE_PROJECT_TRANSACTION", "fail", "recovery", "Incomplete or damaged project transaction",
            "Preserve recovery records and resolve rollback before release.")
    from .project_backup import restored_roots
    try:
        restored_roots(project.root)
    except GuardError as error:
        add("INCOMPLETE_FULL_RESTORE", "fail", "recovery", "Full project restore is incomplete", str(error))

    try:
        require_supported(project)
    except GuardError as error:
        add("ENGINE_VERSION", "review", "configuration", "Engine version needs review", str(error))
    if project.engine == "unity":
        add("UNITY_ADAPTER", "pass" if (project.root / UNITY_ADAPTER).is_file() else "review", "configuration",
            "Unity release adapter", "Installing the adapter does not change PlayerSettings or prove a release build. Use its Windows release build command.")
        settings = read_optional(project.root, "ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset").decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        match = re.search(r"(?m)^  scriptingBackend:\s*\r?\n((?:[ \t]+[^\r\n]*\r?\n)*)", settings)
        backend = re.search(r"(?m)^\s+Standalone:\s*(\d+)\s*$", match.group(1)) if match else None
        add("UNITY_BACKEND_CONFIG", "pass" if backend and backend.group(1) == "1" else "review", "code",
            "Global Standalone backend setting", "The global YAML hint is not build evidence; custom Build Profiles can override it. The adapter selects IL2CPP at build time and restores the previous settings.")
        add("UNITY_ASSET_LIMIT", "review", "assets", "Unity assets remain extractable",
            "IL2CPP changes code compilation, not asset encryption. AssetBundles/Addressables and compression are not encryption. Review shipped StreamingAssets and third-party plugins.")
    else:
        game = ini_values(read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultGame.ini"), PACKAGING)
        shipping = game.get("buildconfiguration", "").casefold() == "ppbc_shipping"
        debug_off = game.get("includedebugfiles", "").casefold() in {"false", "0"}
        add("UE_SHIPPING", "pass" if shipping else "fail", "configuration", "Shipping packaging configuration",
            "DefaultGame.ini requests Shipping." if shipping else "Set BuildConfiguration=PPBC_Shipping before distribution.")
        add("UE_DEBUG_FILES", "pass" if debug_off else "review", "debug", "Exclude packaged debug files",
            "IncludeDebugFiles=False is explicit; retain private symbols." if debug_off else "Set IncludeDebugFiles=False explicitly and inspect the staged output.")
        crypto = read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultCrypto.ini")
        values = ini_values(crypto, CRYPTO)
        encrypt = values.get("bencryptpakindex", "").casefold() in {"true", "1"}
        valid_key = valid_crypto_key(crypto)
        add("UE_PAK_INDEX", "fail" if encrypt and not valid_key else "review", "assets", "Pak-index encryption policy",
            "Enabled without a valid nonzero 256-bit key." if encrypt and not valid_key else
            ("Requested with a valid-length key. Verify packaged Pak behavior; this does not encrypt asset contents." if encrypt else "Optional index encryption is not enabled. Decide based on compatibility and protection needs."))
        if values.get("bencryptallassetfiles", "").casefold() in {"true", "1"}:
            add("UE_ALL_ASSETS", "review", "assets", "Full asset encryption is enabled",
                "Benchmark loading, patch size, DLC and updates with this setting. The tool never enables it automatically.")
        if game.get("buseiostore", "").casefold() in {"true", "1"}:
            add("UE_IOSTORE_CONFIG", "review", "assets", "IoStore is enabled",
                "Pak-index policy alone cannot establish protection of .utoc/.ucas containers; verify with this engine's tools.")
        config = project.root / "Config"
        overrides = []
        if config.is_dir():
            for path in walk_files(config, operation=op):
                if path.suffix.lower() == ".ini" and path != config / "DefaultGame.ini":
                    values = ini_values(path.read_bytes(), PACKAGING)
                    if any(key in values for key in ("buildconfiguration", "includedebugfiles", "usepakfile", "buseiostore")):
                        overrides.append(path.relative_to(project.root).as_posix())
        if overrides:
            add("UE_CONFIG_LAYERS", "review", "configuration", "Other packaging config layers found",
                "Platform configuration, launch profiles and command-line flags can override defaults. Inspect the actual Shipping build.", overrides)
    op.step("inspecting debug candidates", 1, 3)
    candidates = source_candidates(project, op)
    if candidates:
        add("DEBUG_SOURCE_CANDIDATES", "warn", "debug", "Potential developer commands",
            "Heuristic matches only: comments and correctly guarded code can match. Binary plugins and Blueprints are not analyzed. Review each location and test the packaged game.", candidates)
    add("RUNTIME_DEBUG_REVIEW", "review", "debug", "Run the actual release build",
        "Try test menus, cheat consoles, level skipping and developer shortcuts. A clean text scan cannot establish their absence, including in Blueprints, scenes, prefabs and plugins.")
    inventory = {}
    op.step("inspecting packaged files", 2, 3)
    if build is None:
        add("BUILD_REQUIRED", "review", "artifact", "No packaged build supplied", "Select the exact Steam depot content folder for post-build inspection.")
    else:
        build = absolute_path(build)
        if not build.is_dir():
            raise GuardError("The packaged build folder does not exist.")
        if build == project.root or project.root.is_relative_to(build):
            raise GuardError("Select a packaged build folder, not the source project or its parent.")
        marker = build / MARKER
        if marker.exists():
            try:
                staged = json.loads(marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                complete = (staged.get("schema") == 2 and staged.get("operation") == "stage"
                            and staged.get("status") == "COMPLETE")
            except (ValueError, AttributeError):
                complete = False
            if not complete:
                add("INCOMPLETE_STAGE", "fail", "recovery", "Staging did not complete",
                    "Do not upload this directory. Preserve the private journal and use stage-cleanup or manual recovery.")
        files = list(walk_files(build, operation=op))
        relative = [path.relative_to(build) for path in files]
        names = [path.as_posix() for path in relative]
        inventory = {"file_count": len(files), "bytes": sum(path.stat().st_size for path in files)}
        exes = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.suffix.lower() == ".exe"]
        add("BUILD_EXECUTABLE", "pass" if exes else "fail", "artifact", "Windows executable inventory",
            "Executable presence is not proof the game starts or uses release flags.", exes)
        sensitive = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.name.lower() in {"steam_appid.txt", "defaultcrypto.ini", "crypto.json", ".env"}
                     or any(part.lower() in {".git", STATE} for part in p.parts)]
        if sensitive:
            add("PRIVATE_BUILD_FILES", "fail", "artifact", "Development/private files in the depot",
                "Remove these from the distribution staging folder after retaining any required private copies. No secret values are included in this report.", sensitive)
        symbols = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if private_symbol(p)]
        add("BUILD_SYMBOLS", "fail" if symbols else "pass", "debug", "Debug symbols and Unity debug folders",
            "Use stage to create separate release and private-symbol copies; never delete the originals." if symbols else "No known debug-symbol files or Unity do-not-ship folders were found.", symbols)
        raw = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.suffix.lower() in RAW_EXTENSIONS and not private_symbol(p)]
        if raw:
            add("LOOSE_ASSETS", "warn", "assets", "Directly accessible audio, models or textures",
                "These files can be copied directly. Some may be intentional (icons, mods, streaming audio); review rather than blindly remove or rename.", raw)
        source = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.suffix.lower() in SOURCE_EXTENSIONS and not private_symbol(p)]
        if source:
            add("SOURCE_IN_BUILD", "warn", "code", "Source/script files in the depot",
                "Review whether these are intentionally shipped scripts or accidental source. This tool does not obfuscate or rewrite them.", source)
        archives = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.suffix.lower() in {".pak", ".utoc", ".ucas", ".bundle", ".assets"}]
        add("ARCHIVE_REVIEW", "review", "assets", "Container extraction needs a separate test",
            "Archive names and extensions cannot prove encryption. Test your own build with version-matched engine tools; review index access and asset-content access separately.", archives)
        if any(p.suffix.lower() in {".utoc", ".ucas"} for p in relative):
            add("IOSTORE_ARTIFACT", "review", "assets", "IoStore containers detected",
                "No IoStore encryption verification is performed. A Pak index setting does not attest these containers.")
        if project.engine == "unity":
            managed = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if p.suffix.lower() == ".dll" and any(x.lower() == "managed" for x in p.parts)]
            native = any(p.name.lower() == "gameassembly.dll" for p in relative)
            metadata = any(p.name.lower() == "global-metadata.dat" for p in relative)
            add("UNITY_BUILD_CODE", "fail" if managed else ("pass" if native and metadata else "review"), "code",
                "Unity compiled-code indicators", "Managed DLLs remain readily inspectable." if managed else
                ("GameAssembly.dll and IL2CPP metadata are present. Native code and metadata can still be reverse engineered." if native and metadata else "Could not establish an IL2CPP layout. Check the actual build and adapter log."), managed)
            debug_boot = []
            for path in files:
                op.check_cancel()
                if path.name.lower() == "boot.config":
                    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig", errors="replace")
                    if re.search(r"(?mi)^\s*(player-connection-debug|wait-for-managed-debugger|profiler-enable|development)\s*=\s*(1|true)\s*$", text):
                        debug_boot.append(path.relative_to(build).as_posix())
            if debug_boot:
                add("UNITY_DEBUG_BOOT", "fail", "debug", "Player debugging/profiling flags found",
                    "Rebuild with development, debugging and profiling disabled.", debug_boot)
        else:
            nonshipping = [p.as_posix() for p in relative if re.search(r"-(debug|debuggame|development|test)(?:\.|-)", p.name, re.I)]
            if nonshipping:
                add("UE_NONSHIPPING_BINARY", "fail", "debug", "Non-Shipping binary name found",
                    "Check the build configuration and actual executable. Renaming a binary is not a fix.", nonshipping)
            add("UE_BUILD_PROOF", "review", "configuration", "Verify the packaged configuration",
                "File names and project settings are not proof of the executable's configuration. Retain the UAT log/receipt showing Shipping and test runtime console behavior.")
    add("STEAM_DRM", "review", "steam", "Optional Steam DRM wrapper",
        "Wrapping uploads the executable to Valve. Use the separate opt-in script only with your Steamworks authorization, then reinstall from a test branch. It cannot prevent piracy.")
    add("PLAYER_COMPATIBILITY", "review", "runtime", "Compatibility and paying-player checks",
        "Retest plugins, old/new saves, multiplayer, offline startup, Steam restart behavior, controllers, loading time and patch size using the distributed build.")
    failed = sum(f["status"] == "fail" for f in findings)
    op.step("audit completed", 3, 3)
    return {"schema": 1, "tool_version": __version__, "created_utc": utc_now(), "engine": project.engine,
            "engine_version": project.version, "project_name": project.root.name, "inventory": inventory,
            "automated_gate": "failed" if failed else "no_detected_blocker",
            "release_status": "BLOCKED" if failed else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW", "findings": findings}


def validate_report_directory(directory: Path) -> Path:
    directory = absolute_path(directory)
    if directory.exists():
        if not directory.is_dir():
            raise GuardError("Report output must be a directory.")
        marker = directory / ".srg-report-directory"
        if any(directory.iterdir()) and (not marker.is_file() or marker.read_bytes() != b"Steam Release Guard report v1\n"):
            raise GuardError("Report directory is not empty and was not created by this tool. Choose a new dedicated report directory.")
    return directory


@serialized
def save_report(report: dict, directory: Path) -> Path:
    directory = validate_report_directory(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    atomic_write(directory / ".srg-report-directory", b"Steam Release Guard report v1\n")
    atomic_write(directory / ".gitignore", b"*\n")
    write_json(directory / "report.json", report)
    cards = []
    for finding in report["findings"]:
        evidence = ""
        if finding["evidence"]:
            lines = [html.escape(item if isinstance(item, str) else json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False)) for item in finding["evidence"]]
            evidence = "<details><summary>Evidence / 证据位置 (" + str(len(lines)) + ")</summary><pre>" + "\n".join(lines) + "</pre></details>"
        cards.append(f'<article class="{finding["status"]}"><span>{finding["status"].upper()}</span>'
                     f'<h2>{html.escape(finding["title"])}</h2><p>{html.escape(finding["detail"])}</p>{evidence}</article>')
    page = '<!doctype html><html lang="en"><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
    page += '<title>Steam Release Guard report</title><style>body{font:16px/1.6 system-ui,sans-serif;background:#0e1420;color:#e7eef8;margin:0 auto;padding:40px 24px;max-width:1080px}h1{font-size:36px;margin-bottom:4px}header p{color:#abc0d6}article{background:#172235;border-left:4px solid #7194b5;border-radius:8px;padding:18px 24px;margin:16px 0}article.fail{border-color:#ff6868}article.warn{border-color:#eec05a}article.pass{border-color:#42cfa4}span{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:2px;color:#9dc6f0}h2{font-size:19px;margin:6px 0}p{margin:6px 0}pre{white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:13px}summary{cursor:pointer}footer{color:#abc0d6;margin-top:28px}</style><header><p>LOCAL RELEASE REVIEW / 本地发布检查</p><h1>Steam Release Guard</h1>'
    page += f'<p>{html.escape(report["project_name"])} · {html.escape(report["engine"])} {html.escape(report["engine_version"])}</p>'
    page += f'<h2>{html.escape(report["release_status"])}</h2><p>Configuration, artifact evidence and manual verification are separate. This is not a piracy-proof certificate.</p></header>'
    page += "".join(cards) + f'<footer>v{__version__} · {html.escape(report["created_utc"])} · No network requests; no source snippets or key values in this report.</footer></html>'
    atomic_write(directory / "report.html", page.encode("utf-8"))
    return directory / "report.html"


def stage_release(build: Path, destination: Path, symbols: Path, **kwargs) -> dict:
    from .staging import stage_release as execute
    return execute(build, destination, symbols, **kwargs)


def powershell_literal(value: str) -> str:
    # Extended paths are for our filesystem I/O, not external engine/batch arguments.
    value = value.replace("\\\\?\\UNC\\", "\\\\").replace("\\\\?\\", "")
    # Batch files ultimately use cmd.exe on Windows. Reject shell expansion characters there.
    if re.search(r'[\r\n"&|<>^%!]', value):
        raise GuardError("Build command paths must not contain shell metacharacters (including %, !, &, quotes or newlines).")
    return "'" + value.replace("'", "''") + "'"


def build_script(project: Project, tool: Path, output: Path) -> str:
    require_supported(project)
    tool, output = absolute_path(tool), absolute_path(output)
    if not tool.is_file():
        raise GuardError("Choose the installed Unity.exe or Unreal Engine/Build/BatchFiles/RunUAT.bat.")
    if output == project.root or project.root.is_relative_to(output):
        raise GuardError("Build output cannot be the project root or its parent.")
    if project.engine == "unity":
        if not (project.root / UNITY_ADAPTER).is_file():
            raise GuardError("Apply the Unity adapter before generating a build script.")
        if tool.name.lower() != "unity.exe":
            raise GuardError("Select Unity.exe for the project's exact Editor version.")
        args = ["-batchmode", "-quit", "-projectPath", str(project.root), "-buildTarget", "Win64",
                "-executeMethod", "SteamReleaseGuard.Editor.ReleaseBuilder.BuildWindows",
                "-srgOutput", str(output / "Game.exe"), "-logFile", str(project.root / STATE / "unity-build.log")]
    else:
        if tool.name.lower() != "runuat.bat":
            raise GuardError("Select this UE version's Engine/Build/BatchFiles/RunUAT.bat.")
        args = ["BuildCookRun", f"-project={project.descriptor}", "-noP4", "-platform=Win64",
                "-clientconfig=Shipping", "-serverconfig=Shipping", "-build", "-cook", "-stage", "-pak", "-package",
                "-archive", f"-archivedirectory={output}", "-nodebuginfo", "-utf8output"]
        game = ini_values(read_optional(project.root, "Config/DefaultGame.ini"), PACKAGING)
        if game.get("buseiostore", "").casefold() in {"true", "1"}:
            args.append("-iostore")
    command = "& " + powershell_literal(str(tool)) + " " + " ".join(powershell_literal(arg) for arg in args)
    out = powershell_literal(str(output))
    return ("# Generated release build command. Review prerequisites and close the Editor first.\n"
            "$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'\n"
            f"$srgOutput = {out}\n"
            "if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $srgOutput) -and (@(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $srgOutput -Force).Count -gt 0)) { throw 'Output must be empty; use a new version directory.' }\n"
            + command + "\nif ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw \"Engine build failed: $LASTEXITCODE\" }\n"
            "Write-Host 'Engine command completed. Inspect the log, stage private symbols, and audit the exact depot folder.'\n")

```

`steam_release_guard/gui.py`:

```py
"""Local Tk desktop UI. No web server, telemetry or external assets."""
from __future__ import annotations

import queue
import json
import threading
import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, ttk

from .core import (STATE, GuardError, apply, audit, atomic_write, build_script, detect_project,
                   plan, rollback, save_report, stage_release)
from .operations import Operation, safe_error, writer
from .transactions import rollback_preview
from . import project_backup


class App:
    def __init__(self, root: tk.Tk):
        self.root = root
        self.project = tk.StringVar()
        self.build = tk.StringVar()
        self.encrypt = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
        self.backup_dir = tk.StringVar()
        self.backup_note = tk.StringVar(value="完整备份：默认存到工程旁的 .srg-backups 目录；适配前自动复制并核验。")
        self.status = tk.StringVar(value="选择工程即可开始。先检查，也可以直接一键适配。")
        self.report_path = None
        self.events = queue.Queue()
        self.busy = False
        self.operation = None
        self.closing = False
        root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.close)
        root.title("Steam Release Guard · 发布加固助手")
        root.geometry("1050x800")
        root.minsize(920, 690)
        root.configure(bg="#0e1420")
        style = ttk.Style(root)
        style.theme_use("clam")
        style.configure("TFrame", background="#0e1420")
        style.configure("TLabel", background="#0e1420", foreground="#dce8f5", font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10))
        style.configure("Hint.TLabel", foreground="#9cb1cb", font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10))
        style.configure("Title.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 26, "bold"), foreground="#f2f7fc")
        style.configure("TButton", padding=(14, 9), font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10))
        style.configure("Accent.TButton", background="#43d6b5", foreground="#09231e")
        style.map("Accent.TButton", background=[("active", "#7ce8ce")])
        style.configure("TCheckbutton", background="#0e1420", foreground="#dce8f5", font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10))
        style.map("TCheckbutton", background=[("active", "#0e1420")])
        frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=20)
        frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        ttk.Label(frame, text="STEAM / WINDOWS / LOCAL", style="Hint.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
        ttk.Label(frame, text="Steam Release Guard", style="Title.TLabel").pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 8))
        ttk.Label(frame, text="把发布配置、调试入口和资源暴露分开检查。每次修改都有可核验备份。", style="Hint.TLabel").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 20))
        self.path_row(frame, "工程目录", self.project)
        self.path_row(frame, "发布包目录（可选）", self.build)
        ttk.Checkbutton(frame, text="Unreal：启用 Pak 索引加密（需已有有效密钥；不等于资源内容加密）", variable=self.encrypt).pack(anchor="w", pady=(10, 12))
        actions = ttk.Frame(frame)
        actions.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
        self.buttons = []
        for label, action, accent in [
            ("检查工程 / 发布包", lambda: self.run("audit"), False),
            ("一键适配", lambda: self.run("apply"), True),
            ("回滚最近一次适配", self.restore, False),
            ("打开检查报告", self.open_report, False),
        ]:
            button = ttk.Button(actions, text=label, command=action, style="Accent.TButton" if accent else "TButton")
            button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
            self.buttons.append(button)
        extra = ttk.Frame(frame)
        extra.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 14))
        for label, action in [("完整备份", self.make_backup), ("恢复完整备份…", self.restore_backup),
                              ("备份位置…", self.choose_backup_dir),
                              ("生成构建脚本…", self.make_build_script), ("分离调试符号…", self.stage)]:
            button = ttk.Button(extra, text=label, command=action)
            button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
            self.buttons.append(button)
        ttk.Label(frame, textvariable=self.backup_note, wraplength=980, style="Hint.TLabel").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
        ttk.Label(frame, textvariable=self.status, wraplength=940).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 10))
        progress_row = ttk.Frame(frame)
        progress_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 8))
        self.progress = ttk.Progressbar(progress_row, maximum=100)
        self.progress.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        self.cancel_button = ttk.Button(progress_row, text="取消", command=self.cancel, state="disabled")
        self.cancel_button.pack(side="right", padx=(8, 0))
        box = ttk.Frame(frame)
        box.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.log = tk.Text(box, bg="#172235", fg="#dce8f5", insertbackground="white", relief="flat", padx=16, pady=14,
                           font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10), wrap="word", height=15)
        scroll = ttk.Scrollbar(box, command=self.log.yview)
        self.log.configure(yscrollcommand=scroll.set)
        scroll.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        self.log.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.write("Unity 6：安装仅在 Editor 中使用的 IL2CPP / 非开发构建入口。\nUnreal 5：配置 Shipping，并排除发布包调试文件。\n\n适配不会自动构建、上传 Steam、生成密钥或声称无法盗版。\n构建完成后，选择实际发布包再次检查，并进行实机验收。")
        ttk.Label(frame, text="先关闭引擎和同步工具。完整备份在工程外；逐文件回滚记录仍在工程内 .steam-release-guard。", style="Hint.TLabel").pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 0))
        self.poll_id = root.after(100, self.poll)
        root.bind("<Destroy>", self.on_destroy, add="+")

    def on_destroy(self, event):
        if event.widget is self.root:
            self.root.after_cancel(self.poll_id)

    def path_row(self, parent, label, variable):
        ttk.Label(parent, text=label).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 4))
        row = ttk.Frame(parent)
        row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 4))
        ttk.Entry(row, textvariable=variable, font=("Microsoft YaHei UI", 10)).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, ipady=5)
        ttk.Button(row, text="选择目录", command=lambda: self.choose(variable)).pack(side="left", padx=(8, 0))

    def choose(self, variable):
        value = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择工程根目录" if variable is self.project else "选择实际发布包目录")
        if value:
            variable.set(value)

    def write(self, text):
        self.log.configure(state="normal")
        self.log.insert("end", text + "\n")
        self.log.see("end")
        self.log.configure(state="disabled")

    def choose_backup_dir(self):
        value = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择工程外的完整备份存放目录（可与工程不同磁盘）")
        if value:
            self.backup_dir.set(value)
            self.backup_note.set("完整备份存放目录：" + value)

    def make_backup(self):
        try:
            if not self.project.get().strip():
                raise GuardError("请先选择工程。")
            project = detect_project(self.project.get())
            destination = project_backup.backup_root(project, self.backup_dir.get() or None)
            if not messagebox.askyesno("完整备份工程", f"原工程：{project.root}\n备份存放目录：{destination}\n\n复制工程目录内全部文件，包括隐藏文件、缓存和资源；需要足够磁盘空间。\n请先关闭编辑器和同步工具。完成逐文件核验后才标记成功。继续？"):
                return

            def work():
                result = project_backup.create(project, destination, operation=self.operation)
                return ("完整备份已核验；原工程未修改。", f"已核验 {result['file_count']} 个文件。\n完整备份：{result['backup_path']}\n恢复时请选择新的工程目录。", None)
            self.start_worker(work)
        except Exception as error:
            messagebox.showerror("无法备份", safe_error(error))

    def restore_backup(self):
        try:
            source = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择带 srg-project-backup.json 的日期备份目录")
            if not source:
                return
            parent = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择恢复副本的存放目录（会创建新子目录，不覆盖原工程）")
            if not parent:
                return
            target = Path(parent) / ("RestoredProject-" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-%f"))
            preview = project_backup.restore_preview(source, target)
            if not messagebox.askyesno("恢复到新工程目录", f"完整备份：{source}\n新工程：{target}\n文件数：{preview['file_count']}\n\n先核验全部备份，再复制并核验恢复结果。原工程保留。继续？"):
                return

            def work():
                result = project_backup.restore(source, target, operation=self.operation, expected_preview=preview)
                return ("完整恢复已核验；请先打开恢复副本检查。", f"新工程目录：{result['output']}\n恢复记录：{result['receipt']}\n原工程和备份均保留。", None)
            self.start_worker(work)
        except Exception as error:
            messagebox.showerror("无法恢复完整备份", safe_error(error))

    def start_worker(self, work):
        if self.busy:
            return
        self.busy = True
        self.report_path = None
        self.progress["value"] = 0
        self.operation = Operation(lambda event: self.events.put(("progress", event)))
        self.cancel_button.configure(state="normal")
        self.status.set("正在本地处理…")
        for button in self.buttons:
            button.configure(state="disabled")

        def worker():
            try:
                result = work()
                self.events.put(("done", result))
            except BaseException as error:
                self.events.put(("error", safe_error(error)))

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=False).start()

    def cancel(self):
        if self.busy and self.operation and self.operation.cancellable:
            self.operation.cancel.set()
            self.status.set("已请求取消；等待当前校验完成，保留原文件和恢复记录。")

    def close(self):
        if not self.busy:
            self.root.destroy()
            return
        self.closing = True
        self.cancel()
        if self.operation and not self.operation.cancellable:
            self.status.set("当前正在提交，不能取消。完成恢复记录后关闭窗口。")

    def run(self, operation):
        project_text, build_text, encrypt = self.project.get().strip(), self.build.get().strip(), self.encrypt.get()
        backup_dir = self.backup_dir.get() or None
        if not project_text:
            messagebox.showinfo("选择工程", "请先选择 Unity 或 Unreal 的工程根目录。")
            return
        try:
            preview = None
            if operation == "apply":
                project = detect_project(project_text)
                preview = plan(project, encrypt)
                summary = "\n".join(f"• {c.path}: {c.purpose}" for c in preview) or "配置已存在；仅更新检查报告。"
                destination = project_backup.backup_root(project, backup_dir)
                backup_text = f"先完整备份工程内所有文件到：\n{destination}\n校验失败将停止适配。请先关闭编辑器和同步工具。" if preview else "工程无需修改，本次不重复创建完整备份。"
                if not messagebox.askyesno("确认适配范围", f"{project.engine} {project.version}\n{summary}\n\n{backup_text}\n项目写入阶段不可取消。继续？"):
                    return
        except Exception as error:
            messagebox.showerror("无法预览", safe_error(error))
            return

        def work():
            project = detect_project(project_text)
            build = Path(build_text) if build_text else None
            if build:
                audit(project, build, operation=self.operation)  # Reject invalid artifact input before mutation.
            description = []
            if operation == "apply":
                manifest = apply(project, encrypt, operation=self.operation, expected_plan=preview, backup_dir=backup_dir)
                description.append(f"适配已写入，恢复记录：{manifest}" if manifest else "适配已存在，无需重复修改。")
                if manifest:
                    full = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["full_backup"]
                    description.append(f"完整工程备份已核验：{full['backup_path']}")
            self.operation.step("检查工程和发布包", cancellable=operation != "apply")
            report = audit(project, build, operation=self.operation if operation == "audit" else None)
            self.operation.step("保存检查报告", cancellable=False)
            path = save_report(report, project.root / STATE / "reports" / datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-%f"))
            for finding in report["findings"]:
                description.append(f"[{finding['status'].upper()}] {finding['title']}\n  {finding['detail']}")
            return ("发现需要修复的发布问题。" if report["automated_gate"] == "failed" else "自动检查完成；仍需实机与 Steam 测试分支验收。", "\n".join(description), path)

        def locked_work():
            with writer():
                return work()
        self.start_worker(locked_work)

    def restore(self):
        value = self.project.get().strip()
        if not value:
            messagebox.showinfo("选择工程", "请先选择工程。")
            return
        try:
            project = detect_project(value)
            preview = rollback_preview(project)
            if messagebox.askyesno("恢复最近适配", "将恢复：\n" + "\n".join(preview["files"]) + "\n\n后续修改会阻止回滚。写入阶段不可取消。继续？"):
                self.start_worker(lambda: ("已回滚；重新检查以更新报告。", str(rollback(project, operation=self.operation, expected_preview=preview)), None))
        except Exception as error:
            messagebox.showerror("无法预览回滚", safe_error(error))

    def open_report(self):
        if self.report_path:
            webbrowser.open(self.report_path.as_uri())
        else:
            messagebox.showinfo("检查报告", "先执行一次检查或适配。")

    def make_build_script(self):
        try:
            if not self.project.get().strip():
                raise GuardError("请先选择工程。")
            project = detect_project(self.project.get())
            tool = filedialog.askopenfilename(title="选择准确版本的 Unity.exe / RunUAT.bat")
            if not tool:
                return
            output = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择新的空构建输出目录")
            if not output:
                return
            script = build_script(project, Path(tool), Path(output))
            target = project.root / STATE / ("build-" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-%f") + ".ps1")
            if not messagebox.askyesno("保存构建脚本", f"保存到：{target}\n引擎：{tool}\n输出：{output}\n只保存，不运行。继续？"):
                return
            with writer(project):
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with target.open("xb") as handle:
                    handle.write(script.encode("utf-8-sig"))
            self.write(f"构建脚本已生成（尚未运行）：{target}\n在 PowerShell 中运行该文件；引擎、许可证、Windows 构建工具需已就绪。")
        except Exception as error:
            messagebox.showerror("无法生成构建脚本", safe_error(error))

    def stage(self):
        value = self.build.get().strip()
        if not value:
            messagebox.showinfo("选择发布包", "先选择已经完成构建的发布包目录。")
            return
        release = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择空的发布目录（与原构建目录分开）")
        if not release:
            return
        symbols = filedialog.askdirectory(title="选择空的私有符号目录（不要上传 Steam）")
        if not symbols:
            return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("确认暂存范围", f"原构建：{value}\n发布副本：{release}\n私有符号：{symbols}\n\n复制全部构建文件，按符号规则分离；原文件保留，目标必须为空。继续？"):
            return

        def work():
            result = stage_release(Path(value), Path(release), Path(symbols), operation=self.operation)
            return ("复制校验完成；请对新的发布目录再次检查。", f"已复制并校验 {len(result['files'])} 个文件。原始文件保留。\n发布目录：{release}\n私有符号目录：{symbols}", None)

        self.start_worker(work)

    def poll(self):
        for _ in range(100):
            try:
                kind, value = self.events.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
            if kind == "progress":
                if value.get("backup_path"):
                    self.backup_note.set("已核验完整备份：" + value["backup_path"])
                    self.write(self.backup_note.get())
                self.progress["value"] = 100 * value["current"] / max(1, value["total"])
                self.status.set(f"{value['phase']} ({value['current']}/{value['total']})" + ("" if value["cancellable"] else " · 当前阶段不可取消"))
                self.cancel_button.configure(state="normal" if value["cancellable"] else "disabled")
                continue
            self.busy = False
            self.cancel_button.configure(state="disabled")
            for button in self.buttons:
                button.configure(state="normal")
            if kind == "error":
                self.status.set("操作未完成；请查看原因。")
                self.write("ERROR: " + value)
            else:
                status, text, report = value
                self.progress["value"] = 100
                self.status.set(status)
                self.write("\n" + text)
                self.report_path = report
        if self.closing and not self.busy:
            self.root.destroy()
            return
        self.poll_id = self.root.after(100, self.poll)


def launch():
    root = tk.Tk()
    App(root)
    root.mainloop()
```
