`steam_release_guard/operations.py`:

```py
"""Local cooperative locking, progress and safe error presentation (stdlib only)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import errno
import functools
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import tempfile
import threading
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path


class GuardError(Exception):
    """A safe, actionable message; never include configuration contents."""


class Cancelled(GuardError):
    pass


class Operation:
    def __init__(self, progress=None):
        self.cancel = threading.Event()
        self.progress = progress or (lambda event: None)
        self.cancellable = True

    def step(self, phase, current=0, total=1, *, cancellable=True):
        self.cancellable = cancellable
        self.check_cancel()
        self.progress({"phase": phase, "current": current, "total": total, "cancellable": cancellable})

    def check_cancel(self):
        if self.cancellable and self.cancel.is_set():
            raise Cancelled("Operation cancelled; originals preserved. Inspect any recovery journal before retrying.")


def safe_error(error):
    if isinstance(error, GuardError):
        return str(error)
    if isinstance(error, OSError):
        if error.errno == errno.ENOSPC:
            return "Insufficient disk space. Preserve recovery records, free space, then retry recovery."
        if isinstance(error, PermissionError):
            return "File access denied or file in use. Close the editor and check permissions before retrying."
        return "Filesystem operation failed. Check paths, free space and recovery records."
    if isinstance(error, (ValueError, UnicodeError)):
        return "Invalid data or encoding. Check the input and recovery record; no secret values are logged."
    return f"Unexpected {type(error).__name__}. Preserve recovery records; operation did not complete."


_held = threading.local()


@contextmanager
def _file_lock(path):
    from .core import linked
    if any(linked(p) for p in (path, *path.parents)):
        raise GuardError("Lock path must not contain symlinks or junctions.")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    handle = path.open("a+b")
    acquired = False
    try:
        handle.seek(0)
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(handle.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            acquired = True
        except OSError:
            raise GuardError("Another write task holds the operation lock. Wait for it to finish; do not delete lock files.") from None
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            if acquired:
                handle.seek(0)
                if os.name == "nt":
                    import msvcrt
                    msvcrt.locking(handle.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
                else:
                    import fcntl
                    fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
        finally:
            handle.close()


@contextmanager
def writer(project=None):
    """Serialize this host's writers, including legacy stage calls without a project.

    Keep lock files forever: unlinking a locked inode would allow a second lock.
    OS locks are released when the owning process exits. Thread-local reentrancy
    allows apply + report to share one outer operation, but not two GUI workers.
    """
    lock_root = Path(os.environ.get("SRG_LOCK_DIRECTORY", Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "steam-release-guard-locks"))
    paths = [lock_root / "writer.lock"]
    if project is not None:
        from .core import STATE, local_path
        paths.append(local_path(project.root, f"{STATE}/operation.lock"))

    @contextmanager
    def enter(index):
        if index == len(paths):
            yield
            return
        key = str(paths[index].absolute())
        held = getattr(_held, "paths", set())
        _held.paths = held
        if key in held:
            with enter(index + 1):
                yield
        else:
            with _file_lock(paths[index]):
                held.add(key)
                try:
                    with enter(index + 1):
                        yield
                finally:
                    held.remove(key)
    with enter(0):
        yield


def serialized(function):
    @functools.wraps(function)
    def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
        with writer():
            return function(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapped


def check_space(requirements):
    """Group estimates by filesystem; reserve metadata space. Still handle ENOSPC."""
    volumes = {}
    for destination, size in requirements:
        existing = Path(destination)
        while not existing.exists():
            existing = existing.parent
        device = existing.stat().st_dev
        previous, needed = volumes.get(device, (existing, 0))
        volumes[device] = (previous, needed + size)
    for path, needed in volumes.values():
        if shutil.disk_usage(path).free < needed + 2 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise GuardError("Insufficient disk space for verified copies and recovery records; no payload files changed.")


def seal(journal):
    payload = {k: v for k, v in journal.items() if k != "integrity_sha256"}
    journal["integrity_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return journal


def load_journal(path, operation, *, allow_legacy=False):
    try:
        journal = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if not isinstance(journal, dict) or not isinstance(journal.get("files"), list):
            raise ValueError
        if journal.get("schema") == 1 and allow_legacy:
            if any(key in journal for key in ("integrity_sha256", "operation", "file_count")):
                raise ValueError  # A damaged schema 2 record must not silently downgrade.
            return journal
        if journal.get("schema") != 2 or journal.get("operation") != operation:
            raise ValueError
        expected = journal.get("integrity_sha256")
        if seal(dict(journal))["integrity_sha256"] != expected:
            raise ValueError
        if type(journal.get("file_count")) is not int or journal["file_count"] != len(journal["files"]) or not journal["files"]:
            raise ValueError
        return journal
    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, UnicodeError):
        raise GuardError("Transaction journal integrity/schema check failed. Preserve it for manual recovery.") from None

```

`steam_release_guard/project_backup.py`:

```py
"""Verified full project files, outside the project; restore only to a new folder."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import stat
import uuid
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

from . import core as c
from .operations import Cancelled, GuardError, Operation, check_space, load_journal, safe_error, seal, writer

MANIFEST = "srg-project-backup.json"
PAYLOAD = "project"


def safe_path(value):
    # Inspect the spelling before resolve() can hide a linked ancestor.
    path = Path(value).expanduser().absolute()
    if any(c.linked(p) for p in (path, *path.parents)):
        raise GuardError("Full backup paths must not contain symlinks or junctions.")
    return c.absolute_path(path)


def disjoint(first, second):
    if first == second or first.is_relative_to(second) or second.is_relative_to(first):
        raise GuardError("Backup, original project and restored project must be in separate, non-nested directories.")


def backup_root(project, destination=None):
    source = safe_path(project.root)
    name = source.name if len(source.name) < 180 else source.name[:100] + "-" + c.digest(str(source).encode())[:8]
    root = safe_path(destination or source.parent / (name + ".srg-backups"))
    disjoint(source, root)
    if root.exists() and not root.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Choose a directory for full project backups.")
    return root


def relative_name(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or "\\" in value or ":" in value:
        raise GuardError("Invalid path in full project backup.")
    parts = value.split("/")
    if PurePosixPath(value).is_absolute() or any(p in {"", ".", ".."} for p in parts):
        raise GuardError("Invalid path in full project backup.")
    return value


def signature(info):
    # Windows stat infers executable bits from the filename; fstat does not.
    # Its ctime semantics can also differ. Compare stable fields there and rehash
    # contents on both sides; keep POSIX ctime/permissions as extra detectors.
    return (info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns if os.name != "nt" else 0,
            info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode & ~0o111 if os.name == "nt" else info.st_mode)


def inventory(root, op):
    root = safe_path(root)
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Full backup source directory is missing.")
    files, directories, seen = {}, [], set()
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        op.check_cancel()
        folder = pending.pop()
        safe_path(folder)
        with os.scandir(folder) as iterator:
            entries = sorted(iterator, key=lambda e: e.name)
        for item in entries:
            op.check_cancel()
            path = Path(item.path)
            name = relative_name(path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise GuardError("Case-colliding paths cannot be backed up portably.")
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            info = path.lstat()
            if stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode) or getattr(info, "st_file_attributes", 0) & 0x400:
                raise GuardError("Full backup refuses symlinks/junctions; no files will be silently omitted.")
            if stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):
                directories.append(name)
                pending.append(path)
            elif stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
                files[name] = signature(info)
            else:
                raise GuardError("Full backup requires regular files and directories; special files are unsupported.")
    return dict(sorted(files.items())), sorted(directories)


def preview(project, destination=None, *, operation=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    root = backup_root(project, destination)
    op.step("scanning full project backup")
    files, directories = inventory(project.root, op)
    return {"source": str(project.root), "backup_root": str(root), "file_count": len(files),
            "directory_count": len(directories), "total_bytes": sum(v[0] for v in files.values()),
            "scope": "All regular files, hidden files and empty directories inside the project; no exclusions.",
            "restore": "A new directory only; never overwrite the current project."}


def _copy(source, target, expected, op):
    safe_path(source)
    safe_path(target)
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with source.open("rb") as reader:
        if signature(os.fstat(reader.fileno())) != expected:
            raise GuardError("Project changed during full backup; close the editor and retry.")
        with target.open("xb") as output:
            while True:
                op.check_cancel()
                block = reader.read(1024 * 1024)
                if not block:
                    break
                output.write(block)
                digest.update(block)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        if signature(os.fstat(reader.fileno())) != expected or signature(source.stat()) != expected:
            raise GuardError("Project changed during full backup; close the editor and retry.")
    os.utime(target, ns=(expected[1], expected[1]))
    os.chmod(target, stat.S_IMODE(expected[5]))
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _hash(path, op):
    safe_path(path)
    result = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            op.check_cancel()
            result.update(block)
    return result.hexdigest()


def _record(path, journal, status):
    journal.update(schema=2, operation="project-backup", status=status,
                   file_count=len(journal["files"]), tool_version=c.__version__)
    c.write_json(path, seal(journal))


def _verify_payload(root, journal, op):
    files, directories = inventory(root, op)
    if set(files) != {f["path"] for f in journal["files"]} or directories != sorted(journal["directories"]):
        raise GuardError("Full backup inventory mismatch: missing or unexpected files/directories.")
    for index, entry in enumerate(journal["files"]):
        op.step("verifying full project files", index, len(files))
        if files[entry["path"]][0] != entry["size"] or _hash(c.local_path(root, entry["path"]), op) != entry["sha256"]:
            raise GuardError("Full backup file integrity check failed. Nothing will be restored or adapted.")


def create(project, destination=None, *, operation=None, expected_preview=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer():
        current = preview(project, destination, operation=op)
        if expected_preview is not None and current != expected_preview:
            raise GuardError("Full backup scope changed after preview; review it again.")
        files, directories = inventory(project.root, op)
        root = backup_root(project, destination)
        identities = list(dict.fromkeys([str(project.root), *restored_roots(project.root)]))
        check_space([(root, sum(v[0] for v in files.values()) + len(files) * 1024)])
        stamp = c.datetime.now(c.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ") + "-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
        snapshot = root / stamp
        snapshot.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        manifest = snapshot / MANIFEST
        payload = snapshot / PAYLOAD
        journal = {"source": str(project.root), "engine": project.engine, "engine_version": project.version,
                   "project_roots": identities,
                   "created_utc": c.utc_now(), "directories": directories,
                   "files": [{"path": name, "size": info[0], "mtime_ns": info[1],
                              "mode": stat.S_IMODE(info[5]), "sha256": None} for name, info in files.items()]}
        try:
            _record(manifest, journal, "INCOMPLETE")
            payload.mkdir()
            for name in directories:
                c.local_path(payload, name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            for index, entry in enumerate(journal["files"]):
                op.step("copying full project backup", index, len(files))
                name = entry["path"]
                entry["sha256"] = _copy(c.local_path(project.root, name), c.local_path(payload, name), files[name], op)
            # Re-read both source and copy: a successful write alone is insufficient.
            _verify_payload(payload, journal, op)
            _verify_payload(project.root, journal, op)
            if inventory(project.root, op) != (files, directories):
                raise GuardError("Project changed during full backup verification; close the editor and retry.")
            op.step("committing verified full backup", len(files), len(files))
            _record(manifest, journal, "COMPLETE")
            op.progress({"phase": "full project backup verified", "current": len(files), "total": len(files),
                         "cancellable": True, "backup_path": str(snapshot)})
        except BaseException as error:
            try:
                _record(manifest, journal, "INCOMPLETE")
            except (OSError, GuardError):
                pass
            if isinstance(error, (Cancelled, KeyboardInterrupt)):
                raise Cancelled(f"Full backup cancelled; project untouched. Incomplete backup: {snapshot}") from None
            raise GuardError(f"Full backup failed ({safe_error(error)}). Project not adapted. Incomplete backup: {snapshot}") from None
        finally:
            op.cancellable = True
        return {**current, "status": "COMPLETE", "backup_path": str(snapshot), "manifest": str(manifest)}


def _load(value):
    snapshot = safe_path(value)
    manifest = snapshot / MANIFEST
    if not manifest.is_file():
        raise GuardError("Select a full backup directory containing srg-project-backup.json.")
    safe_path(manifest)
    journal = load_journal(manifest, "project-backup")
    if journal.get("status") != "COMPLETE":
        raise GuardError("Full backup is INCOMPLETE; it cannot be used for restoration.")
    if not isinstance(journal.get("source"), str) or not isinstance(journal.get("directories"), list):
        raise GuardError("Invalid full backup manifest.")
    roots = journal.get("project_roots", [journal["source"]])
    if not isinstance(roots, list) or not roots or any(not isinstance(p, str) or not Path(p).is_absolute() for p in roots):
        raise GuardError("Invalid full backup project identity.")
    seen, directories = set(), set()
    for name in journal["directories"]:
        key = relative_name(name).casefold()
        if key in seen:
            raise GuardError("Duplicate full backup path.")
        seen.add(key)
        directories.add(name)
    for entry in journal["files"]:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise GuardError("Invalid full backup file record.")
        name = relative_name(entry.get("path"))
        if name.casefold() in seen:
            raise GuardError("Duplicate full backup path.")
        seen.add(name.casefold())
        if (type(entry.get("size")) is not int or entry["size"] < 0
                or not isinstance(entry.get("sha256"), str) or not re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{64}", entry["sha256"])):
            raise GuardError("Invalid full backup file record.")
    for name in [*directories, *(f["path"] for f in journal["files"])]:
        if any(str(parent) not in directories for parent in PurePosixPath(name).parents if str(parent) != "."):
            raise GuardError("Missing parent directory in full backup manifest.")
    return snapshot, journal


def verify(value, *, operation=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    snapshot, journal = _load(value)
    _verify_payload(snapshot / PAYLOAD, journal, op)
    return {"status": "VERIFIED", "backup_path": str(snapshot), "file_count": len(journal["files"]),
            "directory_count": len(journal["directories"]), "total_bytes": sum(f["size"] for f in journal["files"])}


def restore_preview(value, output):
    snapshot, journal = _load(value)
    target = safe_path(output)
    disjoint(snapshot, target)
    # Original media need not be mounted to restore a verified independent copy.
    disjoint(Path(journal["source"]), target)
    if target.exists() or target.with_name(target.name + ".srg-restore.json").exists():
        raise GuardError("Full restore requires a NEW directory; existing directories will never be overwritten.")
    return {"backup_path": str(snapshot), "output": str(target), "file_count": len(journal["files"]),
            "total_bytes": sum(f["size"] for f in journal["files"]), "integrity_sha256": journal["integrity_sha256"]}


def restore(value, output, *, operation=None, expected_preview=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer():
        previewed = restore_preview(value, output)
        if expected_preview is not None and previewed != expected_preview:
            raise GuardError("Full restore scope changed after preview.")
        snapshot, journal = _load(value)
        _verify_payload(snapshot / PAYLOAD, journal, op)  # Verify everything before creating the destination.
        target = safe_path(output)
        check_space([(target, previewed["total_bytes"] + len(journal["files"]) * 1024)])
        receipt = target.with_name(target.name + ".srg-restore.json")
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        record = {**previewed, "status": "INCOMPLETE"}
        # Never replace a receipt created by somebody else between preview and commit.
        with receipt.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            handle.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        try:
            target.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
            source = snapshot / PAYLOAD
            files, directories = inventory(source, op)
            for name in directories:
                c.local_path(target, name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            for index, entry in enumerate(journal["files"]):
                op.step("restoring full project to new directory", index, len(files))
                name = entry["path"]
                checksum = _copy(c.local_path(source, name), c.local_path(target, name), files[name], op)
                if checksum != entry["sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Backup changed during restore. Keep the partial output for inspection.")
            _verify_payload(target, journal, op)
            op.step("committing verified restoration", len(files), len(files), cancellable=False)
            record["status"] = "COMPLETE"
            c.write_json(receipt, record)
        except BaseException as error:
            if isinstance(error, (Cancelled, KeyboardInterrupt)):
                raise Cancelled(f"Full restore cancelled; original preserved. Incomplete output: {target}") from None
            raise GuardError(f"Full restore incomplete ({safe_error(error)}). Original preserved; partial output: {target}") from None
        finally:
            op.cancellable = True
        return {**record, "receipt": str(receipt)}


def restored_roots(root):
    """Recognize transaction histories carried by a verified whole-project restore."""
    root = c.absolute_path(root)
    receipt = root.with_name(root.name + ".srg-restore.json")
    if not receipt.exists():
        return []
    try:
        safe_path(receipt)
        record = json.loads(receipt.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if record["status"] != "COMPLETE" or c.absolute_path(record["output"]) != root:
            raise ValueError
        _, journal = _load(record["backup_path"])
        if record["integrity_sha256"] != journal["integrity_sha256"]:
            raise ValueError
        return journal.get("project_roots", [journal["source"]])
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, GuardError):
        raise GuardError("Full project restoration is incomplete or its receipt/backup is invalid. Preserve it and restore to another new directory.") from None

```

`steam_release_guard/staging.py`:

```py
"""Independent copy transaction and conservative cleanup of incomplete outputs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from . import core as c
from .operations import GuardError, Operation, check_space, load_journal, seal, writer

MARKER = ".srg-stage.json"
JOURNAL = "srg-stage-journal.json"
SYMBOL_MANIFEST = "srg-symbol-manifest.json"


def _root(path):
    path = Path(path).expanduser().absolute()
    if any(c.linked(p) for p in (path, *path.parents)):
        raise GuardError("Staging paths must not contain symlinks or junctions.")
    return c.absolute_path(path)


def _disjoint(paths):
    for i, path in enumerate(paths):
        for other in paths[i + 1:]:
            if path == other or path.is_relative_to(other) or other.is_relative_to(path):
                raise GuardError("Build, release and private-symbol folders must be disjoint, not nested.")


def preview(build, destination, symbols, *, operation=None):
    build, destination, symbols = map(_root, (build, destination, symbols))
    _disjoint([build, destination, symbols])
    if not build.is_dir():
        raise GuardError("Build folder does not exist.")
    for folder in (destination, symbols):
        if folder.exists() and (not folder.is_dir() or any(folder.iterdir())):
            raise GuardError("Release and private-symbol folders must be new or empty. Existing builds are never overwritten.")
    op = operation or Operation()
    paths = list(c.walk_files(build))
    if not paths:
        raise GuardError("Cannot stage an empty build.")
    files = []
    for number, path in enumerate(paths):
        op.step("hashing source build", number, len(paths))
        relative = path.relative_to(build)
        if any(part.casefold() in {".git", c.STATE, MARKER, JOURNAL, SYMBOL_MANIFEST}
               or ".srg-part-" in part.casefold() for part in relative.parts):
            raise GuardError("Build contains source-control/private-state/staging data; select only original packaged content.")
        files.append({"path": relative.as_posix(), "sha256": c.file_digest(path), "bytes": path.stat().st_size,
                      "location": "private_symbols" if c.private_symbol(relative) else "release"})
    return {"build": str(build), "release": str(destination), "symbols": str(symbols), "files": files,
            "file_count": len(files), "bytes": sum(f["bytes"] for f in files)}


def _marker(journal, status):
    return {"schema": 2, "operation": "stage", "transaction": journal["transaction"],
            "status": status, "file_count": journal["file_count"], "release_approved": False}


def _part(target, journal):
    # Keep valid long source filenames below the filesystem's per-component limit.
    suffix = c.digest(target.name.encode("utf-8"))[:24]
    return target.with_name(".srg-part-" + journal["transaction"] + "-" + suffix)


def _copy(source, partial, operation, number, total):
    with source.open("rb") as reader, partial.open("xb") as output:
        while True:
            operation.step("copying verified files", number, total)
            block = reader.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not block:
                break
            output.write(block)
        output.flush()
        os.fsync(output.fileno())


def _new_json(path, data):
    with path.open("xb") as handle:
        handle.write((json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
        handle.flush()
        os.fsync(handle.fileno())


def stage_release(build, destination, symbols, *, operation=None, project=None, expected_preview=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer(project):
        planned = preview(build, destination, symbols, operation=op)
        if expected_preview is not None and planned != expected_preview:
            raise GuardError("Build changed after preview; review again before staging.")
        build, destination, symbols = (Path(planned[k]) for k in ("build", "release", "symbols"))
        check_space([(destination, sum(f["bytes"] for f in planned["files"] if f["location"] == "release")),
                     (symbols, sum(f["bytes"] for f in planned["files"] if f["location"] == "private_symbols"))])
        journal = {**planned, "schema": 2, "operation": "stage", "tool_version": c.__version__,
                   "transaction": uuid.uuid4().hex, "created_utc": c.utc_now(), "status": "INCOMPLETE",
                   "originals_untouched": True}
        manifest = symbols / JOURNAL
        # The private record and public INCOMPLETE marker precede every payload copy.
        for folder in (destination, symbols):
            folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # Exclusive creation outside recovery handling: never overwrite another owner's record.
        _new_json(manifest, seal(journal))
        try:
            _new_json(destination / MARKER, _marker(journal, "INCOMPLETE"))
            for number, entry in enumerate(journal["files"]):
                source = c.local_path(build, entry["path"])
                target = c.local_path(symbols if entry["location"] == "private_symbols" else destination, entry["path"])
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                partial = _part(target, journal)
                if target.exists() or partial.exists():
                    raise GuardError("An output file appeared during staging; no existing file will be overwritten.")
                if c.file_digest(source) != entry["sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Source build changed after staging preflight.")
                _copy(source, partial, op, number, journal["file_count"])
                if c.file_digest(partial) != entry["sha256"] or c.file_digest(source) != entry["sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Source/copy SHA256 mismatch.")
                # Hard-link commit fails if target appeared, including on POSIX where rename replaces.
                # Both names are on the same filesystem; no copy is silently overwritten.
                os.link(partial, target)
                partial.unlink()
            op.step("verifying staged inventory", 0, journal["file_count"])
            expected_sources = {f["path"] for f in journal["files"]}
            if {p.relative_to(build).as_posix() for p in c.walk_files(build)} != expected_sources:
                raise GuardError("Source inventory changed while staging.")
            for entry in journal["files"]:
                target = c.local_path(symbols if entry["location"] == "private_symbols" else destination, entry["path"])
                if c.file_digest(target) != entry["sha256"] or c.file_digest(c.local_path(build, entry["path"])) != entry["sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Final staging verification failed.")
            expected_outputs = {manifest, destination / MARKER} | {
                c.local_path(symbols if f["location"] == "private_symbols" else destination, f["path"])
                for f in journal["files"]}
            if set(c.walk_files(destination)) | set(c.walk_files(symbols)) != expected_outputs:
                raise GuardError("Unexpected files appeared in stage output; preserve them for manual review.")
            op.step("ready to finalize stage", journal["file_count"], journal["file_count"])
            # From here cancellation is disabled: finish both records before presenting completion.
            op.step("finalizing stage (cannot cancel)", journal["file_count"], journal["file_count"], cancellable=False)
            _new_json(symbols / SYMBOL_MANIFEST, {"schema": 2, "created_utc": journal["created_utc"],
                                                   "files": journal["files"], "originals_untouched": True})
            journal["status"] = "COMPLETE"
            c.write_json(manifest, seal(journal))
            c.write_json(destination / MARKER, _marker(journal, "COMPLETE"))
        except BaseException as error:
            # Initial marker remains INCOMPLETE even if ENOSPC prevents recording the error.
            journal["status"] = "INCOMPLETE"
            try:
                c.write_json(manifest, seal(journal))
            except (OSError, GuardError):
                pass
            from .operations import Cancelled
            kind = Cancelled if isinstance(error, (Cancelled, KeyboardInterrupt)) else GuardError
            raise kind(f"Stage INCOMPLETE ({type(error).__name__}). Original build preserved. Use stage-cleanup with the private journal: {manifest}") from None
        return journal


def _is_prefix(partial, source, sha256):
    if c.file_digest(source) != sha256 or partial.stat().st_size > source.stat().st_size:
        return False
    with partial.open("rb") as left, source.open("rb") as right:
        while True:
            block = left.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not block:
                return True
            if block != right.read(len(block)):
                return False


def cleanup_preview(manifest):
    manifest = _root(manifest)
    journal = load_journal(manifest, "stage")
    try:
        build, release, symbols = (_root(journal[k]) for k in ("build", "release", "symbols"))
        _disjoint([build, release, symbols])
        if manifest != symbols / JOURNAL or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", journal["transaction"]):
            raise ValueError
        if journal["status"] not in {"INCOMPLETE", "COMPLETE", "CLEANING", "CLEANED"}:
            raise ValueError
        marker_path = release / MARKER
        if marker_path.exists():
            marker = json.loads(marker_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if marker == _marker(journal, "COMPLETE"):
                raise GuardError("A completed release cannot be cleaned by stage-cleanup.")
            if marker != _marker(journal, "INCOMPLETE"):
                raise ValueError
        elif journal["status"] == "COMPLETE":
            raise GuardError("Missing completion marker; preserve this ambiguous output for manual inspection.")
        targets, allowed = [], {manifest, marker_path, symbols / SYMBOL_MANIFEST}
        seen = set()
        for entry in journal["files"]:
            if entry["location"] not in {"private_symbols", "release"} or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", entry["sha256"]):
                raise ValueError
            target = c.local_path(symbols if entry["location"] == "private_symbols" else release, entry["path"])
            if str(target).casefold() in seen or target in allowed:
                raise ValueError
            seen.add(str(target).casefold())
            partial = _part(target, journal)
            allowed.update((target, partial))
            if target.exists():
                if c.file_digest(target) != entry["sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Cleanup conflict: a copied file was modified. No files removed.")
                targets.append(target)
            if partial.exists():
                if not _is_prefix(partial, c.local_path(build, entry["path"]), entry["sha256"]):
                    raise GuardError("Cleanup cannot verify a partial copy. No files removed.")
                targets.append(partial)
        if (symbols / SYMBOL_MANIFEST).exists():
            saved = json.loads((symbols / SYMBOL_MANIFEST).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if saved != {"schema": 2, "created_utc": journal["created_utc"], "files": journal["files"], "originals_untouched": True}:
                raise GuardError("Private symbol manifest changed. No files removed.")
            targets.append(symbols / SYMBOL_MANIFEST)
        for folder in (release, symbols):
            if folder.exists() and any(path not in allowed for path in c.walk_files(folder)):
                raise GuardError("Cleanup found unrecognized output files. Preserve them; no files removed.")
        return journal, targets
    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, UnicodeError):
        raise GuardError("Invalid stage recovery record/marker; no files removed.") from None


def cleanup_stage(manifest, *, operation=None, expected_integrity=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer():
        op.step("verifying cleanup")
        journal, targets = cleanup_preview(manifest)
        if expected_integrity is not None and journal["integrity_sha256"] != expected_integrity:
            raise GuardError("Stage journal changed after preview. Review cleanup again.")
        if journal["status"] == "CLEANED":
            return {"status": "CLEANED", "removed": 0, "journal": str(manifest)}
        fingerprints = {path: c.file_digest(path) for path in targets}
        op.step("ready to clean failed copies", 0, len(targets))
        journal["status"] = "CLEANING"
        c.write_json(Path(manifest), seal(journal))
        # Keep the INCOMPLETE marker until all copied payload is removed, including after a crash.
        for number, path in enumerate(targets):
            op.step("cleaning failed copies (cannot cancel)", number, len(targets), cancellable=False)
            if c.linked(path) or c.file_digest(path) != fingerprints[path]:
                raise GuardError("Cleanup conflict during removal; remaining copies and journal preserved.")
            path.unlink()
        release = Path(journal["release"])
        # Never recursively delete directories; remove only empty parents of our known payload paths.
        directories = set()
        for path in targets:
            stop = Path(journal["symbols"]) if path.is_relative_to(Path(journal["symbols"])) else release
            parent = path.parent
            while parent != stop:
                directories.add(parent)
                parent = parent.parent
        for directory in sorted(directories, key=lambda p: len(p.parts), reverse=True):
            if directory.exists() and not any(directory.iterdir()):
                directory.rmdir()
        marker = release / MARKER
        remaining = set(c.walk_files(release)) | set(c.walk_files(Path(journal["symbols"])))
        if remaining - {c.absolute_path(manifest), marker}:
            raise GuardError("New output files appeared during cleanup; preserved for manual review.")
        if marker.exists():
            if json.loads(marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) != _marker(journal, "INCOMPLETE"):
                raise GuardError("Stage marker changed during cleanup; preserved for manual inspection.")
            marker.unlink()
        journal["status"] = "CLEANED"
        c.write_json(Path(manifest), seal(journal))
        return {"status": "CLEANED", "removed": len(targets), "journal": str(manifest)}

```

`steam_release_guard/transactions.py`:

```py
"""Project transactions. Legacy schema 1 recovery remains supported."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from . import core as c
from .operations import GuardError, Operation, check_space, load_journal, seal, writer

STATES = {"prepared", "applying", "applied", "apply_incomplete", "rolling_back", "rollback_incomplete", "rolled_back"}


def record(path, journal, status):
    journal.update(schema=2, operation="apply", file_count=len(journal["files"]), status=status,
                   tool_version=c.__version__)
    c.write_json(path, seal(journal))


def read_manifest(project, manifest):
    from .project_backup import restored_roots
    journal = load_journal(manifest, "apply", allow_legacy=True)
    allowed_roots = {project.root, *(c.absolute_path(p) for p in restored_roots(project.root))}
    if not isinstance(journal.get("root"), str) or c.absolute_path(journal["root"]) not in allowed_roots or journal.get("status") not in STATES:
        raise GuardError("Backup belongs to a different project or has an invalid status. No files restored.")
    seen = set()
    if not journal["files"]:
        raise GuardError("Empty recovery journal. No files restored.")
    for number, entry in enumerate(journal["files"]):
        if (not isinstance(entry, dict) or not isinstance(entry.get("path"), str)
                or not {"path", "before_sha256", "after_sha256", "backup"}.issubset(entry)):
            raise GuardError("Invalid recovery entry. No files restored.")
        target = c.local_path(project.root, entry["path"])
        key = str(target).casefold()
        if key in seen or target.is_relative_to(project.root / c.STATE):
            raise GuardError("Duplicate or private recovery target. No files restored.")
        seen.add(key)
        for field in ("before_sha256", "after_sha256"):
            value = entry.get(field)
            if not (field == "before_sha256" and value is None) and not (
                    isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{64}", value)):
                raise GuardError("Invalid recovery hash. No files restored.")
        expected = f"{number:03d}.bak" if entry["before_sha256"] is not None else None
        if entry.get("backup") != expected:
            raise GuardError("Invalid backup path. No files restored.")
    return journal


def manifests(project):
    root = c.local_path(project.root, f"{c.STATE}/backups")
    if root.exists():
        for folder in sorted(root.iterdir(), reverse=True):
            manifest = c.local_path(project.root, (folder.relative_to(project.root) / "manifest.json").as_posix())
            if manifest.is_file():
                yield manifest


def pending(project):
    for path in manifests(project):
        try:
            journal = read_manifest(project, path)
        except GuardError:
            return True
        if journal["status"] not in {"applied", "rolled_back"}:
            return True
    return False


def apply(project, encrypt_index=False, *, operation=None, expected_plan=None, backup_dir=None,
          expected_backup_preview=None, planner=None):
    from . import project_backup
    make_plan = planner or (lambda: c.plan(project, encrypt_index))
    make_plan()
    op = operation or Operation()
    # Full snapshot precedes even the project lock/state-directory writes.
    with writer():
        if pending(project):
            raise GuardError("An incomplete project transaction needs rollback before a new apply.")
        changes = make_plan()
        if expected_plan is not None and changes != expected_plan:
            raise GuardError("Project changed after the preview. Review a fresh plan before applying.")
        if not changes:
            return None
        full = project_backup.create(project, backup_dir, operation=op, expected_preview=expected_backup_preview)
        op.check_cancel()
        return _apply(project, encrypt_index, operation=op, expected_plan=changes, full_backup=full, planner=make_plan)


def _apply(project, encrypt_index=False, *, operation=None, expected_plan=None, full_backup=None, planner=None):
    # Invalid versions/keys must not even create a project state directory.
    make_plan = planner or (lambda: c.plan(project, encrypt_index))
    make_plan()
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer(project):
        if pending(project):
            raise GuardError("An incomplete project transaction needs rollback before a new apply.")
        changes = make_plan()
        if expected_plan is not None and changes != expected_plan:
            raise GuardError("Project changed after the preview. Review a fresh plan before applying.")
        if not changes:
            return None
        op.step("backup preflight", 0, len(changes))
        check_space([(project.root, sum(len(x.data) * 2 for x in changes) + sum(
            c.local_path(project.root, x.path).stat().st_size * 2 for x in changes if x.before_sha256 is not None))])
        tx = c.datetime.now(c.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ") + "-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
        folder = c.local_path(project.root, f"{c.STATE}/backups/{tx}")
        manifest = folder / "manifest.json"
        entries = []
        try:
            c.atomic_write(c.local_path(project.root, f"{c.STATE}/.gitignore"), b"*\n")
            for number, change in enumerate(changes):
                op.step("verifying backups", number, len(changes))
                target = c.local_path(project.root, change.path)
                before = target.read_bytes() if target.exists() else None
                if (c.digest(before) if before is not None else None) != change.before_sha256:
                    raise GuardError("Project changed during planning. No project files changed; review a fresh plan.")
                backup = f"{number:03d}.bak" if before is not None else None
                if backup:
                    c.atomic_write(folder / backup, before)
                    if c.file_digest(folder / backup) != change.before_sha256:
                        raise GuardError("Backup integrity check failed; no project files changed.")
                entries.append({"path": change.path, "before_sha256": change.before_sha256,
                                "after_sha256": c.digest(change.data), "backup": backup})
            journal = {"root": str(project.root), "created_utc": c.utc_now(), "files": entries,
                       "full_backup": full_backup}
            record(manifest, journal, "prepared")
            # Preflight every destination before the first mutation, not only as we go.
            for entry in entries:
                target = c.local_path(project.root, entry["path"])
                if (c.file_digest(target) if target.exists() else None) != entry["before_sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Project changed during backup preparation; no project files changed.")
            op.step("ready to commit", len(changes), len(changes))
        except BaseException:
            # Only our newly created, flat preparation directory is eligible. No recursive delete.
            if folder.exists():
                try:
                    for path in folder.iterdir():
                        if path.is_file() and not c.linked(path) and (
                                path.name == "manifest.json" or re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.bak", path.name)):
                            path.unlink()
                    folder.rmdir()
                except OSError:
                    raise GuardError("Backup preparation failed; no project files changed. Retain the incomplete backup folder for manual inspection.") from None
            raise
        try:
            record(manifest, journal, "applying")
            for number, (change, entry) in enumerate(zip(changes, entries)):
                op.step("committing project (cannot cancel)", number, len(changes), cancellable=False)
                target = c.local_path(project.root, change.path)
                if (c.file_digest(target) if target.exists() else None) != entry["before_sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Project changed during commit.")
                c.atomic_write(target, change.data)
                if c.file_digest(target) != entry["after_sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Project write verification failed.")
            record(manifest, journal, "applied")
            op.step("project committed", len(changes), len(changes), cancellable=False)
        except BaseException as error:
            try:
                record(manifest, journal, "apply_incomplete")
            except (OSError, GuardError):
                pass  # Last prepared/applying record is sufficient for conflict-aware rollback.
            raise GuardError(f"Apply interrupted ({type(error).__name__}). Close the editor and run rollback. Recovery: {manifest}") from None
        return manifest


def prepare_rollback(project):
    for manifest in manifests(project):
        journal = read_manifest(project, manifest)
        if journal["status"] == "rolled_back":
            continue
        prepared = []
        for entry in journal["files"]:
            target = c.local_path(project.root, entry["path"])
            current = c.file_digest(target) if target.exists() else None
            if current not in (entry["before_sha256"], entry["after_sha256"]):
                raise GuardError(f"Rollback conflict: {entry['path']} changed after adaptation. No files restored.")
            before = None
            if entry["before_sha256"] is not None:
                before = c.local_path(manifest.parent, entry["backup"]).read_bytes()
                if c.digest(before) != entry["before_sha256"]:
                    raise GuardError("Backup integrity check failed. No files restored.")
            prepared.append((target, before, current))
        return manifest, journal, prepared
    raise GuardError("No pending release-guard backup exists; all recorded changes may already be rolled back.")


def rollback_preview(project):
    manifest, journal, _ = prepare_rollback(project)
    return {"journal": str(manifest), "legacy_journal": journal["schema"] == 1,
            "files": [entry["path"] for entry in journal["files"]]}


def rollback(project, *, operation=None, expected_preview=None):
    op = operation or Operation()
    with writer(project):
        op.step("verifying rollback")
        if expected_preview is not None and rollback_preview(project) != expected_preview:
            raise GuardError("Recovery target changed after preview. Review again before rollback.")
        manifest, journal, prepared = prepare_rollback(project)
        check_space([(project.root, sum(len(data) for _, data, _ in prepared if data is not None))])
        op.step("ready to restore")
        try:
            record(manifest, journal, "rolling_back")
            for number, (target, before, expected) in enumerate(reversed(prepared)):
                op.step("restoring project (cannot cancel)", number, len(prepared), cancellable=False)
                c.local_path(project.root, target.relative_to(project.root).as_posix())
                if (c.file_digest(target) if target.exists() else None) != expected:
                    raise GuardError("Rollback conflict during restore.")
                if before is None:
                    if target.exists():
                        target.unlink()
                else:
                    c.atomic_write(target, before)
                if (c.file_digest(target) if target.exists() else None) != (c.digest(before) if before is not None else None):
                    raise GuardError("Restore verification failed.")
            journal["restored_utc"] = c.utc_now()
            record(manifest, journal, "rolled_back")
            op.step("rollback completed", len(prepared), len(prepared), cancellable=False)
        except BaseException as error:
            try:
                record(manifest, journal, "rollback_incomplete")
            except (OSError, GuardError):
                pass
            raise GuardError(f"Rollback interrupted ({type(error).__name__}). Preserve the journal and retry rollback after resolving file access/disk space: {manifest}") from None
        return manifest

```

```py
def create(destination):
    destination = Path(destination).resolve()
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    manifest = {}
    for engine, files in SCENARIOS.items():
        for mode in ("new", "existing"):
            root = destination / engine / mode
            selected = dict(files)
            if mode == "new":
                selected = {p: t for p, t in selected.items() if "DevReward" not in p and "dev_reward" not in p}
            selected["LOCAL_NOTES.txt"] = (
                "Synthetic offline single-player fixture; not a complete buildable project.\n"
                "No engine, export template, SDK, license or packaged output is installed here.\n"
                "Do not upload, install, change keys or execute bundled game code.\n"
                "The Unreal Evidence file is a copied version record, not an engine installation.\n"
                "GameMaker Runtime version and output mode are intentionally not recorded.\n"
            )
            selected["private-config.txt"] = "service_credential=synthetic_nonfunctional_value_do_not_echo_57d849\n"
            selected["unknown-resource.dat"] = "Synthetic unknown asset: preserve exactly.\n"
            selected["user-work.txt"] = "Uncommitted user note: keep this local edit.\n"
            for path, content in selected.items():
                target = root / path
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
                manifest[target.relative_to(destination).as_posix()] = hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (destination / "original-hashes.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Created {len(manifest)} synthetic files in {destination}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2:
        raise SystemExit(__doc__)
    create(sys.argv[1])
```

`tests/test_advanced_acceptance.py`:

```py
import unittest

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core
from steam_release_guard.advanced import acceptance, integrity


class AcceptanceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put

    def setUp(self):
        fixtures.GuardTests.setUp(self)
        self.record = acceptance.template()
        self.baseline = self.root / "baseline"
        self.candidate = self.root / "candidate"
        self.evidence = self.root / "evidence"
        self.evidence.mkdir()
        self.manifests = []
        for side, build in (("baseline", self.baseline), ("candidate", self.candidate)):
            self.put("Game.exe", b"synthetic-" + side.encode(), build)
            manifest = self.root / (side + ".json")
            result = integrity.create(build, self.record[side]["build_id"], manifest)
            self.record[side]["manifest_sha256"] = result["manifest_sha256"]
            self.manifests.append(manifest)

    def check(self):
        return acceptance.check(self.record, self.baseline, self.candidate, *self.manifests, self.evidence)

    def evidence_ref(self):
        path = self.put("measurement.txt", "synthetic evidence only", self.evidence)
        return {"path": "measurement.txt", "sha256": core.file_digest(path)}

    def test_missing_measurements_and_runtime_are_never_pass(self):
        report = self.check()
        self.assertEqual(report["release_status"], "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW")
        self.assertTrue(all(m["status"] == "NOT_VERIFIED" for m in report["metrics"].values()))
        self.assertEqual(report["runtime_checks"]["startup"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")

    def test_real_evidence_files_do_not_upgrade_review_or_runtime_claims(self):
        ref = self.evidence_ref()
        for status in ("REVIEW", "NOT_VERIFIED", "PASS"):
            self.record["runtime_checks"]["startup"] = {"status": status, "reason": "fixture", "evidence": [ref]}
            expected = "RECORDED_PASS" if status == "PASS" else status
            self.assertEqual(self.check()["runtime_checks"]["startup"]["status"], expected)
        self.record["runtime_checks"]["startup"]["evidence"] = []
        self.assertEqual(self.check()["runtime_checks"]["startup"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")

    def test_metric_regression_and_zero_baseline_are_blockers(self):
        data = {"baseline_samples": [100, 110, 100], "candidate_samples": [140, 145, 140],
                "max_regression_pct": 10, "evidence": [self.evidence_ref()]}
        self.record["metrics"]["startup_ms"] = data
        self.assertEqual(self.check()["release_status"], "BLOCKED")
        data.update(baseline_samples=[0, 0, 0], candidate_samples=[1, 1, 1])
        metric = self.check()["metrics"]["startup_ms"]
        self.assertEqual(metric["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertIsNone(metric["regression_pct"])

    def test_changed_build_or_evidence_invalidates_acceptance(self):
        ref = self.evidence_ref()
        self.record["protection_checks"][0]["artifact"] = {"status": "PASS", "reason": "fixture", "evidence": [ref]}
        (self.evidence / ref["path"]).write_bytes(b"changed")
        self.assertEqual(self.check()["release_status"], "BLOCKED")
        self.record["metrics"]["file_bytes"]["evidence"] = [ref]
        self.record["metrics"]["file_bytes"]["max_regression_pct"] = 100
        self.assertEqual(self.check()["metrics"]["file_bytes"]["status"], "FAIL")
        (self.candidate / "Game.exe").write_bytes(b"updated")
        self.assertIn("Build bytes", self.check()["reason"])

    def test_malformed_metrics_typo_and_traversal_rejected(self):
        self.record["metrics"]["startup_ms"]["candidate_samples"] = [True]
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            self.check()
        self.record["metrics"]["startup_ms"]["candidate_samples"] = []
        self.record["runtime_checks"]["startup"]["evidence"] = [{"path": "../secret", "sha256": "a" * 64}]
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            self.check()
```

`tests/test_advanced_binary.py`:

```py
import json
import struct
import unittest

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core
from steam_release_guard.advanced.binary import pe_info, unity_audit, literal_candidates


def minimal_pe(managed=False):
    """Synthetic structural PE fixture, NOT a working game/native dependency."""
    data = bytearray(2048)
    data[:2] = b"MZ"
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, 0x3c, 128)
    data[128:132] = b"PE\0\0"
    struct.pack_into("<HH", data, 132, 0x8664, 1)
    struct.pack_into("<H", data, 148, 240)
    struct.pack_into("<H", data, 152, 0x20b)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, 260, 16)
    struct.pack_into("<IIII", data, 400, 1024, 0x1000, 1024, 512)
    if managed:
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, 264 + 14 * 8, 0x1000, 72)
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, 520, 0x1100, 100)
        data[768:772] = b"BSJB"
    return bytes(data)


class BinaryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unity = fixtures.GuardTests.unity

    def test_valid_native_and_managed_structures_are_distinct(self):
        self.assertFalse(pe_info(self.put("native.dll", minimal_pe()))["managed"])
        self.assertTrue(pe_info(self.put("managed.dll", minimal_pe(True)))["managed"])

    def test_truncated_headers_and_unbacked_clr_are_rejected(self):
        for content in (b"MZ", minimal_pe()[:500], b"not PE"):
            with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                pe_info(self.put("broken.dll", content))
        data = bytearray(minimal_pe(True))
        struct.pack_into("<I", data, 520, 0xffffff00)
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            pe_info(self.put("bad-clr.dll", data))

    def test_header_mismatch_absence_and_rename_are_never_encryption_proof(self):
        project = self.unity()
        build = self.root / "build"
        path = self.put("global-metadata.dat", b"renamed-compressed-unknown", build)
        report = unity_audit(project, build)
        metadata = next(f for f in report["findings"] if f["id"] == "IL2CPP_METADATA")
        self.assertEqual(metadata["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        path.write_bytes(struct.pack("<II", 0xfab11baf, 31))
        report = unity_audit(project, build)
        self.assertEqual(next(f for f in report["findings"] if f["id"] == "IL2CPP_METADATA")["status"], "REVIEW")
        self.assertFalse(any(f["status"] == "PASS" for f in report["findings"]))

    def test_secret_findings_redact_values_and_do_not_delete_modules(self):
        project = self.unity()
        build = self.root / "build"
        file = self.put("SDK.dll", minimal_pe() + b'api_key="private_value_123456"', build)
        original = file.read_bytes()
        report = unity_audit(project, build)
        self.assertIn("BINARY_SECRET_CANDIDATE", json.dumps(report))
        self.assertNotIn("private_value_123456", json.dumps(report))
        self.assertEqual(original, file.read_bytes())
        boundary = self.put("boundary.bin", b"x" * 65530 + b'\nclient_secret="private_value_123456"')
        self.assertEqual(len(literal_candidates(boundary)), 1)
```

`tests/test_advanced_integrity.py`:

```py
import json
import unittest

import test_guard as fixtures
from test_advanced_binary import minimal_pe
from steam_release_guard import core
from steam_release_guard.advanced import integrity


class IntegrityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli

    def release(self):
        self.build = self.root / "build"
        for name in ("Game.exe", "SDK.dll", "audio.bank", "config.ini", "Textures/Hero.dds"):
            self.put(name, name.encode(), self.build)
        self.manifest = self.root / "private/manifest.json"
        self.record = integrity.create(self.build, "r1", self.manifest, ["Saved/*"])

    def verify(self, **kwargs):
        return integrity.verify(self.build, self.manifest, kwargs.get("identifier", "r1"),
                                kwargs.get("pin", self.record["manifest_sha256"]))

    def test_detects_modified_missing_and_added_files_without_mutating(self):
        self.release()
        self.assertEqual(self.verify()["status"], "INTEGRITY_MATCH")
        (self.build / "Game.exe").write_bytes(b"tamper")
        (self.build / "SDK.dll").unlink()
        self.put("extra.dll", b"extra", self.build)
        before = {str(p): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(self.build)}
        result = self.verify()
        self.assertEqual(result["changed"], ["Game.exe"])
        self.assertEqual(result["missing"], ["SDK.dll"])
        self.assertEqual(result["unexpected"], ["extra.dll"])
        self.assertEqual(before, {str(p): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(self.build)})

    def test_replaced_manifest_or_wrong_build_never_establishes_trust(self):
        self.release()
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            self.verify(identifier="r2")
        self.manifest.write_bytes(self.manifest.read_bytes() + b" ")
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            self.verify()

    def test_traversal_duplicate_unknown_schema_block_even_when_hash_matches(self):
        self.release()
        original = json.loads(self.manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for mode in ("path", "duplicate", "schema", "mutable"):
            value = json.loads(json.dumps(original))
            if mode == "path": value["files"][0]["path"] = "../outside"
            elif mode == "duplicate": value["files"].append(value["files"][0])
            elif mode == "schema": value["schema"] = 99
            else: value["mutable"] = ["*"]
            self.manifest.write_text(json.dumps(value), encoding="utf-8")
            with self.subTest(mode=mode), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                self.verify(pin=core.file_digest(self.manifest))

    def test_mutable_files_explicit_and_updates_require_new_baseline(self):
        self.release()
        self.put("Saved/player.json", "user save", self.build)
        self.assertEqual(self.verify()["status"], "INTEGRITY_MATCH")
        (self.build / "Game.exe").write_bytes(b"legitimate update")
        new = self.root / "r2.json"
        record = integrity.create(self.build, "r2", new, ["Saved/*"])
        self.assertEqual(integrity.verify(self.build, new, "r2", record["manifest_sha256"])["status"], "INTEGRITY_MATCH")
        self.assertEqual(self.verify()["status"], "INTEGRITY_MISMATCH")

    def test_preview_and_existing_output_never_write(self):
        self.release()
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            integrity.create(self.build, "r1", self.manifest)
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            integrity.create(self.build, "r1", self.build / "manifest.json")
        output = self.root / "not-created.json"
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("advanced", "integrity-create", self.build, "--build-id", "r1", "--output", output, "--dry-run", "--json")[0], 0)
        self.assertFalse(output.exists())

    def test_drm_checks_structure_and_preserved_original_without_claiming_wrap(self):
        original = self.put("original.exe", minimal_pe())
        wrapped = self.put("wrapped.exe", minimal_pe() + b"fake change, not Steam DRM")
        result = integrity.drm_inspect(original, wrapped, core.file_digest(original))
        self.assertTrue(result["original_preserved"])
        self.assertEqual(result["wrapper_success"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            integrity.drm_inspect(original, wrapped, "a" * 64)
```

`tests/test_advanced_obfuscation.py`:

```py
"""Adapter contracts on synthetic projects; no claim of engine protection."""
import json
import os
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core, project_backup
from steam_release_guard.advanced import obfuscation as ob
from steam_release_guard.advanced.common import private_run, read_json


class ObfuscationTests(fixtures.GuardTests.__bases__[0]):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unity = fixtures.GuardTests.unity
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli

    def config(self):
        return {"schema": 1, "engine_version": "6000.6.6f1", "adapter": "obfuz", "tool_version": "4.0.0",
                "assemblies": [{"name": "Game.Core", "include_types": ["Game.Core.Rules"],
                                "exclude_types": ["Game.Core.Save*"], "rename_fields": False}],
                "reference_assemblies": ["Assembly-CSharp"], "strings": False, "control_flow": False}

    def test_plan_is_read_only_excludes_all_until_explicitly_selected(self):
        project = self.unity()
        before = sorted(core.walk_files(project.root))
        planned = ob.ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, self.config())
        self.assertEqual(before, sorted(core.walk_files(project.root)))
        xml = ET.fromstring(next(p.data for p in planned if p.path.endswith("rename.xml")))
        types = xml.find("assembly").findall("type")
        self.assertEqual(types[0].attrib["obName"], "0")
        self.assertEqual(types[1].attrib["name"], "Game.Core.Rules")
        self.assertEqual(types[1].find("field").attrib["obName"], "0")
        self.assertEqual(types[-1].attrib["name"], "Game.Core.Save*")
        self.assertEqual(types[-1].attrib["obName"], "0")

    def test_unknown_version_wildcard_default_and_unverified_passes_rejected(self):
        project = self.unity()
        for change in ({"tool_version": "99"}, {"engine_version": "6000.1.0f1"}, {"strings": True},
                       {"control_flow": True}, {"assemblies": []}, {"typo": True}):
            with self.subTest(change=change), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                ob.validate(project, {**self.config(), **change})
        config = self.config()
        config["assemblies"][0]["include_types"] = ["*"]
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            ob.validate(project, config)

    def test_detect_missing_and_exact_version_without_execution(self):
        project = self.unity()
        adapter = ob.ObfuzAdapter()
        self.assertEqual(adapter.detect(project)["status"], "MISSING")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "missing"):
            ob.apply(project, self.config())
        self.assertFalse((project.root / core.STATE).exists())
        package = self.put("Packages/com.code-philosophy.obfuz/package.json",
                           '{"name":"com.code-philosophy.obfuz","version":"4.0.0"}', project.root)
        self.assertTrue(adapter.detect(project)["installed_candidates"][0]["api_supported"])
        package.write_text('{"name":"com.code-philosophy.obfuz","version":"5.0.0"}')
        self.assertFalse(adapter.detect(project)["installed_candidates"][0]["api_supported"])

    def test_advanced_changes_use_full_backup_and_byte_exact_old_rollback(self):
        project = self.unity()
        self.put("Packages/com.code-philosophy.obfuz/package.json", '{"name":"com.code-philosophy.obfuz","version":"4.0.0"}', project.root)
        before = {str(p.relative_to(project.root)): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(project.root)}
        planned = ob.ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, self.config())
        manifest = ob.apply(project, self.config(), expected_plan=planned)
        journal = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(project_backup.verify(journal["full_backup"]["backup_path"])["status"], "VERIFIED")
        core.rollback(project)
        after = {str(p.relative_to(project.root)): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(project.root)
                 if not p.is_relative_to(project.root / core.STATE)}
        self.assertEqual(before, after)

    def test_failed_full_backup_and_stale_plan_do_not_mutate_project(self):
        project = self.unity()
        self.put("Packages/com.code-philosophy.obfuz/package.json", '{"name":"com.code-philosophy.obfuz","version":"4.0.0"}', project.root)
        planned = ob.ObfuzAdapter().plan(project, self.config())
        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.project_backup.create", side_effect=core.GuardError("backup failed")):
            with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                ob.apply(project, self.config(), expected_plan=planned)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / ob.CONFIG).exists())
        self.put(ob.CONFIG, "user change", project.root)
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            ob.apply(project, self.config(), expected_plan=planned)
        self.assertEqual((project.root / ob.CONFIG).read_text(), "user change")

    def test_build_refuses_original_project_even_when_explicitly_requested(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "backup-restore"):
            ob.build_preview(self.unity(), self.config(), Path("Unity.exe"), self.root / "build", self.root / "logs")

    def test_cli_preview_consent_missing_tool_and_status(self):
        project = self.unity()
        config = self.put("config.json", json.dumps(self.config()))
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("advanced", "unity-plan", project.root, "--config", config, "--json")[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("advanced", "unity-apply", project.root, "--config", config, "--json")[0], 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("advanced", "unity-apply", project.root, "--config", config, "--yes", "--json")[0], 1)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / ob.CONFIG).exists())

    def test_duplicate_json_fields_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            read_json(self.put("config.json", '{"strings":true,"strings":false}'))

    def test_real_subprocess_has_redacted_bounded_log_and_no_shell(self):
        folder = self.root / "private"
        folder.mkdir()
        record = private_run([Path(sys.executable).resolve(strict=True), "-c", "print('EncryptionKey=DO_NOT_LOG');print('done')"], folder)
        self.assertEqual(record["exit_code"], 0)
        self.assertNotIn("DO_NOT_LOG", (folder / "tool.log").read_text())
        self.assertIn("done", (folder / "tool.log").read_text())
        self.assertFalse((folder / "tool-output.private").exists())

    def test_fast_tool_exceeding_log_limit_is_not_success(self):
        folder = self.root / "private"
        folder.mkdir()

        def fast_process(*args, **kwargs):
            # Explicit fake process: exits before the first poll. The retained
            # bytes must be bounded even without the polling loop running.
            kwargs["stdout"].write(b"x" * (16 * 1024 * 1024 + 1))
            return mock.Mock(poll=mock.Mock(return_value=0), returncode=0)

        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.advanced.common.subprocess.Popen", side_effect=fast_process):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "log limit"):
                private_run([Path(sys.executable).resolve(strict=True)], folder)
        self.assertLessEqual((folder / "tool.log").stat().st_size, 16 * 1024 * 1024)
        self.assertFalse((folder / "tool-output.private").exists())

    def test_untrusted_build_receipt_requires_exact_assembly_and_output_binding(self):
        project, config = self.unity(), self.config()
        output = self.root / "build"
        executable = self.put("Game.exe", b"synthetic receipt subject, not a runnable game", output)
        receipt = {"configuration": "PASS", "artifact": "PASS", "runtime": "NOT_VERIFIED",
                   "engine_version": project.version, "tool_version": "4.0.0",
                   "executable_sha256": core.file_digest(executable),
                   "assemblies": [{"name": "Game.Core", "before_sha256": "a" * 64,
                                   "after_sha256": "b" * 64, "renamed_selected_types": 1}]}

        for index, change in enumerate(({}, {"runtime": "PASS"}, {"executable_sha256": "0" * 64},
                                        {"assemblies": []}, {"assemblies": receipt["assemblies"] * 2})):
            def fake_tool(argv, folder, **kwargs):
                (folder / "obfuscation.json").write_text(json.dumps({**receipt, **change}), encoding="utf-8")
                return {"exit_code": 0}

            with self.subTest(change=change), mock.patch.object(ob, "build_preview", return_value={}), \
                 mock.patch.object(ob.ObfuzAdapter, "plan", return_value=[]), \
                 mock.patch.object(ob, "private_run", side_effect=fake_tool):
                if change:
                    with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                        ob.build(project, config, self.root / "Unity.exe", output, self.root / f"proof{index}")
                else:
                    record = ob.build(project, config, self.root / "Unity.exe", output, self.root / f"proof{index}")
                    self.assertEqual(record["status"], "REVIEW")
                    self.assertEqual(record["artifact"]["runtime"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
```

`tests/test_advanced_security.py`:

```py
import json
import unittest

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core
from steam_release_guard.advanced.security import audit


class SecurityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unity = fixtures.GuardTests.unity
    unreal = fixtures.GuardTests.unreal
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli

    def test_unity_finds_concrete_locations_without_secret_values_or_writes(self):
        project = self.unity()
        self.put("Assets/Game/Economy.cs", '''string api_key = "private-secret-not-in-output";
coins += clientReward;
PlayerPrefs.SetInt("coins", coins);
isAdmin = true;
[ServerRpc(RequireOwnership = false)]
void RequestTrade() { }
// loot result
var result = Random.Range(0, 100);
void DebugMenu() { }
''', project.root)
        before = {str(p): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(project.root)}
        report = audit(project)
        ids = {f["id"] for f in report["findings"]}
        self.assertTrue({"HARDCODED_SECRET", "CLIENT_ECONOMY_WRITE", "LOCAL_CRITICAL_SAVE", "CLIENT_PRIVILEGE", "NETWORK_MUTATION", "LOCAL_REWARD_RANDOMNESS", "RELEASE_DEBUG_ENTRY"}.issubset(ids))
        first = report["findings"][0]["evidence"][0]
        self.assertEqual((first["path"], first["line"]), ("Assets/Game/Economy.cs", 1))
        self.assertNotIn("private-secret-not-in-output", json.dumps(report))
        self.assertEqual(before, {str(p): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(project.root)})

    def test_unreal_authority_and_dev_guards_are_acknowledged_not_proven(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        self.put("Source/Game/Trade.cpp", '''UFUNCTION(Server, Reliable, WithValidation)
void RequestTrade();
if (HasAuthority()) { balance += reward; }
#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
void EnableCheats();
#endif
''', project.root)
        report = audit(project)
        row = next(f for f in report["findings"] if f["id"] == "CLIENT_ECONOMY_WRITE")
        self.assertTrue(row["evidence"][0]["authority_tokens_present_in_file"])
        self.assertTrue(row["evidence"][0]["development_guard_tokens_present_in_file"])
        self.assertEqual(row["status"], "REVIEW")
        self.assertFalse(any(f["status"] == "PASS" for f in report["findings"]))

    def test_clean_empty_and_oversized_source_never_gets_security_pass(self):
        project = self.unity()
        self.put("Assets/Large.cs", b"x" * (2 * 1024 * 1024 + 1), project.root)
        self.put("Library/Ignored.cs", 'api_key="not-scanned"', project.root)
        report = audit(project)
        coverage = next(f for f in report["findings"] if f["id"] == "SCAN_COVERAGE")
        self.assertEqual(coverage["evidence"][0]["path"], "Assets/Large.cs")
        self.assertFalse(any(f["status"] == "PASS" for f in report["findings"]))
        self.assertEqual(report["release_status"], "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW")

    def test_utf16_and_cli_do_not_generate_report_files(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        self.put("Config/Client.ini", 'client_secret="fixture-value-123456"'.encode("utf-16"), project.root)
        code, text, _ = self.cli("advanced", "security-inspect", project.root, "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, text)
        self.assertIn("HARDCODED_SECRET", text)
        self.assertNotIn("fixture-value-123456", text)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / core.STATE).exists())
```
