`tests/test_project_backup.py`:

```py
"""Full project backup/restore contracts, including failures before adaptation."""
import errno
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core, project_backup as backups
from steam_release_guard.operations import Cancelled, Operation, seal


class FullBackupTests(unittest.TestCase):
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unity = fixtures.GuardTests.unity
    unreal = fixtures.GuardTests.unreal
    config = fixtures.GuardTests.config
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli

    def setUp(self):
        fixtures.GuardTests.setUp(self)
        patch = mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"SRG_LOCK_DIRECTORY": str(self.root / "locks")})
        patch.start()
        self.addCleanup(patch.stop)
        self.project = self.unity()
        for name in (".hidden", ".git/objects/example", "Library/cache.bin", "Assets/音效.wav", "Assets/音效.wav.meta",
                     "Builds/Demo.exe", "Tools/build.bat"):
            self.put(name, name.encode("utf-8") + b"\0payload", self.project.root)
        (self.project.root / "Assets/Empty Folder").mkdir()
        self.destination = self.root / "private-full-backups"

    def contents(self, root):
        return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(root)}

    def create(self):
        result = backups.create(self.project, self.destination)
        return Path(result["backup_path"])

    def test_backup_and_restore_include_hidden_cache_git_meta_and_empty_directories(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        snapshot = self.create()
        payload = snapshot / backups.PAYLOAD
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(payload))
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        self.assertTrue((payload / "Assets/Empty Folder").is_dir())
        self.assertNotEqual((payload / "Assets/音效.wav").stat().st_ino,
                            (self.project.root / "Assets/音效.wav").stat().st_ino)
        (self.project.root / "Assets/音效.wav").write_bytes(b"later user changes")
        self.assertEqual(backups.verify(snapshot)["status"], "VERIFIED")
        restored = self.root / "restored"
        result = backups.restore(snapshot, restored)
        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "COMPLETE")
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(restored))
        self.assertTrue((restored / "Assets/Empty Folder").is_dir())
        self.assertEqual((self.project.root / "Assets/音效.wav").read_bytes(), b"later user changes")
        self.assertEqual(backups.verify(snapshot)["status"], "VERIFIED")

    def test_apply_has_verified_full_copy_before_first_project_file_write(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        original = core.atomic_write
        observed = []

        def inspect(path, data):
            if path == self.project.root / ".gitignore":
                snapshots = list(self.destination.iterdir())
                self.assertEqual(len(snapshots), 1)
                self.assertEqual(backups.verify(snapshots[0])["status"], "VERIFIED")
                self.assertEqual(self.contents(snapshots[0] / backups.PAYLOAD), before)
                observed.append(True)
            return original(path, data)
        with mock.patch.object(core, "atomic_write", side_effect=inspect):
            journal = core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination)
        self.assertEqual(observed, [True])
        full = json.loads(journal.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["full_backup"]
        self.assertEqual(full["status"], "COMPLETE")
        self.assertTrue(Path(full["backup_path"]).is_dir())
        core.rollback(self.project)
        self.assertTrue(Path(full["backup_path"]).is_dir(), "Rollback must retain the independent snapshot")

    def test_full_backup_failure_prevents_every_project_mutation(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)

        def fail(source, target, expected, op):
            target.write_bytes(b"partial")
            raise OSError(errno.ENOSPC, "private-secret-must-not-appear")
        with mock.patch.object(backups, "_copy", side_effect=fail):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "Full backup failed") as caught:
                core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination)
        self.assertNotIn("private-secret", str(caught.exception))
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        self.assertFalse((self.project.root / core.STATE).exists())
        snapshot = next(self.destination.iterdir())
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "INCOMPLETE"):
            backups.verify(snapshot)
        with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
            backups.restore(snapshot, self.root / "cannot-restore")
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "cannot-restore").exists())

    def test_insufficient_space_does_not_create_snapshot_or_change_project(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        usage = shutil.disk_usage(self.root)
        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.operations.shutil.disk_usage", return_value=usage._replace(free=1)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "space"):
                core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination)
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        self.assertFalse(self.destination.exists())

    def test_changed_source_or_corrupt_copy_never_allows_apply(self):
        original = backups._copy
        for mode in ("source", "copy"):
            destination = self.root / mode
            changed = []

            def alter(source, target, expected, op):
                result = original(source, target, expected, op)
                if not changed:
                    (source if mode == "source" else target).write_bytes(("changed " + mode).encode())
                    changed.append(True)
                return result
            with self.subTest(mode=mode), mock.patch.object(backups, "_copy", side_effect=alter):
                with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                    core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=destination)
            self.assertFalse((self.project.root / ".gitignore").exists())
            self.assertFalse((self.project.root / core.UNITY_ADAPTER).exists())
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "INCOMPLETE"):
                backups.verify(next(destination.iterdir()))

    def test_cancel_between_verified_backup_and_apply_preserves_original(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        op = Operation(lambda event: op.cancel.set() if event.get("backup_path") else None)
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination, operation=op)
        self.assertEqual(before, {k: v for k, v in self.contents(self.project.root).items() if not k.startswith(core.STATE + "/")})
        self.assertEqual(backups.verify(next(self.destination.iterdir()))["status"], "VERIFIED")

    def test_cancel_during_copy_leaves_incomplete_backup(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        op = Operation(lambda e: op.cancel.set() if e["phase"] == "copying full project backup" and e["current"] == 1 else None)
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            backups.create(self.project, self.destination, operation=op)
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "INCOMPLETE"):
            backups.verify(next(self.destination.iterdir()))

    def test_corruption_unknown_files_and_missing_empty_directory_block_restore_before_write(self):
        for mode in ("corrupt", "extra", "empty_dir"):
            snapshot = self.create()
            if mode == "corrupt":
                (snapshot / backups.PAYLOAD / "Assets/音效.wav").write_bytes(b"corrupt")
            elif mode == "extra":
                (snapshot / backups.PAYLOAD / "unknown.txt").write_bytes(b"unexpected")
            else:
                (snapshot / backups.PAYLOAD / "Assets/Empty Folder").rmdir()
            output = self.root / ("restore-" + mode)
            with self.subTest(mode=mode), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                backups.restore(snapshot, output)
            self.assertFalse(output.exists())
            self.assertFalse(output.with_name(output.name + ".srg-restore.json").exists())

    def test_manifest_traversal_duplicates_and_tampering_are_rejected(self):
        snapshot = self.create()
        manifest = snapshot / backups.MANIFEST
        original = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for mode in ("hash", "traversal", "duplicate", "parent", "incomplete"):
            record = json.loads(json.dumps(original))
            if mode == "hash":
                record["files"][0]["sha256"] = "0" * 64
            elif mode == "traversal":
                record["files"][0]["path"] = "../outside"
                seal(record)
            elif mode == "duplicate":
                record["files"].append(record["files"][0])
                record["file_count"] += 1
                seal(record)
            elif mode == "parent":
                record["directories"].remove("Assets")
                seal(record)
            else:
                record["status"] = "INCOMPLETE"
                seal(record)
            manifest.write_text(json.dumps(record), encoding="utf-8")
            with self.subTest(mode=mode), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                backups.restore(snapshot, self.root / "rejected")
            self.assertFalse((self.root / "rejected").exists())

    def test_nested_and_existing_destinations_never_overwrite(self):
        for path in (self.project.root, self.project.root / "backups", self.project.root.parent):
            with self.subTest(path=path), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                backups.create(self.project, path)
        snapshot = self.create()
        existing = self.root / "existing"
        existing.mkdir()
        (existing / "keep").write_bytes(b"user file")
        for path in (existing, self.project.root, self.project.root / "restore", snapshot / "restore"):
            with self.subTest(path=path), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                backups.restore(snapshot, path)
        self.assertEqual((existing / "keep").read_bytes(), b"user file")

    def test_restored_transaction_history_supports_rollback_and_second_generation_restore(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination)
        snapshot = self.create()
        output = self.root / "with-history"
        backups.restore(snapshot, output)
        restored_project = core.detect_project(output)
        core.rollback(restored_project)
        payload = {k: v for k, v in self.contents(output).items() if not k.startswith(core.STATE + "/")}
        self.assertEqual(before, payload)
        second = backups.create(restored_project, self.root / "second-backups")
        again = self.root / "restored-again"
        backups.restore(second["backup_path"], again)
        self.assertIsNotNone(core.apply(core.detect_project(again), backup_dir=self.root / "third-backups"))
        self.assertTrue((self.project.root / core.UNITY_ADAPTER).exists(), "Original project stays adapted")

    def test_incomplete_restore_receipt_blocks_apply_and_audit(self):
        receipt = self.project.root.with_name(self.project.root.name + ".srg-restore.json")
        receipt.write_text(json.dumps({"status": "INCOMPLETE", "output": str(self.project.root)}), encoding="utf-8")
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "restoration is incomplete"):
            core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination)
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        report = core.audit(self.project)
        self.assertEqual(report["release_status"], "BLOCKED")
        self.assertTrue(any(f["id"] == "INCOMPLETE_FULL_RESTORE" and f["status"] == "fail" for f in report["findings"]))

    def test_restore_failure_preserves_original_and_records_incomplete(self):
        snapshot = self.create()
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        original_copy = backups._copy
        copied = []

        def fail(source, target, expected, op):
            if copied:
                target.write_bytes(b"partial")
                raise OSError(errno.ENOSPC, "injected")
            copied.append(True)
            return original_copy(source, target, expected, op)
        output = self.root / "partial-restore"
        with mock.patch.object(backups, "_copy", side_effect=fail), self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "incomplete"):
            backups.restore(snapshot, output)
        receipt = json.loads(output.with_name(output.name + ".srg-restore.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(receipt["status"], "INCOMPLETE")
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        self.assertEqual(backups.verify(snapshot)["status"], "VERIFIED")

    def test_cli_preview_confirmation_verify_restore_and_custom_apply_destination(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        code, text, _ = self.cli("backup", self.project.root, "--backup-dir", self.destination, "--dry-run", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(text)["status"], "PREVIEW")
        self.assertFalse(self.destination.exists())
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("backup", self.project.root, "--json")[0], 3)
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
        code, text, _ = self.cli("backup", self.project.root, "--backup-dir", self.destination, "--yes", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, text)
        snapshot = json.loads(text)["result"]["backup_path"]
        code, text, _ = self.cli("backup-verify", snapshot, "--json")
        self.assertEqual((code, json.loads(text)["status"]), (0, "VERIFIED"))
        output = self.root / "cli-restored"
        self.assertEqual(self.cli("backup-restore", snapshot, "--output", output, "--json")[0], 3)
        self.assertFalse(output.exists())
        code, text, _ = self.cli("backup-restore", snapshot, "--output", output, "--yes", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, text)
        self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(output))
        code, text, _ = self.cli("apply", self.project.root, "--backup-dir", self.destination, "--yes", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, text)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(text)["result"]["full_backup"]["status"], "COMPLETE")

    def test_junction_or_symlink_is_refused_without_copying_external_files(self):
        external = self.root / "external"
        external.mkdir()
        (external / "secret").write_bytes(b"must not be copied")
        link = self.project.root / "linked"
        if os.name == "nt":
            process = subprocess.run(["cmd.exe", "/d", "/c", "mklink", "/J", str(link), str(external)], capture_output=True)
            if process.returncode:
                self.skipTest("Host cannot create a test junction")
            self.addCleanup(lambda: link.rmdir() if link.exists() else None)
        else:
            link.symlink_to(external, target_is_directory=True)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "symlinks/junctions"):
            backups.create(self.project, self.destination)
        self.assertFalse(self.destination.exists())
        self.assertEqual((external / "secret").read_bytes(), b"must not be copied")

    def test_killed_full_backup_cannot_be_restored_or_used_for_adaptation(self):
        before = self.contents(self.project.root)
        ready = self.root / "ready"
        script = '''import sys,time
from pathlib import Path
from steam_release_guard import core,project_backup
def pause(source,target,expected,op):
    target.write_bytes(b'partial')
    Path(sys.argv[3]).write_text('ready')
    time.sleep(60)
project_backup._copy=pause
core.apply(core.detect_project(sys.argv[1]),backup_dir=Path(sys.argv[2]))
'''
        child = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", script, str(self.project.root), str(self.destination), str(ready)],
                                 stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
            while not ready.exists() and child.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                time.sleep(.02)
            self.assertTrue(ready.exists(), "Child failed to reach partial full backup")
            child.kill()
            child.communicate(timeout=10)
            self.assertEqual(before, self.contents(self.project.root))
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "INCOMPLETE"):
                backups.verify(next(self.destination.iterdir()))
            # OS lock released; a new backup and adaptation can proceed safely.
            self.assertIsNotNone(core.apply(self.project, backup_dir=self.destination))
        finally:
            if child.poll() is None:
                child.kill()
            child.communicate(timeout=10)

```

`tests/test_transactions.py`:

```py
"""Failure behavior on synthetic fixtures; these are not engine protection tests."""
import errno
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core, staging, transactions
from steam_release_guard.operations import Cancelled, Operation, load_journal, seal, writer


class P0Tests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unity = fixtures.GuardTests.unity
    unreal = fixtures.GuardTests.unreal
    config = fixtures.GuardTests.config
    unity_build = fixtures.GuardTests.unity_build
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli
    ids = fixtures.GuardTests.ids

    def setUp(self):
        fixtures.GuardTests.setUp(self)
        patch = mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"SRG_LOCK_DIRECTORY": str(self.root / "locks")})
        patch.start()
        self.addCleanup(patch.stop)

    def child(self, script, *arguments):
        proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", script, *map(str, arguments)],
                                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)

        def stop():
            if proc.poll() is None:
                proc.kill()
            proc.communicate(timeout=10)
        self.addCleanup(stop)
        return proc

    def ready(self, path, proc):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
        while not path.exists() and time.monotonic() < deadline and proc.poll() is None:
            time.sleep(.02)
        if not path.exists():
            if proc.poll() is None:
                proc.kill()
            _, error = proc.communicate(timeout=10)
            self.fail("Child did not reach interruption point: " + error.decode(errors="replace"))

    def stage_paths(self):
        return self.unity_build(), self.root / "release", self.root / "symbols"

    def interrupted_stage(self):
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()

        def fail(source, partial, *args):
            partial.write_bytes(source.read_bytes()[:3])
            raise OSError(errno.ENOSPC, "private-value-must-not-appear")
        with mock.patch.object(staging, "_copy", side_effect=fail):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "INCOMPLETE") as caught:
                core.stage_release(build, release, symbols)
        self.assertNotIn("private-value", str(caught.exception))
        return build, release, symbols, symbols / staging.JOURNAL

    def test_process_lock_blocks_apply_rollback_stage_and_releases_on_kill(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        core.apply(project)
        ready = self.root / "ready"
        proc = self.child('''import sys,time
from pathlib import Path
from steam_release_guard.operations import writer
with writer():
    Path(sys.argv[1]).write_text('ready')
    time.sleep(60)
''', ready)
        self.ready(ready, proc)
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()
        for call in (lambda: core.apply(project), lambda: core.rollback(project),
                     lambda: core.stage_release(build, release, symbols)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "lock"):
                call()
        proc.kill()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        core.rollback(project)
        core.stage_release(build, release, symbols)

    def test_killed_apply_recovers_from_durable_journal(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        ready = self.root / "ready"
        proc = self.child('''import sys,time
from pathlib import Path
from steam_release_guard import core
write=core.atomic_write
def interrupt(path,data):
    write(path,data)
    if path.name=='.gitignore' and path.parent==Path(sys.argv[1]):
        Path(sys.argv[2]).write_text('ready')
        time.sleep(60)
core.atomic_write=interrupt
core.apply(core.detect_project(sys.argv[1]))
''', project.root, ready)
        self.ready(ready, proc)
        proc.kill()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        self.assertTrue(transactions.pending(project))
        self.assertEqual(self.ids(core.audit(project))["INCOMPLETE_PROJECT_TRANSACTION"]["status"], "fail")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "rollback"):
            core.apply(project)
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / ".gitignore").exists())

    def test_journal_damage_and_duplicate_targets_block_all_restore(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        manifest = core.apply(project)
        original = manifest.read_bytes()
        after = config.read_bytes()
        for mode in ("count", "status", "hash", "duplicate", "version", "downgrade"):
            journal = json.loads(original)
            if mode == "count":
                journal["file_count"] += 1
                seal(journal)
            elif mode == "status":
                journal["status"] = "ready-ish"
                seal(journal)
            elif mode == "hash":
                journal["files"][0]["after_sha256"] = "f" * 64
            elif mode == "duplicate":
                journal["files"][1]["path"] = journal["files"][0]["path"]
                seal(journal)
            elif mode == "version":
                journal["schema"] = 99
                seal(journal)
            else:
                journal["schema"] = 1
            core.write_json(manifest, journal)
            with self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                core.rollback(project)
            self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), after)
        manifest.write_bytes(original)
        core.rollback(project)

    def test_legacy_schema1_rollback_remains_usable(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        manifest = core.apply(project)
        journal = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        journal["schema"] = 1
        journal["root"] = str(project.root).replace("\\\\?\\", "")  # v0.1 used normal Windows paths
        for field in ("integrity_sha256", "file_count", "operation"):
            journal.pop(field)
        core.write_json(manifest, journal)
        self.assertTrue(transactions.rollback_preview(project)["legacy_journal"])
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        self.assertEqual(load_journal(manifest, "apply")["status"], "rolled_back")

    def test_insufficient_space_prevents_payload_writes(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        original = config.read_bytes()
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()
        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.operations.shutil.disk_usage", return_value=mock.Mock(free=0)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "space"):
                core.apply(project)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "space"):
                core.stage_release(build, release, symbols)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), original)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / ".gitignore").exists())
        self.assertFalse(release.exists())

    def test_backup_disk_full_cleans_preparation(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        write = core.atomic_write

        def fail(path, data):
            if path.suffix == ".bak":
                raise OSError(errno.ENOSPC, "disk full")
            return write(path, data)
        with mock.patch.object(core, "atomic_write", side_effect=fail):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                core.apply(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        self.assertEqual(list((project.root / core.STATE).rglob("*.bak")), [])
        self.assertEqual(list(transactions.manifests(project)), [])

    def test_rollback_failure_can_be_retried(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        manifest = core.apply(project)
        write = core.atomic_write

        def occupied(path, data):
            if path == config:
                raise PermissionError(errno.EACCES, "file in use")
            return write(path, data)
        with mock.patch.object(core, "atomic_write", side_effect=occupied):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "Rollback interrupted"):
                core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(load_journal(manifest, "apply")["status"], "rollback_incomplete")
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)

    @unittest.skipUnless(os.name == "nt", "Windows sharing-violation test")
    def test_actual_windows_occupied_config_preserves_recovery(self):
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        kernel = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
        kernel.CreateFileW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD,
                                      wintypes.LPVOID, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.HANDLE]
        kernel.CreateFileW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
        kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
        handle = kernel.CreateFileW(str(config), 0x80000000, 1, None, 3, 0, None)
        self.assertNotEqual(handle, ctypes.c_void_p(-1).value)
        try:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "interrupted"):
                core.apply(project)
            self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        finally:
            kernel.CloseHandle(handle)
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)

    def test_preview_change_blocks_stale_apply(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        preview = core.plan(project)
        config.write_bytes(config.read_bytes() + b"; subsequent user edit\n")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "preview"):
            core.apply(project, expected_plan=preview)
        self.assertIn(b"subsequent user edit", config.read_bytes())
        self.assertFalse((project.root / ".gitignore").exists())

    def test_cancel_before_commit_and_ignore_cancel_during_commit(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        op = Operation()
        op.cancel.set()
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            core.apply(project, operation=op)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        op = Operation(lambda event: op.cancel.set() if not event["cancellable"] else None)
        manifest = core.apply(project, operation=op)
        self.assertEqual(load_journal(manifest, "apply")["status"], "applied")
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)

    def test_long_chinese_paths_stage_and_rollback(self):
        deep = self.root / ("工程路径" * 8) / ("版本目录" * 8) / ("长文件夹" * 8)
        self.put("Demo.uproject", '{"EngineAssociation":"5.6"}', deep)
        project = core.detect_project(deep)
        config = self.config(project)
        before = config.read_bytes()
        core.apply(project)
        core.rollback(project)
        self.assertEqual(config.read_bytes(), before)
        self.put("游戏.exe", "原始数据", deep / "构建")
        core.stage_release(deep / "构建", deep / "发布", deep / "私有符号")
        self.assertEqual((deep / "构建/游戏.exe").read_bytes(), (deep / "发布/游戏.exe").read_bytes())

    def test_stage_failure_is_blocked_and_verified_cleanup_is_repeatable(self):
        build, release, symbols, manifest = self.interrupted_stage()
        originals = {p: p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(build)}
        self.assertEqual(self.ids(core.audit(self.unity(), release))["INCOMPLETE_STAGE"]["status"], "fail")
        result = staging.cleanup_stage(manifest)
        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "CLEANED")
        self.assertEqual(list(release.iterdir()), [])
        self.assertEqual(staging.cleanup_stage(manifest)["removed"], 0)
        for path, data in originals.items():
            self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), data)

    def test_cleanup_refuses_unknown_files_and_modified_partial(self):
        _, release, _, manifest = self.interrupted_stage()
        unknown = self.put("user-notes.txt", "keep", release)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "unrecognized"):
            staging.cleanup_stage(manifest)
        self.assertEqual(unknown.read_text(), "keep")
        unknown.unlink()
        partial = next(release.rglob("*.srg-part-*"))
        partial.write_bytes(b"different")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "partial"):
            staging.cleanup_stage(manifest)
        self.assertEqual(partial.read_bytes(), b"different")

    def test_killed_stage_can_safely_clean_partial_copy(self):
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()
        ready = self.root / "ready"
        proc = self.child('''import sys,time
from pathlib import Path
from steam_release_guard import core,staging
def interrupt(source,partial,*args):
    partial.write_bytes(source.read_bytes()[:5])
    Path(sys.argv[4]).write_text('ready')
    time.sleep(60)
staging._copy=interrupt
core.stage_release(*map(Path,sys.argv[1:4]))
''', build, release, symbols, ready)
        self.ready(ready, proc)
        proc.kill()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads((release / staging.MARKER).read_text())["status"], "INCOMPLETE")
        staging.cleanup_stage(symbols / staging.JOURNAL)
        self.assertEqual(list(release.iterdir()), [])
        self.assertTrue((build / "Game.exe").exists())

    def test_stage_cancel_keeps_journal_and_completed_stage_cannot_be_cleaned(self):
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()
        op = Operation(lambda event: op.cancel.set() if event["phase"] == "copying verified files" else None)
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            core.stage_release(build, release, symbols, operation=op)
        staging.cleanup_stage(symbols / staging.JOURNAL)
        release2, symbols2 = self.root / "release2", self.root / "symbols2"
        core.stage_release(build, release2, symbols2)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "completed"):
            staging.cleanup_stage(symbols2 / staging.JOURNAL)
        self.assertEqual((release2 / "Game.exe").read_bytes(), (build / "Game.exe").read_bytes())

    def test_cli_json_confirmation_and_preview_do_not_mutate(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        original = list(project.root.rglob("*"))
        for options, expected in ((["--json"], 3), (["--dry-run", "--json"], 0)):
            code, out, err = self.cli("apply", project.root, *options)
            self.assertEqual(code, expected, err)
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(out)["command"], "apply")
            self.assertEqual(original, list(project.root.rglob("*")))
        code, out, err = self.cli("--json", "apply", project.root, "--yes")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, err)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(out)["status"], "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW")
        code, out, _ = self.cli("rollback", project.root, "--yes", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(out)["status"], "COMPLETE")

    def test_unexpected_exception_has_json_error_without_sensitive_contents(self):
        project = self.unreal()
        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.cli.plan", side_effect=RuntimeError("secret-password")):
            code, out, err = self.cli("plan", project.root, "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertEqual(err, "")
        self.assertNotIn("secret-password", out)
        self.assertIn("RuntimeError", json.loads(out)["error"])
        code, out, _ = self.cli("nonsense", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(out)["status"], "ERROR")

    def test_audit_can_cancel_before_scanning_without_writing_project(self):
        project = self.unity()
        before = list(project.root.rglob("*"))
        op = Operation(lambda event: op.cancel.set())
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            core.audit(project, operation=op)
        self.assertEqual(list(project.root.rglob("*")), before)

    def test_valid_long_asset_filename_is_not_lengthened_by_staging(self):
        build, release, symbols = self.stage_paths()
        name = "音频" + "a" * 220 + ".wav"
        source = self.put(name, b"original-audio", core.absolute_path(build))
        self.addCleanup(source.unlink, missing_ok=True)
        target = core.absolute_path(release) / name
        self.addCleanup(target.unlink, missing_ok=True)
        core.stage_release(build, release, symbols)
        self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), source.read_bytes())

```

`tests/test_unreal_review.py`:

```py
"""PR review regressions on synthetic files/explicitly fake module capabilities."""
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

import test_guard as fixtures
from steam_release_guard import core, project_backup
from steam_release_guard.advanced import unreal
import test_advanced_unreal as previous


class UnrealReviewTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUp = fixtures.GuardTests.setUp
    put = fixtures.GuardTests.put
    unreal_project = fixtures.GuardTests.unreal
    crypto = fixtures.GuardTests.crypto
    cli = fixtures.GuardTests.cli
    profile = previous.UnrealAdvancedTests.profile
    engine = previous.UnrealAdvancedTests.engine

    def inventory(self, root):
        return {str(p.relative_to(root)): p.read_bytes() for p in core.walk_files(root)
                if not p.is_relative_to(root / core.STATE)}

    def test_explicit_container_choices_and_keys_round_trip(self):
        project = self.unreal_project()
        fixtures.GuardTests.config(self, project)
        self.crypto(project)
        before = self.inventory(project.root)
        for policy, expected in (("pak-only", ("True", "False")), ("pak+iostore", ("True", "True"))):
            with self.subTest(policy=policy):
                config = {**self.profile(), "container_policy": policy}
                planned = unreal.UnrealAdapter().plan(project, config)
                self.assertEqual(self.inventory(project.root), before)
                journal = unreal.apply(project, config, expected_plan=planned)
                saved = json.loads(journal.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                self.assertEqual(project_backup.verify(saved["full_backup"]["backup_path"])["status"], "VERIFIED")
                settings = core.ini_values((project.root / "Config/DefaultGame.ini").read_bytes(), core.PACKAGING)
                self.assertEqual((settings["usepakfile"], settings["buseiostore"]), expected)
                core.rollback(project)
                self.assertEqual(self.inventory(project.root), before)

    def test_iostore_only_unsupported_never_enables_pak_or_touches_project(self):
        project = self.unreal_project()
        self.crypto(project)
        self.put("Config/DefaultGame.ini", f"[{core.PACKAGING}]\nUsePakFile=False\nbUseIoStore=True\n", project.root)
        before = self.inventory(project.root)
        with mock.patch("steam_release_guard.project_backup.create") as backup:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(core.GuardError, "IoStore-only is NOT_VERIFIED"):
                unreal.apply(project, {**self.profile(), "container_policy": "iostore-only"})
        backup.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.inventory(project.root), before)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / core.STATE).exists())

    def test_ambiguous_legacy_and_missing_policy_are_rejected_before_mutation(self):
        project = self.unreal_project()
        self.crypto(project)
        before = self.inventory(project.root)
        legacy = {**self.profile(), "schema": 1, "iostore": True}
        legacy.pop("container_policy")
        missing = self.profile()
        missing.pop("container_policy")
        for config in (legacy, missing, {**self.profile(), "container_policy": "auto"}):
            with self.subTest(config=config), self.assertRaises(core.GuardError):
                unreal.apply(project, config)
        self.assertEqual(self.inventory(project.root), before)
        self.assertFalse((project.root / core.STATE).exists())

    def test_example_policy_and_cli_preview_expose_explicit_choice(self):
        project = self.unreal_project()
        self.crypto(project)
        config_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "examples/advanced/unreal-policy.json"
        value = json.loads(config_path.read_text())
        unreal.validate(project, value)
        code, out, _ = self.cli("advanced", "unreal-apply", project.root, "--config", config_path, "--dry-run", "--json")
        self.assertEqual(code, 0, out)
        result = json.loads(out)["result"]
        self.assertEqual(result["container_policy"], "pak+iostore")
        self.assertTrue(result["pak_requested"] and result["iostore_requested"])
        self.assertFalse((project.root / core.STATE).exists())

    def packaged(self):
        build = self.root / "build"
        for name in ("Demo.pak", "Demo.utoc", "Demo.ucas"):
            self.put(name, b"synthetic container", build)
        return build

    def runner(self, pak="UnrealPak -Test -List -CryptoKeys=<file>",
               iostore="IoStoreUtilities -List=<container> -Csv=<file> -CryptoKeys=<file>",
               failure=None, listing=True, help_exit=0):
        self.calls = []
        def fake_run(argv, folder, **kwargs):
            self.calls.append(argv)
            is_io = "IoStore" in argv
            text = (iostore if is_io else pak) if "-Help" in argv else "synthetic only"
            (folder / "tool.log").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
            if "-Help" in argv:
                return {"exit_code": help_exit, "log_sha256": core.file_digest(folder / "tool.log")}
            if failure == ("iostore" if is_io else "pak"):
                return {"exit_code": 7, "log_sha256": core.file_digest(folder / "tool.log")}
            for arg in argv:
                if arg.startswith("-Csv=") and listing:
                    Path(arg[5:]).write_text("fixture,not-real-assets\n", encoding="utf-8")
            return {"exit_code": 0, "log_sha256": core.file_digest(folder / "tool.log")}
        return fake_run

    def inspect(self, **kwargs):
        with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run", side_effect=self.runner(**kwargs)):
            return unreal.verify(self.unreal_project(), self.engine(), self.packaged(), self.root / "proof")

    def test_only_pak_entrypoint_cannot_verify_iostore(self):
        report = self.inspect(iostore="UnrealPak -Test -List")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["pak"]["status"], "REVIEW")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        self.assertFalse(any("IoStore" in call and "-Help" not in call for call in self.calls))

    def test_only_iostoreutilities_entrypoint_cannot_verify_pak(self):
        report = self.inspect(pak="UnrealPak host; no PakFileUtilities commands")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["pak"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["status"], "REVIEW")
        self.assertTrue(all("IoStore" in c or "-Help" in c for c in self.calls))
        self.assertTrue(all(row["container"].endswith(".utoc") for row in report["groups"]["iostore"]["checks"]))

    def test_missing_host_and_undocumented_standalone_do_not_execute(self):
        project, build = self.unreal_project(), self.packaged()
        engine = self.root / "no-host"
        self.put("Engine/Build/Build.version", '{"MajorVersion":5,"MinorVersion":6,"PatchVersion":0}', engine)
        self.put("Engine/Binaries/Win64/IoStoreUtilities.exe", b"not an established official standalone CLI", engine)
        with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run") as run:
            report = unreal.verify(project, engine, build, self.root / "proof")
        run.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        self.assertTrue(all(g["status"] == "NOT_VERIFIED" for g in report["groups"].values()))

    def test_version_mismatch_unknown_version_and_malformed_version_never_execute(self):
        project, build = self.unreal_project(), self.packaged()
        for index, version in enumerate(((5, 5, 0), (5, 9, 0), (5, True, 0))):
            engine = self.engine()
            self.put("Engine/Build/Build.version", json.dumps(dict(zip(("MajorVersion", "MinorVersion", "PatchVersion"), version))), engine)
            with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run") as run:
                report = unreal.verify(project, engine, build, self.root / f"proof{index}")
            run.assert_not_called()
            self.assertEqual(report["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")

    def test_iostore_unreviewed_version_does_not_borrow_pak_capability(self):
        project = self.unreal_project("5.5")
        entry = unreal.UnrealAdapter().detect(project, self.engine(5))["tools"]
        self.assertEqual(entry["pak"]["status"], "DETECTED")
        self.assertEqual(entry["iostore"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")

    def test_unsupported_iostore_parameters_and_help_failure_are_not_verified(self):
        self.assertFalse(unreal._supports("-ListFormats -TestSomething", "-List"))
        for index, options in enumerate(({"iostore": "IoStore -List -Info"}, {"help_exit": 1})):
            with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run", side_effect=self.runner(**options)):
                report = unreal.verify(self.unreal_project(), self.engine(), self.packaged(), self.root / f"proof{index}")
            self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
            self.assertFalse(any("IoStore" in c and "-Help" not in c for c in self.calls))

    def test_failed_container_execution_does_not_destroy_other_group_result(self):
        report = self.inspect(failure="iostore")
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["pak"]["status"], "REVIEW")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["checks"][0]["exit_code"], 7)

    def test_os_execution_failure_is_recorded_without_secret_exception(self):
        with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run", side_effect=OSError("SECRET_DIAGNOSTIC")):
            report = unreal.verify(self.unreal_project(), self.engine(), self.packaged(), self.root / "proof")
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertNotIn("SECRET_DIAGNOSTIC", json.dumps(report))

    def test_exit_zero_without_iostore_listing_is_not_an_artifact_check(self):
        report = self.inspect(listing=False)
        self.assertEqual(report["groups"]["iostore"]["status"], "NOT_VERIFIED")
        self.assertFalse(any(f["status"] == "PASS" for f in report["findings"]))

    def test_only_one_container_kind_does_not_probe_the_other(self):
        for index, names in enumerate((("Demo.pak",), ("Demo.utoc", "Demo.ucas"))):
            build = self.root / f"build{index}"
            for name in names:
                self.put(name, b"synthetic", build)
            with mock.patch.object(unreal, "private_run", side_effect=self.runner()):
                report = unreal.verify(self.unreal_project(), self.engine(), build, self.root / f"proof{index}")
            absent = "iostore" if index == 0 else "pak"
            self.assertEqual(report["groups"][absent]["status"], "NOT_REQUESTED")
            self.assertTrue(all(("IoStore" in c) == (index == 1) for c in self.calls))

```

`workflows/advanced-protection.md`:

```md
# P3 高级保护（候选，真实游戏验收未完成）

先读 [开发前审查](../docs/P3-REVIEW.md)。P0 旧命令和 GUI 保留；高级功能使用 `advanced` 命令空间。默认所有引擎运行、保护效果及性能均未验证，不因配置正确而批准发行。只有你拥有的游戏副本和官方可信工具可用于试验。

## Unity 符号混淆

1. 按 [完整备份](backup.md) 先 backup、backup-verify，再 backup-restore 到新的工程副本。关闭编辑器和同步工具。
2. 用户自行安装 Obfuz，官方地址 `https://github.com/focus-creative-games/obfuz.git#b83c53d4df525232a4d8e783d68995706b3a5d5d`。SRG 不下载、不修改 Packages manifest；本机安装检测只读。按该版本官方说明配置 Obfuz.asset 和生成 VM，保留原有配置/密钥。即使仅符号混淆，该工具也要求已有 VM；SRG 不代为生成或轮换密钥。
3. 复制并编辑 [示例配置](../examples/advanced/unity-obfuz.json)：填写精确 Unity 版本、自己拥有的 asmdef 程序集及少量普通 C# 类型。不要直接照抄示例类型。反射、JSON/存档 DTO、序列化、DI 注册、RPC、第三方 SDK 先加入 exclude_types；reference_assemblies 必须覆盖引用被混淆程序集的程序集。
4. 默认保留所有名称，只对 include_types 中明确类型启用类型/方法改名；字段默认保留。exclude_types 后匹配优先。Unity 自带特殊类型规则仍可能阻止改名，不能用空改动宣称成功。strings/control_flow 暂不开放；传 true 明确报错，不静默忽略。需额外运行时初始化的保护仍为 NOT_VERIFIED。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced obfuscator-detect "D:/GameCopy" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced obfuscator-detect "D:/GameCopy" --tool-path "D:/Tools/obfuz" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-plan "D:/GameCopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unity.json" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-apply "D:/GameCopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unity.json" --backup-dir "D:/Backups/GameCopy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-apply "D:/GameCopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unity.json" --backup-dir "D:/Backups/GameCopy" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-build "D:/GameCopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unity.json" --editor "D:/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe" --output "D:/Builds/Protected-001" --evidence-dir "D:/Private/Protected-001" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-build "D:/GameCopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unity.json" --editor "D:/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe" --output "D:/Builds/Protected-001" --evidence-dir "D:/Private/Protected-001" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback "D:/GameCopy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback "D:/GameCopy" --yes --json
```

`--tool-path` 用于候选包检测；实际构建使用 Unity 已解析的 UPM 包，桥接再次检查包版本与 Editor 版本。使用标准 Windows x64 Profile，启用发布场景；没有 Windows IL2CPP 模块/编译环境时停止。执行引擎只接受本工具验证恢复得到的副本；会产生正常的 Unity 导入缓存。输出与证据目录必须全新且与工程互不嵌套。

日志、符号映射、原程序集备份为私有资料。不要上传 Steam 或 GitHub。桥接检查 Obfuz 的 success 事件和实际程序集元数据，要求选中类型有改名证据；配置和原 Obfuz.asset 在 finally 中恢复。断电/进程被杀时可能无法执行 finally，保留副本，使用完整备份重新恢复；不要对唯一工程执行。

构建完成仍需插件、旧/新存档、反射/JSON、RPC/联机、离线、更新和真实保护效果验收。`execution.json` 的 REVIEW 不是发行 PASS。关闭混淆使用普通 P0 构建入口或回滚桥接；关闭后名称分析更容易。

## Unity 元数据与原生插件（只读）

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unity-inspect "D:/GameCopy" --build "D:/Builds/Protected-001" --json
```

仅输出检查结果，不写报告文件、不执行/删除 DLL。[技术限制](../docs/P3-METADATA.md) 包含版本核查、插件依赖和元数据方案排除理由。

## Unreal 独立配置与容器检查

编辑 [UE 策略](../examples/advanced/unreal-policy.json)。schema 2 把容器策略和 CryptoKeys 开关分开，`container_policy` 必须明确选择；schema 1 的 `iostore` 布尔值不能表达是否允许启用 Pak，旧配置会停止并要求人工迁移，不会猜测或自动修改。

| 策略 | UsePakFile / bUseIoStore | 当前处理 |
|---|---|---|
| `pak-only` | True / False | 按明确选择预览、备份后写入 |
| `pak+iostore` | True / True | 按明确选择预览、备份后写入；Pak 和 IoStore 分别验收 |
| `iostore-only` | False / True | 当前 Windows/UAT profile 没有已验证的独立组合，NOT_VERIFIED，写入前停止；不自动改成 True / True |

策略允许你明确改变原容器方式，但 CryptoKeys 开关本身不会选择容器。先对照原工程 Packaging、平台覆盖与发布流程选择策略，再用 dry-run 审核；没有授权的策略变化就不执行 apply。官方 Zen Loader 流程还使用伴随 Pak 承载 loose files，并在挂载 Pak 时挂载对应容器；不能只凭两个 INI 布尔值承诺纯 IoStore 支持。

配置审查范围仍是 UE 5.0–5.6；未知/自编译 GUID 和更新版本需重新核查。现有 AES key 必须是有效非零 32 字节 Base64；签名字段只检查编码/非空，**不是 RSA key 对正确性验证**。生产 key 必须由你在 UE 管理；SRG 不生成/轮换，不在结果中输出其值。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unreal-plan "D:/UECopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unreal.json" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced asset-plan "D:/UECopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unreal.json" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unreal-apply "D:/UECopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unreal.json" --backup-dir "D:/Backups/UECopy" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unreal-apply "D:/UECopy" --config "D:/Profiles/unreal.json" --backup-dir "D:/Backups/UECopy" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unreal-verify "D:/UECopy" --engine-root "D:/UE_5.6" --build "D:/Builds/UE-001" --evidence-dir "D:/Private/UE-001" --crypto-json "D:/Private/Crypto.json" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced unreal-verify "D:/UECopy" --engine-root "D:/UE_5.6" --build "D:/Builds/UE-001" --evidence-dir "D:/Private/UE-001" --crypto-json "D:/Private/Crypto.json" --yes --json
```

实际构建继续使用原有 `build-script` 流程。`unreal-verify` 分别建立 PakFileUtilities 和 IoStoreUtilities 检查入口，先从同一安装的 `Engine/Build/Build.version` 核对版本，再检查该入口自己的帮助；版本不符、缺工具或未知参数不会调用容器命令，记录 NOT_VERIFIED。

- Pak 使用 `Engine/Binaries/Win64/UnrealPak.exe -Help`，仅对 `.pak` 调用帮助中明确支持的 `-Test` / `-List`。
- IoStoreUtilities 是 UE Developer 模块。已查到的官方入口由 `UnrealPak.exe IoStore` 承载，不是已确认存在的独立 `IoStoreUtilities.exe`。当前只开放已核对文档的 UE 5.6 入口；独立调用 `IoStore -Help`，要求它明确给出 IoStore 身份、`-List=<container>` 和 `-Csv=<path>`，才对 `.utoc` 运行 `IoStore -List=<utoc> -Csv=<私有输出>`。不复用 Pak 帮助，不把 `.utoc` 交给普通 Pak 检查。不认识的独立 EXE/DLL 不当作可执行替代品。
- 使用 `--crypto-json` 时，各入口还必须明确支持 `-CryptoKeys=`。缺少参数只记录未验证，不尝试其他拼法/命令。只检查与构建内现有容器类型对应的入口；另一类记录 NOT_REQUESTED。
- 分别保存 `pak/verification.json`、`iostore/verification.json`、各自帮助/执行日志；总记录为 `verification.json`。每类记录包含模块、版本/程序哈希、单容器结果。一个入口失败不覆盖另一类记录；执行失败标 FAIL，缺参数标 NOT_VERIFIED。IoStore 返回零但没有生成非空清单仍为 NOT_VERIFIED。
- 返回零且有检查证据最多记 REVIEW，两个容器的加密/签名和真实运行仍分别 NOT_VERIFIED。没有提取资源或自动安装工具。Build.version 绑定所选安装，程序哈希记录所用二进制；它们不能替代官方来源核验或真实引擎测试。

参考 [UE5.6 Zen Loader 官方说明](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/zen-loader-in-unreal-engine?application_version=5.6) 和 [UE5.6 命令行参数参考](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-engine-command-line-arguments-reference?application_version=5.6)（`csv` 给出 `-list=<ContainerFile> -csv=<path>`）。Context7 仅提供未分版本索引，本次转查上述精确版本文档；本机没有 UE，帮助输出和实际执行尚未实测，能力门槛不满足就停止，不伪造兼容性。

`.utoc` 描述容器结构，`.ucas` 承载数据；Pak index 加密不能证明 IoStore 加密。部分项目还有分区 `.ucas`、DLC 和平台配置覆盖，全部需纳入版本清单和实际加载验证。签名有效性当前保留 NOT_VERIFIED，不能依据 `.sig` 存在或退出码推断有效。

`asset-plan` 对 Content 文件按显式路径规则分 A/B/C，缺规则和重复命中都标 REVIEW。分级不会自动创建 Chunk/PrimaryAssetLabel/AssetManager 或分配 secondary key。先在 Editor 审核依赖和加载关系后实现分块；全资源加密是全局开关，不能称为分级加密。分别评估 A 核心资源、B 普通资源、C 公开资源的收益，记录启动、加载、内存、补丁和 DLC 基线。

回滚仍使用普通 `rollback`。关闭某一开关只写该项 false，含义是放弃相应加密/签名保护；不会删除 key。所有修改保持原 INI 编码、无关配置、注释与数组；后续编辑冲突会阻止回滚。

## 版本文件完整性与 Steam Wrapper 产物

最终完成构建、DRM/签名、符号分离后再为确切 depot 创建 SHA256 清单；更新后创建新 build-id 和新清单。预览不写文件，创建需要明确确认。默认包含所有文件，包括 EXE/DLL/资源/配置；只有显式 mutable 路径被排除。manifest 存在构建外，不能覆盖。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced integrity-create "D:/Builds/Final-001" --build-id "release-001" --output "D:/Private/release-001.json" --mutable "Saved/*" --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced integrity-create "D:/Builds/Final-001" --build-id "release-001" --output "D:/Private/release-001.json" --mutable "Saved/*" --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced integrity-verify "D:/Builds/Final-001" --manifest "D:/Private/release-001.json" --build-id "release-001" --expected-manifest-sha256 "REPLACE_WITH_TRUSTED_64_HEX" --json
python -m steam_release_guard advanced drm-inspect --original "D:/Private/original.exe" --wrapped "D:/Builds/Final-001/Game.exe" --expected-original-sha256 "REPLACE_WITH_PRE_WRAPPER_64_HEX" --json
```

把创建结果中的 manifest_sha256 固定到独立可信发布记录/CI/服务端，校验时传该值；不要从同一个可替换目录即时计算并当成信任依据。SHA256 清单不是数字签名，不防攻击者同时替换客户端验证器/清单。校验命令只读，报告改动、缺失、多出的文件。退出码 2 表示字节不符，不等于作弊或盗版。

运行时研究结论：可由启动器在游戏未运行、更新已结束时调用 `steam_release_guard.advanced.integrity.verify`，或在现有游戏框架中实现等价标准 SHA256 校验；应异步/分批处理，实测大资源 IO 成本，避免逐帧全量散列。可信清单/签名和重要状态优先由服务端验证。本模块没有注入游戏、内核驱动、反调试、自动删除或封禁逻辑；尚未验证任何游戏内运行时集成。遇到异常提示 Steam 验证文件/修复，保留存档和允许的 mod；合法补丁暂停检查并原子更新基线，不把旧版本清单套到新游戏。

原 `scripts/Invoke-SteamDrm.ps1` 保留原有上传明确确认。先独立记录原 EXE SHA256，再执行已授权 Wrapper。`drm-inspect` 检查原件未变、输出 PE 结构及差异，**不能单靠二进制变化证明 Wrapper 成功**。Wrapper 日志、Steam 启动、离线行为和测试分支重新安装全部需要真实验收；本模块不运行 EXE 或上传 Steam。

## 关键数据与多人客户端（只读）

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced security-inspect "D:/GameCopy" --json
```

直接输出文件、行号、候选问题、原因和修复方向，不写独立报告、不改源码、不连接服务器。覆盖字面量秘密、货币/背包/交易/奖励/结果、管理员标志、RPC、附近的本地随机数、调试入口、危险反序列化和证书绕过候选。发现 IsServer/HasAuthority/开发宏会记为上下文线索，不能认定安全或不安全；存在服务端检查时应逐条读取对应实现，避免重复开发。

主要建议：客户端提交操作意图，服务端验证身份、所有权、数量/余额/价格、时序、速率、重放和幂等性，并原子处理交易/结算/奖励。离线游戏的可编辑存档和显示用数值应按实际威胁模型处理，不能一律判成漏洞。Blueprint、外部服务、动态/编码数据和跳过的大文件明确未验证，正则扫描不能证明服务端缺乏校验。不会自行改服务器、撤销 key 或输出秘密值。

## 性能、兼容性和三层验收

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard advanced acceptance-template
python -m steam_release_guard advanced acceptance-check "D:/Private/acceptance.json" --baseline-build "D:/Builds/Baseline" --candidate-build "D:/Builds/Protected" --baseline-manifest "D:/Private/baseline.json" --candidate-manifest "D:/Private/protected.json" --evidence-root "D:/Private/Measurements" --json
```

第一条仅打印模板。把对象保存为自己的 acceptance.json，填写不同 build-id 及从可信记录得到的两个 manifest_sha256；这两个清单分别由 integrity-create 创建。不要使用 `--json` 的外层 envelope 作为记录；若用 --json，取其 result 对象。

- 同一硬件/OS/引擎、场景、画质、网络条件和冷热缓存条件下，分别测基线与每个单项保护，再测组合。时间/内存/CPU/网络/存档指标至少三次；单次可用于实际文件/补丁字节、崩溃次数。记录测量工具。不要以 Editor Play Mode 代替分发的游戏。
- metrics 包含 startup_ms、scene_load_ms、memory_mib、cpu_percent、file_bytes、patch_bytes、crash_count、network_rtt_ms、save_read_ms、save_write_ms。file_bytes 自动计算清单范围；其他值需真实采样。每项填写 baseline_samples、candidate_samples 和自行选择的 max_regression_pct，未填阈值保持 NOT_VERIFIED。patch_bytes 使用实际 Steam/DLC 补丁产物，不能拿文件差异量冒充下载量。
- 私有 evidence 引用形式为 `{"path":"session/load-times.csv","sha256":"实际64位小写哈希"}`，路径相对 evidence-root。记录数据来源与过程，保留脱敏日志/录像/测量文件。工具只核对文件存在和哈希，不能自动判断录像/日志含义。
- runtime_checks 分别记录启动、资源、旧/新存档、联机、插件、离线、补丁、DLC、Steam 测试分支、调试入口。protection_checks 每个已启用保护单独填写 configuration、artifact、runtime。支持 PASS/FAIL/REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED/NOT_APPLICABLE；不适用仍需人审，不能用来绕过必要验收。
- PASS 声明缺证据降为 NOT_VERIFIED；有匹配证据也只记 RECORDED_PASS。REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED 原样保留，不自动批准发行。游戏文件、证据被改或指标回退超阈值会 BLOCKED。候选崩溃次数非零会阻断。

真实游戏验收当前尚未完成。待有可用磁盘、正确引擎、用户选择安装的保护器及游戏副本后执行；本工具不自动启动/上传真实游戏来补齐记录。

```

`workflows/backup.md`:

```md
# 完整工程文件备份与恢复

先读 [统一操作说明](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)，核实工程目录和引擎版本，再运行 plan 或 audit。关闭引擎、版本控制写任务和同步工具。命令在工具仓库根目录执行，占位路径需替换为实际路径。

## 首次试用

先完整备份，再恢复出一个新工程副本，对这个副本进行适配。备份/恢复只在本地执行，原工程保留。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup 'D:/Games/MyGame' --backup-dir 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame' --dry-run --json
```

解释文件数、总字节数、源目录和备份存放位置。备份范围是工程目录内所有普通文件和空目录，包括隐藏文件、`.git`、`.meta`、缓存和已有构建；不根据 `.gitignore` 省略文件。用户已授权复制范围后：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard backup 'D:/Games/MyGame' --backup-dir 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame' --yes --progress --json
```

从 JSON 的 `result.backup_path` 取得真正的时间目录，将下面占位的“时间编号”替换成该路径。检查 `status=COMPLETE` 后再核验；不能自行把 INCOMPLETE 改成 COMPLETE。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard backup-verify 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/时间编号' --json
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/时间编号' --output 'D:/Games/MyGame-Trial' --dry-run --json
```

解释恢复目标，确认是工程和备份之外的全新目录。用户已授权后：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard backup-restore 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame/时间编号' --output 'D:/Games/MyGame-Trial' --yes --progress --json
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyGame-Trial' --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyGame-Trial' --backup-dir 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame-Trial' --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyGame-Trial' --backup-dir 'D:/PrivateBackups/MyGame-Trial' --yes --json
```

每次实际有修改的 apply 都创建新的完整备份并核验，再进入原有逐文件事务。没有跳过开关；无配置变更时不重复备份。后续适配失败仍用 [rollback](rollback.md) 撤销这次修改，完整备份继续保留。

GUI 对应入口：“备份位置…”选择存放父目录，“完整备份”创建时间目录，“恢复完整备份…”选择时间目录和恢复父目录；程序自动创建新的 `RestoredProject-<时间>`。一键适配的确认框同时显示备份位置，成功后在界面与日志显示实际备份路径。

## 故障与边界

- 备份目录必须在工程外且互不嵌套；默认同级 `<工程名>.srg-backups/`。可选其他磁盘，不默认上传云端，也不会自动删除历史备份。
- 文件占用、写满、取消、进程强退、文件在复制中变化、校验不符都会阻止适配。失败副本保留为 INCOMPLETE，不能恢复；排除原因后创建新的备份。
- 完整恢复先核验全部备份，再复制到新目录，最后核对全部文件和空目录。拒绝已存在的目标，包括空目录；不提供强制覆盖。失败时保留部分输出，相邻 `<新工程目录>.srg-restore.json` 为 INCOMPLETE。不要把部分输出作为恢复完成的工程；改用新的目录重试。
- 保留恢复目录旁的 `.srg-restore.json` 和对应完整备份；工具据此识别恢复副本中原有的回滚记录。INCOMPLETE 或损坏的恢复凭据会阻断适配和审计，不能删除凭据来绕过。完整恢复可离线读取备份，不要求原工程仍然存在。
- 完整工程文件备份保留文件内容和目录结构，不承诺文件系统镜像：ACL、所有者、NTFS 备用流等元数据不保证保留，链接/连接点与特殊文件拒绝。工程根目录外的依赖和玩家存档需另外保留。大缓存也会复制，请预留空间。
- 本机锁只协调工具自身，不阻止编辑器或同步软件写入；源/副本双重校验能检测已观察到的变化，不是跨文件系统的瞬时快照。SHA256 用于检测意外损坏，不是加密或认证签名。
- 备份包含源码、资源和可能的密钥，保持私有。CLI 的 COMPLETE/VERIFIED 只说明文件复制/校验完成；引擎实际打开、构建、插件、存档和联机仍待验收。

交付时给出完整备份路径、文件数/大小、校验结果、恢复新目录、恢复记录和未验证项，不能只说“已备份”。

```

`workflows/rollback.md`:

```md
# 回滚与失败暂存清理

先阅读 [统一操作规则](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)。核实工程、版本、原始路径与恢复日志，关闭编辑器。不要删除备份、修改日志哈希或删除锁文件绕过冲突。在工具仓库根目录执行下面命令，替换占位目录。

## 工程适配回滚

如需恢复整份工程到修改前版本，使用 [完整备份恢复流程](backup.md) 创建新目录，保留当前工程。本节 rollback 只撤销工具本次涉及的文件，完整备份另在工程外保留。

先 plan 或 audit 了解当前状态；随后预览最近一次未回滚事务的全部目标：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard rollback 'D:/Games/MyGame' --dry-run --json
```

确认预览中的恢复日志与文件属于本次工程。用户已授权恢复范围后：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard rollback 'D:/Games/MyGame' --yes --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --json
```

回滚前核验全部目标与备份。原文件必须处于已知修改前/修改后状态；发现后来人工修改就停止，不能自行覆盖或强制回滚。旧 schema 1 记录支持恢复但没有新日志封印，仍逐文件检查。schema 2 校验版本、数量、状态、路径、重复项和 SHA256；这不是防恶意伪造签名。

回滚写入阶段不可取消。文件占用、磁盘写满或进程退出后保留恢复记录，解决问题再运行相同预览与回滚；已恢复的文件可识别。不要把恢复失败宣称为完成。新增空目录及引擎生成的 `.meta` 可能保留，不删除用途不明文件。

Python 工具回滚不能替代存档、引擎构建输出、Unity 崩溃后 PlayerSettings 或 Steam 发布版本的恢复。

## 清理 INCOMPLETE 暂存副本

本版提供安全清理，**没有 resume 命令**。先对相应工程/失败发布目录 audit，并保存私有目录 `srg-stage-journal.json`。原构建不可删除或修改，部分副本可能需要与它进行前缀验证。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --build 'D:/Release/MyGame-v1' --json
python -m steam_release_guard stage-cleanup 'D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1/srg-stage-journal.json' --dry-run --json
```

解释预览中将删除的已核验副本，用户授权后再执行：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard stage-cleanup 'D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1/srg-stage-journal.json' --yes --json
```

只清理失败事务的可核验文件、对应部分副本和已知空子目录，保留原构建及私有事务日志。发现未知文件、修改过的副本、无法确认的 partial、损坏日志/标记时停止，交给人工处理。已完成的发布目录不能通过此命令删除。清理后的 journal 为 CLEANED，私有目录仍非空；下一次 stage 使用新的发布与符号目录。

原始备份准备阶段若被强制终止，可能留下尚无 manifest 的私有准备目录；不自动删它。检查原工程未被改写，保留该目录供人工审查。若磁盘满到无法保存新的失败状态，使用最后一个有效的 prepared/applying/rolling_back 或 stage 日志，不手工将它改为完成。

完成后报告：恢复/清理状态、实际处理文件、保留位置、冲突或未知文件、随后 audit 结果与未验证事项。

```

`workflows/security-audit.md`:

```md
# 安全检查与发布包检查

先阅读 [统一操作规则](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)，核实项目和引擎版本。

**默认的 AI 工程检查**：只读查看指定工程和可用产物，按统一 Skill 的“场景 B”在对话中给出文件/位置、风险、理由和建议，不修改代码，不创建独立报告。以下 CLI 流程仅用于用户明确要求生成本地报告或执行相应发布操作的场景；audit 即使加 --json 也会写报告，没有 --no-report 参数。

需要以下流程时，先读取实际 README、cli.py 和相关实现并核对 --help。在工具仓库根目录执行，替换占位目录。

## 工程检查

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --report-dir 'D:/PrivateReports/MyGame-project-v1' --json
```

plan 只预览；audit 保存本地 HTML/JSON。报告目录必须是新的空目录或工具已有专用目录。报告列出路径/行号/规则，不输出源码片段或密钥值。源码候选扫描不覆盖所有 Blueprint、Prefab、二进制或服务端权限；没有命中不能证明不存在问题。

## 原始构建与符号分离

原始构建必须来自成功的实际引擎打包；不能提供工程根目录代替发布包。先检查，再预览复制范围：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --build 'D:/Builds/MyGame-v1' --report-dir 'D:/PrivateReports/MyGame-build-v1' --json
python -m steam_release_guard stage 'D:/Builds/MyGame-v1' --project 'D:/Games/MyGame' --release 'D:/Release/MyGame-v1' --symbols 'D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1' --dry-run --json
```

发布目录与私有符号目录必须新建/为空，且与原构建互不嵌套。stage 只复制并分离已知符号，不自动删除其他候选风险文件。用户已授权该复制范围后：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard stage 'D:/Builds/MyGame-v1' --project 'D:/Games/MyGame' --release 'D:/Release/MyGame-v1' --symbols 'D:/PrivateSymbols/MyGame-v1' --yes --progress --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyGame' --build 'D:/Release/MyGame-v1' --report-dir 'D:/PrivateReports/MyGame-release-v1' --json
```

复制前后与最终库存会验证 SHA256。私有符号目录保存事务日志与清单；发布目录 `.srg-stage.json` 标记 COMPLETE 或 INCOMPLETE，不包含密钥或私有绝对路径。COMPLETE 只表示复制事务完成，不能作为上线批准。INCOMPLETE 或损坏标记会阻断 audit；用 [安全清理](rollback.md) 处理，不能删除标记伪装完成。

## 人工检查结果

- 代码：IL2CPP/原生指标与 Managed DLL 分开记录；不声称原生代码无法反编译。
- 调试入口：运行最终 EXE，检查菜单、控制台、跳关、开发快捷键；保留符号供私有崩溃排查。
- 资源：分别审查散装音频/模型/贴图、Pak、IoStore、Bundle；打包/压缩不代表加密。资源提取与加载/补丁测试需要真实工具和产物证据。
- 私有文件：检查源码、备份、DefaultCrypto.ini、steam_appid.txt、凭据候选，明确归档后再由用户决定处理；不因文件名相似自动删除。
- 玩家体验：启动、存档、插件、联机、离线、Steam 启动和更新均需真实结果。测试分支安装/上传必须单独授权。

每项记录状态、证据位置、版本与原因：PASS 有限于已验证事实；WARN 是候选风险；FAIL 是阻断；REVIEW 需人工判断；NOT_VERIFIED 缺少证据；NOT_APPLICABLE 需注明不适用理由。P0 自动报告沿用 pass/warn/fail/review 小写格式，其余状态可在人工验收表记录。禁止把待验证改写为 PASS。

退出码 2 代表发现阻断，1 代表操作失败；0 仍可能存在 REVIEW。最终报告为 BLOCKED 或 REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW。人类审批前不得声称 RELEASE_APPROVED。

```

`workflows/unity-release.md`:

```md
# Unity 6 Windows 发布流程

前置阅读：[统一操作规则](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)。在工具仓库根目录运行下面命令；替换占位目录。保留原工程和原构建。没有独立 AI 服务或自动安装步骤。

本页用于用户要求发布适配的场景。仅检查已有工程时，按 Skill 在对话中给出发现，不默认执行本页流程；audit 会写本地报告，须在用户要求报告后执行。apply 的授权范围包含配置修改、完整备份和本地报告。

1. 读取工程 `ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt`，核实精确的 6000.x Editor。其他版本停止自动适配。确认 Python 3.10+、Unity 许可证、Windows IL2CPP 模块、C++ 工具链和插件兼容性；本 P0 不自动验证/安装这些依赖。
2. 先检查并解释拟修改的文件。apply 只安装 Editor 适配器和更新 `.gitignore`；不是执行引擎构建。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --dry-run --json
```

3. 首次试用先按 [完整备份与恢复](backup.md) 创建新的工程副本。用户已授权适配范围后，关闭编辑器和同步工具，核实工程外的完整备份位置，再执行；apply 会自动完整复制并核验，失败就停止：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --yes --json
```

4. 使用工程准确版本 Unity.exe 生成构建脚本；先预览，再经授权保存。CLI 不执行脚本。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard build-script 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --tool 'D:/Engines/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe' --output 'D:/Builds/MyUnityGame-v1' --save 'D:/Builds/build-unity-v1.ps1' --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard build-script 'D:/Games/MyUnityGame' --tool 'D:/Engines/Unity/Editor/Unity.exe' --output 'D:/Builds/MyUnityGame-v1' --save 'D:/Builds/build-unity-v1.ps1' --yes --json
```

5. 审阅脚本、场景和构建配置；获得执行引擎构建的授权后运行生成的文件，或在 Unity 使用 **Tools → Steam Release Guard → Build Windows Release**。目标为 Windows x64、IL2CPP、非 Development、关闭脚本调试和分析。自定义 Build Profile 会被拒绝，需要人工集成；普通 Unity Build 按钮不自动使用此适配器。构建失败则保留日志，不拿旧构建替代新输出。
6. 实际构建完成后，按 [发布包检查](security-audit.md) 分离符号并检查最终目录。检查 GameAssembly.dll、metadata、Managed DLL、boot.config、私有符号、StreamingAssets/Bundle/Addressables；文件存在只是指标。
7. 用最终构建验证启动、旧/新存档、反射/序列化、插件、联机、离线、测试菜单/跳关/快捷键、性能和更新。没有证据标 NOT_VERIFIED，需要人工判断标 REVIEW；不把 IL2CPP 写成资源加密或防破解保证。

最终记录：工程与版本、改动清单、恢复日志、实际构建命令/日志、报告路径、私有符号目录、兼容性与 Steam 测试待办。异常时按 [回滚](rollback.md) 处理；引擎强退后的 PlayerSettings 恢复需另查工程版本控制。

```

`workflows/unreal-release.md`:

```md
# Unreal Engine 5 Windows 发布流程

前置阅读：[统一操作规则](../skills/game-release-guard/SKILL.md)。以下命令在工具仓库根目录运行；替换占位目录，保留原工程。

本页用于用户要求发布适配的场景。仅检查已有工程时，按 Skill 在对话中给出发现，不默认执行本页流程；audit 会写本地报告，须在用户要求报告后执行。apply 的授权范围包含配置修改、完整备份和本地报告。

1. 读取 `.uproject` 的 EngineAssociation，核实实际 UE 5.x 版本。自编译/GUID 关联版本不明时只审查，不篡改描述文件绕过检查。P0 沿用基础 INI 适配，不声称已具备逐小版本的真实构建验证。
2. 先检查并预览 Shipping / 排除调试文件的修改；检查平台 INI、Target.cs 和构建配置覆盖。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard audit 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --dry-run --json
```

3. 首次试用先按 [完整备份与恢复](backup.md) 创建新的工程副本。用户已授权后关闭编辑器和同步工具，再适配；apply 会先完整备份工程到工程外并核验，失败就停止：

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --yes --json
```

可选 Pak 索引加密必须单独说明范围并取得授权。要求工程已有合法非零 256-bit 密钥；缺失时由用户在对应引擎中处理，不替用户生成/轮换密钥。不要打印配置中的密钥值。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard plan 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --encrypt-pak-index --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --encrypt-pak-index --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard apply 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --encrypt-pak-index --yes --json
```

4. 使用本工程对应的 RunUAT.bat 生成脚本。只生成，不自动调用引擎；保存后需审阅并获得实际构建授权。

```powershell
python -m steam_release_guard build-script 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --tool 'D:/Engines/UE_5/Engine/Build/BatchFiles/RunUAT.bat' --output 'D:/Builds/MyUnrealGame-v1' --save 'D:/Builds/build-unreal-v1.ps1' --dry-run --json
python -m steam_release_guard build-script 'D:/Games/MyUnrealGame' --tool 'D:/Engines/UE_5/Engine/Build/BatchFiles/RunUAT.bat' --output 'D:/Builds/MyUnrealGame-v1' --save 'D:/Builds/build-unreal-v1.ps1' --yes --json
```

5. 核对 UAT 实际日志/receipt 中的 Shipping、Win64、nodebuginfo，以及原有 IoStore 设置是否保留。配置正确不等于产物正确。
6. 按 [发布包检查](security-audit.md) 分离符号、检查真正 depot 文件。保留未暂存进发布包的原始引擎符号，并核对其构建版本。不要将 DefaultCrypto.ini、私有备份、密钥或源码上传。
7. 对自己的实际构建，用版本匹配的官方引擎工具分别验证 Pak 索引访问、资源内容和 IoStore。当前工具不能证明容器加密有效；保留 REVIEW/NOT_VERIFIED。全资源加密和签名不由 P0 自动启用。
8. 实测正常启动、输入、插件、存档、联机、加载、补丁及调试控制台。Steam Wrapper/上传另行明确授权。

最终交付检查结果、恢复日志、实际构建证据、人工待办；失败时使用 [回滚流程](rollback.md)。

```
